========== FILE: ./.my/initial-prompt.md ==========
# Build  for `PLUS-ULTRA`

A floating desktop assistant for Ubuntu (GNOME, Wayland): an always-on-top widget, a task and reminder panel, a tray icon, and desktop notifications. This is a PBL project, so the code must also make OS and DBMS concepts visible.

This file is the source of truth for **what to build and in what order**. `AGENTS.md` and `.agents/rules/` say **how to behave**. If they conflict, stop and ask.

# Agent Protocol (read before every phase)

The user (Moto) is vibecoding under supervision. Work in **phases**. Each phase ends at a **gate** where you stop.

## **Before writing code for any phase**
1. Read `AGENTS.md` which is mapping to gemini.md and `.agents/rules/` .
2. Reply with: the files you will create or modify, any new dependency (none unless the phase lists it), your assumptions, and any questions. **Wait for "go".**

## **After writing code for any phase**
1. Update the `.docs/progress-log.md` for Changes as it is saving progress with (Phase-no, phase todolist, phase issues and its status, and Important points)
2. Suggest git add and commit command to run in response

## **While implementing:**
- Do only the current phase. Do not build ahead.
- For `config.py`, `db.py`, `repository.py` and `services.py`: write the pytest tests also to test.
- Never add a dependency, change the stack, or edit `pyproject.toml` dependency lists without approval (see `init-and-techstack.md`).
- Do not edit `.agents/workflows/initial-plan.md`, `.agents/` or `.my/` without asking when sugestion.

# Architecture Rules (non-negotiable)

Think of a restaurant: the UI is the waiter (talks to the customer), `services` is the kitchen (the logic), `repository` is the pantry clerk (the only one who touches storage). The waiter never walks into the pantry.

| Layer | May import | Must not |
|---|---|---|
| `ui/*` | `services`, `models`, `ui.theme` | import `sqlite3`, `db`, or `repository` |
| `services` | `repository`, `models` | import `tkinter` |
| `repository` | `db`, `models` | contain UI or business rules; it is the only place with SQL |
| `db` | `sqlite3`, `config` | contain queries for features |
| `desktop` | `subprocess`, `fcntl`, `pathlib` | be called from `repository` |

Concurrency rules (Tk is a single-threaded event loop, like a game loop; touching widgets from another thread is a data race):
1. **Tk widgets are touched only on the main thread.**
2. Other threads (the tray) send commands through a `queue.Queue`. The Tk loop drains it with `root.after(100, ...)`.
3. Reminders use `root.after()` ticks, not a polling thread.
4. `subprocess` is called only from `desktop.py`, always with an argument list.
5. Timestamps are stored in UTC as ISO-8601 text and converted to local time only at display.

# GUI limitations (Wayland / XWayland)

Tkinter is an X11 toolkit. On Wayland it runs through XWayland. Treat these as facts to test, not assumptions:

- **Always-on-top (`-topmost`)** usually works against other XWayland windows. It may not stay above native Wayland windows (Files, GNOME Terminal). Must be tested in Phase 2.
- **Window position:** `geometry("+x+y")` may be ignored or approximate. Save the last position and verify it restores.
- **Transparency:** `-alpha` needs a compositor. True per-pixel shaped windows are unreliable. Fake a "glow" by drawing on a `Canvas` inside a borderless window and animating colors with `after()`.
- **Borderless (`overrideredirect(True)`)** windows can lose keyboard focus on some setups. The floating widget should be click and drag only. Text input happens in the panel, which has normal window decorations.
- **HiDPI:** fractional scaling can make Tk look blurry. Check `tk scaling` if it does.
- **Tray:** GNOME has no legacy tray. Ubuntu ships the AppIndicator extension enabled by default, and pystray must use its AppIndicator backend.
- **Fallbacks if a limit blocks the app:** present options under `ask-before-assuming.md`. Do not switch toolkits or add X11 hacks on your own.

# Target Layout (modules are created in the phase noted)

```
src/plus_ultra/
├── __init__.py
├── main.py          # P1: main(): args, logging, lock, db, UI. Console script target.
├── config.py        # P1: XDG paths (data, state, runtime), constants
├── db.py            # P3: connect, PRAGMAs, migrations, transaction() helper
├── models.py        # P3: dataclasses Task, Category, Reminder
├── repository.py    # P3: all SQL
├── services.py      # P4: TaskService, ReminderService
├── scheduler.py     # P5: reminder tick
├── tray.py          # P5: pystray in a worker thread
├── desktop.py       # P5-6: notify-send, single-instance lock, autostart
└── ui/
    ├── __init__.py
    ├── theme.py     # P2: colors, fonts, glow palette
    ├── floating.py  # P2: the floating widget
    ├── panel.py     # P4: task list and add/edit dialogs
    └── splash.py    # P6: startup log window
tests/               # test_config.py, test_db.py, test_repository.py, test_services.py, ...
```

Never name a module after a standard-library module (`platform`, `queue`, `code`, `logging`, `signal`).

CLI flags (via `argparse` in `main.py`), added when needed: `--smoke-test` (P4), `--install-autostart` and `--uninstall-autostart` (P6), `--import-json PATH` (P7).

# Data Model (SQLite)

Created in Phase 3. Versioned with `PRAGMA user_version` and an ordered list of migrations in `db.py`.

```sql
CREATE TABLE categories (
    id   INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE
);

CREATE TABLE tasks (
    id          INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    title       TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(title) > 0),
    notes       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    category_id INTEGER REFERENCES categories(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    status      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'todo' CHECK (status IN ('todo', 'doing', 'done')),
    created_at  TEXT NOT NULL,   -- UTC ISO-8601
    updated_at  TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE reminders (
    id        INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    task_id   INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES tasks(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    remind_at TEXT NOT NULL,     -- UTC ISO-8601
    fired_at  TEXT               -- NULL until the notification is sent
);

-- Partial index: the scheduler only ever asks "what is due and not yet fired?"
CREATE INDEX idx_reminders_due ON reminders(remind_at) WHERE fired_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE settings (
    key   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    value TEXT NOT NULL          -- e.g. widget_x, widget_y
);
```

Connection setup: `PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;` and `PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;` on every connection. All queries are parameterized.

# Phases

### Phase 0: Repo hygiene (Moto does this, no agent)
- [ ] Replace the four rule files and `pyproject.toml` with the corrected versions.
- [ ] Rename `genral-project-structure.md` to `general-project-structure.md`.
- [ ] Rename `skills/finish-project-security/skill.md` to `SKILL.md`.
- [ ] Delete the stray `.my/prompt` file (keep `.my/prompts/`).
- [ ] Move `GEMINI.md` to the project root (or reduce it to a one-line pointer to `AGENTS.md`). Add a line to `AGENTS.md`: "Read `.agents/workflows/initial-plan.md` and follow its Agent Protocol."
- [ ] Add `.venv/`, `__pycache__/` to `.gitignore`. Exclude `.venv` from Drive sync if the folder syncs.
- [ ] `uv sync`, `uv run plus-ultra`, `git commit`.

### Phase 1: Foundation
**Build:** `config.py` (data dir `$XDG_DATA_HOME/plus-ultra`, state dir `$XDG_STATE_HOME/plus-ultra`, falling back to `~/.local/share` and `~/.local/state`; create dirs with mode `0o700`), logging to `launcher.log` in the state dir, `main.py` with `main()` that logs startup and exits. `uv add --dev ruff pytest`.
**Tests:** path resolution with and without the env vars (use `monkeypatch`).
**Gate:** `uv run plus-ultra` writes startup lines to `launcher.log` and exits 0. ruff and pytest pass.
**Concepts:** XDG base directories, file permissions.

### Phase 2: Wayland spike (go / no-go)
**Build:** `ui/theme.py` and `ui/floating.py`: a borderless, ~90px circular-looking widget (dark rounded canvas), `-topmost`, drag to move, a slow color pulse via `after()`, click toggles a placeholder state. Runs from `main.py`. Position is kept in memory only (persistence arrives in Phase 3).
**Moto tests manually and records results in the log:** stays above a browser? above Files or GNOME Terminal? drag works? survives switching workspaces? pulse smooth? Does it look blurry?
**Gate:** Moto says "go" or "no-go". On no-go, the agent presents options and trade-offs and waits.
**Concepts:** event loop vs threads, window stacking, compositor.

### Phase 3: Database layer
**Build:** `db.py`, `models.py`, `repository.py` per section 6. Migration runner using `user_version`. A `transaction()` context manager that commits on success and rolls back on error. CRUD for tasks, categories, reminders, settings. Wire widget position save and restore through a small service call or a direct setting read in `main.py` (not from `ui/`).
**Tests (first):** constraints (empty title rejected, bad status rejected), `ON DELETE CASCADE` and `SET NULL`, rollback leaves no partial rows, migrations are idempotent, `EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN` shows `idx_reminders_due` is used.
**Gate:** all tests pass; the widget reopens at its last position.
**Concepts:** ACID, primary and foreign keys, normalization (3NF), indexes, migrations.

### Phase 4: Services and panel UI
**Build:** `services.py` (`TaskService`: add, edit, set status, delete, list by category or status). `ui/panel.py`: a normal `Toplevel` with a task list (`ttk.Treeview`), add and edit dialogs, category filter, status toggle. Clicking the widget opens or closes the panel. Add the `--smoke-test` flag: build the UI, `after(1500, root.destroy)`, exit 0.
**Tests:** `test_services.py` against a temp database.
**Gate:** Moto adds three tasks, restarts, and they persist. `xvfb-run -a uv run plus-ultra --smoke-test` exits 0.
**Concepts:** layering (MVC-style), separation of concerns.

### Phase 5: Reminders, notifications, tray
**Decision D1 (needs Moto's approval before coding):** pystray's AppIndicator backend needs system bindings that a uv-managed Python venv may not see. The agent first runs a **tray spike** (just an icon and a Quit item) and reports whether it appears in the GNOME top bar. If not, present these options:
1. Install the AppIndicator typelib and PyGObject through apt, and create the venv from the system Python 3.12 with system site packages. Likely works; touches the environment.
2. Build PyGObject inside the venv with the apt build dependencies. Heavier setup.
3. Drop the tray. The floating widget and notifications are enough. Requires approving a stack change.

Not verified which works on Moto's machine. Do not pick one without asking.

**Build:** `scheduler.py` (a tick every `REMINDER_POLL_MS = 10_000` that asks the service for due, unfired reminders, notifies, and marks them fired inside one transaction). `desktop.notify()` using `notify-send` with an argument list and a timeout. `tray.py` with `run_detached()` and a command queue drained on the Tk thread (menu: Show, Add task, Quit). Add `pystray` and `Pillow` with `uv add` once D1 is settled.
**Tests:** scheduler logic with a fake clock and fake notifier; no real `notify-send` in tests.
**Gate:** a reminder set 1 minute ahead fires with the panel closed, exactly once; the tray menu works; Quit exits cleanly.
**Concepts:** threads vs event loop, producer-consumer queue, subprocess, atomic mark-as-fired.

### Phase 6: Robustness and startup
**Decision D2 (needs approval):** `~/My-Drive` may not be mounted at login. If autostart fails for that reason, options are: copy the project to a local path, or `uv tool install .` so the launcher lives in `~/.local/bin`. The second changes the launch command, so it needs approval.

**Build:**
- Single instance: `fcntl.flock` on `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/plus-ultra.lock` (fallback to the state dir). A second launch exits with a clear message.
- Graceful shutdown on `SIGINT` and `SIGTERM`: stop the tray, close the database.
- `--install-autostart` and `--uninstall-autostart`: write or remove `~/.config/autostart/plus-ultra.desktop` with resolved absolute paths.
- `ui/splash.py`: a small log window showing startup steps (`[1/4] Paths`, `[2/4] Database`, `[3/4] Tray`, `[4/4] Ready`), auto-closing after about 3 seconds. Steps are also written to `launcher.log`.

**Gate:** a second launch is refused; `kill` shuts the app down cleanly with no stack trace; after logout and login the app starts by itself.
**Concepts:** file locking, signals, process lifecycle, XDG autostart.

### Phase 7: Polish and report
**Build:** finish `ui/theme.py` (the glow), README (setup with `uv sync`, how to run, how to autostart), and `--import-json PATH` for the old app's data. **Moto must supply a sample of the old JSON first; the agent must not guess the schema.** Optional backlog from the old app: clipboard and quick-links categories, color-coded task states.
**Report:** `docs/concepts.md` mapping each OS and DBMS concept to the file and function where it appears (XDG paths in `config.py`, locking in `desktop.py`, ACID in `db.transaction`, indexes in the migration, and so on).
**Gate:** a fresh clone, `uv sync` and `uv run plus-ultra` work end to end.

# Moto's Supervision Checklist (every gate)

1. Read the diff. If you cannot explain a function, ask the agent to explain it before accepting.
2. Run the app yourself. Agent-reported success is not verification, especially for Wayland, tray and notifications.
3. Run `uv run ruff check . && uv run pytest`.
4. `git commit -m "phase-N: <what>"`.
5. Paste the agent's draft into `.my/progress-log.md`.
6. Start the next phase in a **fresh chat** so context stays small. The log file carries the memory.

# Definition of Done

- All phase gates passed and committed.
- `uv sync && uv run plus-ultra` works on a fresh clone.
- Floating widget, panel, tray, reminders and notifications work on Wayland, or the limits are documented with the decisions taken.
- `ruff` and `pytest` are clean.
- `docs/concepts.md` maps every OS and DBMS concept to code.
- No secrets or personal data committed (run the `finish-project-security` skill).

========== FILE: ./README.md ==========
# PLUS-ULTRA 🚀

A user-facing floating desktop assistant for Ubuntu (GNOME on Wayland) engineered as a Problem-Based Learning (PBL) project. PLUS-ULTRA starts with the user's session, displays a compact top-center hovering presence, manages tasks and reminders across customizable categories, and serves as an interactive demonstration of core Operating System (OS) concepts (process lifecycle, mutual exclusion via advisory locks, permissions, signals) and Database Management System (DBMS) concepts (ACID transactions, Write-Ahead Logging, schema evolution, cascading soft deletes, indexed queries).

---

# Project Folder Structure

PLUS-ULTRA enforces a strict 3-layer decoupled architecture (`ui/` -> `services/` -> `repositories/`, `database/`) to isolate presentation from business logic and SQLite persistence:

```text
plus-ultra/
├── .agents/                      # AI rules, agent workflows, and guidelines
├── .docs/                        # Architecture, API specs, features, and logs
│   ├── api.md                    # Public interfaces and module specifications
│   ├── architecture.MD           # Project architecture, tech-stack, and deps
│   ├── commands.md               # CLI commands, test flags, manual checklists
│   ├── features.md               # 6-tab workspace & sub-section specs
│   └── progress-log.md           # Chronological development log
├── .env.sample                   # Sample secrets template (Supabase public key)
├── .scripts/                      # Standalone maintenance and seed utilities

│   └── seeds.py                  # Demo seed dataset & standalone CLI
```

---

# Project Tech-Stack

The technology choices are locked and adhere to Problem-Based Learning objectives:

| Component | Technology | Rationale & Concept |
|---|---|---|
| **Host Platform** | Ubuntu Linux (GNOME on Wayland) | Target OS. Tkinter runs via XWayland. |
| **Language Runtime** | CPython 3.12+ | Managed via `uv`. Modern typing, pattern matching. |
| **Package & Env Manager** | `uv` | High-performance virtualenv and dependency locking. |
| **GUI Toolkit** | `tkinter` & `tkinter.ttk` | Standard library GUI toolkit. Kept strictly on UI thread. |
| **Database Engine** | `sqlite3` (Standard Library) | Raw parameterized SQL only (no ORM). WAL mode, foreign keys (`PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON`), ACID transactions. |
| **Machine State Persistence** | SQLite `app_state` table | Dedicated key-value store for machine state (`widget_x`, `widget_y`). Kept separate from user preferences. |
| **User Settings Engine** | Custom JSON Engine | Atomic file replacement (`os.replace`, `0o600`), strict type safety, automatic quarantine of corrupt files. |
| **Process Management** | `fcntl.flock` (Standard Library) | Single-instance mutual exclusion via POSIX advisory file locking. |
| **Desktop Notifications** | `notify-send` via `subprocess` | Desktop notification dispatch without heavyweight dependencies. |
| **Code Quality & Testing** | `ruff` & `pytest` | Fast linting/formatting (strict 80-char line limit) and automated test suite. |

---

# Project Dependencies

All dependencies are defined in `pyproject.toml` and pinned in `uv.lock`.

## Runtime Dependencies
The core application currently relies entirely on the **Python Standard Library** with zero third-party packages:
- `argparse`: Command-line interface argument parsing.
- `contextlib`: Resource management and exception suppression.
- `dataclasses`: Strongly typed domain entities (`Category`, `Task`, `Reminder`, `AppState`).
- `datetime`: ISO-8601 UTC timestamp generation (`datetime.UTC`).
- `fcntl`: POSIX file locking (`flock`) for single-instance enforcement.
- `importlib.metadata` & `importlib.resources`: Version discovery and packaged resource access.
- `json`: User preference serialization and metadata decoding.
- `logging`: Dual logging (console stdout and persistent `launcher.log`).
- `os` & `stat`: Filesystem permissions (`0o700`, `0o600`) and atomic replace (`os.replace`).
- `pathlib`: Object-oriented path resolution per XDG standards.
- `queue`: Thread-safe cross-thread messaging.
- `signal`: POSIX signal trapping (`SIGINT`, `SIGTERM`) for clean shutdown.
- `sqlite3`: Direct ACID transactional database management and migrations.
- `subprocess`: Safe argument-list invocation of `notify-send`.
- `threading`: Background task execution (scheduler, sync).
- `tkinter`: Native desktop GUI rendering and event dispatch.

## Development Dependencies
- **`pytest >= 9.1.1`**: Automated test framework (81 tests across 10 test modules).
- **`ruff >= 0.17.0`**: Fast Python linter and formatter enforcing PEP 8, import sorting, and strict 80-character line lengths.


## Scheduled Runtime Dependencies (Phased)
- **Phase 5**: Decision D1 resolved to drop external tray libraries (`pystray`/`Pillow`) to maintain a 100% pure Python standard library stack with zero C-binding dependencies.
- **Phase 7**: Supabase client library (Incremental cloud sync).

---

## Quick Start

```bash
# Sync environment dependencies
uv sync

# Run the test suite
uv run pytest -v

# Launch the floating desktop assistant
uv run plus-ultra

# Run transient smoke test (validates GUI, DB, and lock, then exits cleanly in 500ms)
uv run plus-ultra --smoke-test
```

        with transaction(conn) as c:
            c.executemany(
                "INSERT INTO quick_links ("
                "id, name, target_uri, category, click_count, created_at, "
                "updated_at, is_deleted) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);",
                demo_links,
            )

    # Categories & Tasks
    cur = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tasks WHERE is_deleted = 0;")
    if cur.fetchone()[0] == 0:
        cat_cur = conn.execute(
            "SELECT id FROM categories WHERE name = 'College' "
            "AND is_deleted = 0;"
        )
        cat_row = cat_cur.fetchone()
        if not cat_row:
            cat_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
            with transaction(conn) as c:
                c.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO categories ("
                    "id, name, color, created_at, updated_at, is_deleted) "
                    "VALUES (?, 'College', '#00e5ff', ?, ?, 0);",
                    (cat_id, now, now),
                )
        else:
            cat_id = cat_row[0]

        demo_tasks = [
            (
                uuid.uuid4().hex,
                cat_id,
                "OS Lab Concurrency Semaphore Assignment",
                "Implement reader-writer lock using POSIX semaphores.",
                "2026-10-25",
                "Pending",
                "College",
                "High",
                "Short-term",
                0,
                now,
                now,
                0,
            ),
            (
                uuid.uuid4().hex,
                cat_id,
                "DBMS B+ Tree Index Optimization",
                "Analyze query plans and write benchmark script.",
                "2026-10-30",
                "In Progress",
                "College",
                "Medium",
                "Short-term",
                0,
                now,
                now,
                0,
            ),
        ]
        with transaction(conn) as c:
            c.executemany(
                "INSERT INTO tasks (id, category_id, title, description, "
                "due_date, status, task_label, critical_level, pace_level, "
                "is_google_task, created_at, updated_at, is_deleted) "
                "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);",
                demo_tasks,
            )


def main() -> None:
    """CLI script entrypoint to seed database with demo data."""
    conn = get_connection()
    print("Seeding demo workspace data...")
    seed_demo_workspace_data(conn)
    print("Seeding complete.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/config.py ==========
"""Configuration and XDG base directory resolution for PLUS-ULTRA.

Applies OS concepts:
- XDG Base Directory specification for separating data, state, and runtime.
- POSIX filesystem security: private directories created with mode 0o700
  (rwx------) to protect sensitive user notes, tasks, and credentials.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import importlib.metadata
import importlib.resources
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

# Shared application constants
APP_NAME = "plus-ultra"
DB_FILENAME = "plus-ultra.sqlite3"
LOG_FILENAME = "launcher.log"
USER_SETTINGS_FILENAME = "user-settings.json"


def _ensure_private_directory(path: Path) -> Path:
    """Ensure directory exists and enforce private mode 0o700 (owner only).

    OS concept: Filesystem permissions. 0o700 ensures that other local users
    on a shared Linux workstation cannot read or modify user task data/logs.
    """
    path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
        path.chmod(0o700)
    return path


def get_data_dir() -> Path:
    """Resolve and create application data directory.

    Conforms to the XDG Base Directory specification:
    - $XDG_DATA_HOME/plus-ultra
    - Fallback: ~/.local/share/plus-ultra
    """
    raw_env = os.environ.get("XDG_DATA_HOME", "").strip()
    base_dir = Path(raw_env) if raw_env else Path.home() / ".local" / "share"
    target_dir = base_dir / APP_NAME
    return _ensure_private_directory(target_dir)


def get_state_dir() -> Path:
    """Resolve and create application state directory for logs.

    Conforms to the XDG Base Directory specification:
    - $XDG_STATE_HOME/plus-ultra
    - Fallback: ~/.local/state/plus-ultra
    """
    raw_env = os.environ.get("XDG_STATE_HOME", "").strip()
    base_dir = Path(raw_env) if raw_env else Path.home() / ".local" / "state"
    target_dir = base_dir / APP_NAME
    return _ensure_private_directory(target_dir)


def get_db_path() -> Path:
    """Return path to SQLite database file in data directory."""
    return get_data_dir() / DB_FILENAME


def get_log_path() -> Path:
    """Return path to launcher log file in state directory."""
    return get_state_dir() / LOG_FILENAME


def get_config_dir() -> Path:
    """Resolve and create application configuration directory.

    Conforms to the XDG Base Directory specification:
    - $XDG_CONFIG_HOME/plus-ultra
    - Fallback: ~/.config/plus-ultra
    """
    raw_env = os.environ.get("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", "").strip()
    base_dir = Path(raw_env) if raw_env else Path.home() / ".config"
    target_dir = base_dir / APP_NAME
    return _ensure_private_directory(target_dir)


def get_user_settings_path() -> Path:
    """Return path to user settings JSON file in configuration directory."""
    return get_config_dir() / USER_SETTINGS_FILENAME


def get_app_version() -> str:
    """Return installed application package version with fallback."""
    try:
        return importlib.metadata.version(APP_NAME)
    except importlib.metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
        return "0.1.0"


def get_metadata() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Load static application metadata from packaged resources."""
    resources = importlib.resources.files("plus_ultra.resources")
    ref = resources.joinpath("data", "metadata.json")
    if not ref.is_file():
        ref = resources.joinpath("metadata.json")
    with ref.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/database/connection.py ==========
"""SQLite database connection management and transaction context manager."""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import logging
import sqlite3
from collections.abc import Iterator
from pathlib import Path

from plus_ultra import config

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def get_connection(db_path: Path | None = None) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    """Create and configure a connection to the SQLite database.

    Enforces:
    - Foreign keys enabled.
    - Write-Ahead Logging (WAL) journal mode.
    - Busy timeout for concurrency resilience.
    - Row factory returning dict-accessible rows.
    """
    path = db_path or config.get_db_path()
    config.get_data_dir()  # Ensure data directory exists with mode 0o700

    conn = sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=5.0)
    conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;")
    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;")
    conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000;")
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return conn


@contextlib.contextmanager
def transaction(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Cursor]:
    """Context manager for ACID transactions.

    Executes a block of operations atomically:
    - Commits automatically if the block finishes without error.
    - Rolls back immediately on any unhandled exception.
    """
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    try:
        yield cursor
        conn.commit()
    except Exception as exc:
        conn.rollback()
        logger.error("Transaction rolled back due to error: %s", exc)
        raise

========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/database/migrations.py ==========
"""Database migration runner and schema version management."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

from plus_ultra.database.connection import get_connection, transaction
from plus_ultra.database.schema import (
    MIGRATION_V1_SQL,
    MIGRATION_V2_SQL,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def get_schema_version(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> int:
    """Return current PRAGMA user_version from SQLite database."""
    cur = conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version;")
    row = cur.fetchone()
    return int(row[0]) if row else 0


def set_schema_version(conn: sqlite3.Connection, version: int) -> None:
    """Set PRAGMA user_version on SQLite database."""
    conn.execute(f"PRAGMA user_version = {int(version)};")


def init_db(db_path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    """Run pending database schema migrations incrementally."""
    conn = get_connection(db_path)
    try:
        current_version = get_schema_version(conn)
        logger.info("Current SQLite database version: %d", current_version)

        if current_version < 1:
            logger.info("Applying Migration 1 (core schema)...")
            with transaction(conn) as cur:
                cur.executescript(MIGRATION_V1_SQL)
            set_schema_version(conn, 1)
            logger.info("Database migrated successfully to version 1.")

        if current_version < 2:
            logger.info("Applying Migration 2 (workspace tabs)...")
            with transaction(conn) as cur:
                cur.executescript(MIGRATION_V2_SQL)
            set_schema_version(conn, 2)
            logger.info("Database migrated successfully to version 2.")

    finally:
        conn.close()

"""SQL DDL schema definitions and migrations for PLUS-ULTRA."""

from __future__ import annotations

CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION = 2

MIGRATION_V1_SQL = """
-- Categories table (reusable across tasks and features)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS categories (
    id         TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    name       TEXT NOT NULL,
    color      TEXT DEFAULT '#4a5568',
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    synced_at  TEXT,
    is_deleted INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);

-- Partial index: allow reusing category names if previous one was soft-deleted
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_categories_name_active
    ON categories(name) WHERE is_deleted = 0;

-- Tasks table with priority, labels, pace, and cloud readiness
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks (
    id             TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    category_id    TEXT REFERENCES categories(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    title          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(trim(title)) > 0),
    description    TEXT DEFAULT '',
    due_date       TEXT,
    status         TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Pending'
                   CHECK(status IN (
                       'Pending', 'In Progress', 'Completed', 'Overdue'
                   )),
    task_label     TEXT DEFAULT 'College',
    critical_level TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Medium'
                   CHECK(critical_level IN ('High', 'Medium', 'Low')),
    pace_level     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Short-term'
                   CHECK(pace_level IN ('Long-term', 'Short-term')),
    is_google_task INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(is_google_task IN (0, 1)),
    created_at     TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at     TEXT NOT NULL,
    synced_at      TEXT,
    is_deleted     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);

-- Reminders table linked to tasks
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reminders (
    id         TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    task_id    TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tasks(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    remind_at  TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    synced_at  TEXT,
    is_deleted INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);

-- Partial index: scheduler queries active reminders
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reminders_due
    ON reminders(remind_at) WHERE is_deleted = 0;

-- Local-only table: reminder fired state is device-specific
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reminder_fires (
    reminder_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES reminders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    fired_at    TEXT NOT NULL
);

-- machine-written state only (widget_x, widget_y, last_synced_at);
-- user preferences live only in user-settings.json
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app_state (
    key   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    value TEXT NOT NULL
);
"""

========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/desktop.py ==========
"""Desktop platform adapter and process management for PLUS-ULTRA.

Applies OS concepts:
- Mutual Exclusion & Single Instance: POSIX advisory file locking (fcntl.flock)
  ensures only one application process runs concurrently per user session.
- Process lifecycle: Clean lock acquisition, PID recording, and release on
  normal shutdown or signal termination.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import fcntl
import logging
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

from plus_ultra import config

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

LOCK_FILENAME = "plus-ultra.lock"


class SingleInstanceLock:
    """Manages an exclusive advisory file lock for the application process."""

    def __init__(self, lock_path: Path | None = None) -> None:
        self.lock_path = lock_path or (config.get_state_dir() / LOCK_FILENAME)
        self._fd: int | None = None

    def acquire(self) -> bool:
        """Attempt to acquire exclusive advisory lock without blocking.

        Returns:
            True if lock was acquired, False if another instance holds it.
        """
        config.get_state_dir()  # Ensure state directory exists (0o700)
        try:
            fd = os.open(
                self.lock_path,
                os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT,
                0o600,
            )
            # Try exclusive non-blocking lock
            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)

            # Record PID for diagnostic inspection
            os.ftruncate(fd, 0)
            os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
            os.write(fd, f"{os.getpid()}\n".encode())

            self._fd = fd
            logger.info(
                "Acquired single-instance lock on %s (pid: %d)",
                self.lock_path,
                os.getpid(),
            )
            return True
        except (BlockingIOError, OSError) as exc:
            logger.warning(
                "Single-instance lock contention on %s: %s",
                self.lock_path,
                exc,
            )
            with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                if "fd" in locals():
                    os.close(fd)
            return False

    def release(self) -> None:
        """Release the advisory lock and close the file descriptor."""
        if self._fd is not None:
            try:
                fcntl.flock(self._fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
                os.close(self._fd)
                logger.info(
                    "Released single-instance lock on %s", self.lock_path
                )
            except OSError as err:
                logger.error("Error releasing lock: %s", err)
            finally:
                self._fd = None
                with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                    self.lock_path.unlink()

    def __enter__(self) -> bool:
        return self.acquire()

    def __exit__(
        self, exc_type: object, exc_val: object, exc_tb: object
    ) -> None:
        self.release()


def apply_window_shape_mask(
    window_id: int, width: int, height: int, radius: int
) -> bool:
    """Clip XWayland window to capsule shape using XShape extension.

    Returns True if the mask was applied, False if Xlib/XShape is unavailable.
    """
    try:
        import ctypes
        import ctypes.util

        x11_name = ctypes.util.find_library("X11") or "libX11.so.6"
        xext_name = ctypes.util.find_library("Xext") or "libXext.so.6"
        x11 = ctypes.cdll.LoadLibrary(x11_name)
        xext = ctypes.cdll.LoadLibrary(xext_name)

        display = x11.XOpenDisplay(None)
        if not display:
            return False

        try:
            event_base = ctypes.c_int()
            error_base = ctypes.c_int()
            if not xext.XShapeQueryExtension(
                display, ctypes.byref(event_base), ctypes.byref(error_base)
            ):
                return False

            row_bytes = (width + 7) // 8
            data = bytearray(row_bytes * height)
            r = radius
            r_sq = r * r

            for y in range(height):
                for x in range(width):
                    inside = False
                    if r <= x <= width - r:
                        inside = True
                    elif x < r:
                        inside = (x - r) ** 2 + (y - r) ** 2 <= r_sq
                    else:
                        inside = (x - (width - r)) ** 2 + (y - r) ** 2 <= r_sq

                    if inside:
                        byte_idx = y * row_bytes + (x // 8)
                        bit_idx = x % 8
                        data[byte_idx] |= 1 << bit_idx

            bitmap_data = (ctypes.c_char * len(data)).from_buffer(data)
            pixmap = x11.XCreateBitmapFromData(
                display, window_id, bitmap_data, width, height
            )
            if not pixmap:
                return False

            # 0: ShapeBounding, 0: ShapeSet
            xext.XShapeCombineMask(display, window_id, 0, 0, 0, pixmap, 0)
            x11.XFreePixmap(display, pixmap)
            x11.XFlush(display)
            return True
        finally:
            x11.XCloseDisplay(display)
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.debug("Window shape mask skipped or unsupported: %s", exc)
        return False


def send_desktop_notification(
    title: str,
    message: str = "",
    urgency: str = "normal",
    timeout_ms: int = 5000,
    app_name: str = config.APP_NAME,
) -> bool:
    """Send a native Linux desktop notification via notify-send without shell.

    Args:
        title: Notification summary heading.
        message: Notification body text.
        urgency: Urgency level ('low', 'normal', 'critical').
        timeout_ms: Expiration timeout in milliseconds.
        app_name: Originating application identifier.

    Returns:
        True if notify-send completed successfully, False otherwise.
    """
    executable = shutil.which("notify-send")
    if not executable:
        logger.warning(
            "notify-send executable not found; desktop notification skipped."
        )
        return False

    cmd = [
        executable,
        "-a",
        app_name,
        "-u",
        urgency,
        "-t",
        str(timeout_ms),
        title,
        message,
    ]
    try:
        subprocess.run(
            cmd,
            check=True,
            capture_output=True,
            timeout=3,
        )
        logger.info("Desktop notification dispatched: %s", title)
        return True
    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as exc:
        logger.warning("Failed to dispatch desktop notification: %s", exc)
        return False


notify = send_desktop_notification

========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/main.py ==========
"""Main application entry point for PLUS-ULTRA.

Applies OS concepts:
- Mutual Exclusion & Single Instance: Advisory locking (fcntl.flock).
- Process Lifecycle: Signal handling (SIGINT, SIGTERM) and clean shutdown.
- Standardized logging to persistent storage in the XDG state directory.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import logging
import os
import signal
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from plus_ultra.config import (
    APP_NAME,
    get_app_version,
    get_config_dir,
    get_data_dir,
    get_log_path,
    get_metadata,
    get_state_dir,
)
from plus_ultra.database import (
    get_connection,
    init_db,

    OpportunityService,
    QuickLinkService,
    ReminderService,
    TaskService,
)
from plus_ultra.ui.floating import FloatingWidget
from plus_ultra.ui.panel import DashboardPanel

logger = logging.getLogger(APP_NAME)


def configure_logging() -> Path:
    """Set up dual logging to console and launcher.log in state directory.

    Returns the path to the active log file.
    """
    log_file = get_log_path()
    formatter = logging.Formatter(
        fmt="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s",
        datefmt="%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S",
    )

    app_logger = logging.getLogger(APP_NAME)
    app_logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    app_logger.handlers.clear()

    # File handler (persistent record in XDG state directory)
    file_handler = logging.FileHandler(log_file, encoding="utf-8")
    file_handler.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
    app_logger.addHandler(file_handler)

    # Console stream handler
    console_handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
    console_handler.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    console_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
    app_logger.addHandler(console_handler)

    return log_file


def parse_args(args: list[str] | None = None) -> argparse.Namespace:
    """Parse command line arguments."""
    meta = get_metadata()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog=meta.get("app_name", APP_NAME),
        description=(
            f"{meta.get('display_name', APP_NAME)}: "
            f"{meta.get('description', '')}"
        ),
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-ui",
        action="store_true",
        help="Initialize foundation and logging without starting GUI.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--smoke-test",
        action="store_true",
        help="Launch floating widget, wait briefly, and exit cleanly.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--seed",
        action="store_true",
        help="Seed or reseed demo workspace data from .scripts/seeds.py.",
    )
    return parser.parse_args(args)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Main application routine."""
    args = parse_args(argv)
    log_path = configure_logging()

    meta = get_metadata()
    version = get_app_version()
    logger.info("PLUS-ULTRA initializing...")
    logger.info(
        "Application: %s v%s (%s)",
        meta.get("display_name", APP_NAME),
        version,
        meta.get("company", ""),
    )
    logger.info("Data directory: %s", get_data_dir())
    logger.info("State directory: %s", get_state_dir())
    logger.info("Config directory: %s", get_config_dir())
    logger.info("Log file: %s", log_path)
    logger.info("PLUS-ULTRA foundation initialized successfully.")

    lock = SingleInstanceLock()
    if not lock.acquire():
        logger.warning("Another instance of PLUS-ULTRA is already running.")
        print("PLUS-ULTRA is already running.")
        return 0

    try:
        init_db()
        conn = get_connection()
        seed_demo_workspace_data(conn)

        app_state_repo = AppStateRepository(conn)
        task_repo = TaskRepository(conn)
        cat_repo = CategoryRepository(conn)
        rem_repo = ReminderRepository(conn)
        code_repo = CodeRepository(conn)
        course_repo = CourseRepository(conn)
        idea_repo = IdeaDoubtRepository(conn)
        opp_repo = OpportunityRepository(conn)
        link_repo = QuickLinkRepository(conn)

        task_service = TaskService(task_repo, cat_repo)
        cat_service = CategoryService(cat_repo)
        rem_service = ReminderService(rem_repo, task_repo)
        code_service = CodeService(code_repo)
        course_service = CourseService(course_repo)
        idea_service = IdeaDoubtService(idea_repo)
        opp_service = OpportunityService(opp_repo)
        link_service = QuickLinkService(link_repo)

        if args.no_ui or (
            "PYTEST_CURRENT_TEST" in os.environ and not args.smoke_test
        ):
            conn.close()
            return 0

        saved_pos = app_state_repo.get_widget_position()
        init_x = saved_pos[0] if saved_pos else None
        init_y = saved_pos[1] if saved_pos else None

        widget = FloatingWidget(
            initial_x=init_x,
            initial_y=init_y,
            on_position_change=app_state_repo.set_widget_position,
        )

        dashboard = DashboardPanel(
            parent=widget.root,
            task_service=task_service,
            category_service=cat_service,
            notification_queue=widget.notifications,
            reminder_service=rem_service,
            code_service=code_service,
            course_service=course_service,
            idea_doubt_service=idea_service,
            opportunity_service=opp_service,
            quick_link_service=link_service,
            on_notifications_cleared=widget._update_bell_state,
            on_open=widget.root.withdraw,
            on_close=widget.root.deiconify,
        )
        widget.on_click_callback = dashboard.toggle

        def _handle_reminder_due(task: Task, _rem: Reminder) -> None:
            """Safely enqueue reminder alert to Tkinter main thread."""

            def _ui_action() -> None:
                if not widget._closed:
                    widget.post_notification(
                        title=f"Due: {task.title}",
                        message=task.description
                        or f"Priority: {task.critical_level}",
                    )
                    if dashboard.winfo_exists():
                        dashboard._refresh_tasks()
                        dashboard._refresh_notifications()

            widget.root.after_idle(_ui_action)

        scheduler = ReminderScheduler(
            reminder_service=rem_service,
            on_reminder_due=_handle_reminder_due,
            desktop_notify=True,
        )
        scheduler.start()

        def _signal_handler(_sig: int, _frame: object) -> None:
            logger.info("Received termination signal, shutting down.")
            scheduler.stop()
            if dashboard.winfo_exists():
                dashboard.destroy()
            widget.close()

        signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, _signal_handler)
        signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, _signal_handler)

        if args.smoke_test:
            logger.info(
                "Running in smoke-test mode; verifying scheduler and UI."
            )
            scheduler.tick()
            dashboard.show()
            widget.root.after(500, widget.close)

        try:
            widget.run()
        finally:
            scheduler.stop()
            conn.close()
        return 0
    finally:
        lock.release()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

"""Domain data models for PLUS-ULTRA.

Applies DBMS concepts:
- Relational schema mapping: Strongly-typed dataclasses mirroring SQLite tables.
- Audit & Synchronization metadata: UUID primary keys, ISO-8601 timestamps,

========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/network.py ==========
"""Network status detection and connectivity utility for PLUS-ULTRA."""

from __future__ import annotations

import queue
import socket
import threading


def is_network_online(
    host: str = "1.1.1.1", port: int = 53, timeout: float = 1.0
) -> bool:
    """Check network connectivity via a lightweight socket probe.

    Probes Cloudflare/Google public DNS on port 53 without dependencies.
    """
    try:
        with socket.create_connection((host, port), timeout=timeout):
            return True
    except (OSError, TimeoutError):
        if host != "8.8.8.8":
            try:
                with socket.create_connection(("8.8.8.8", 53), timeout=timeout):
                    return True
            except (OSError, TimeoutError):
                pass
        return False


class NetworkStatusMonitor:
    """Thread-safe network probe monitor using a pure Python queue."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.result_queue: queue.Queue[bool] = queue.Queue()
        self._probing: bool = False

    def probe_async(self) -> None:
        """Start background socket probe if not already running."""
        if self._probing:
            return
        self._probing = True

        def _worker() -> None:
            try:
                online = is_network_online()
                self.result_queue.put(online)
            finally:
                self._probing = False

        thread = threading.Thread(target=_worker, daemon=True)
        thread.start()

    def get_result(self) -> bool | None:
        """Retrieve latest probe result without blocking, or None."""
        try:
            return self.result_queue.get_nowait()
        except queue.Empty:
            return None


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/notifications.py ==========
"""Notification state management for PLUS-ULTRA capsule.

Applies architectural separation:
- Zero Tkinter imports: pure Python data structures for state and sizing.
- Multi-notification queueing, read/unread state tracking.
- Popup notification text formatting and width calculation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, datetime


def _now_utc() -> str:
    """Return current UTC timestamp in ISO-8601 format."""
    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()


@dataclass(slots=True)
class CapsuleNotification:
    """Notification entity displayed in the dynamic capsule."""

    id: str
    title: str
    message: str = ""
    icon: str = ""
    is_read: bool = False
    created_at: str = ""


class CapsuleNotificationQueue:
    """Manages unread notifications and computes dynamic dimensions."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        min_width: int = 160,
        max_width: int = 440,
        char_width_px: int = 7,
    ) -> None:
        self.min_width = min_width
        self.max_width = max_width
        self.char_width_px = char_width_px
        self._items: list[CapsuleNotification] = []

    def push(
        self,
        title: str,
        message: str = "",
        icon: str = "",
        notification_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> CapsuleNotification:
        """Add or update an active notification in the queue."""
        clean_title = title.strip()
        item_id = notification_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
        item = CapsuleNotification(
            id=item_id,
            title=clean_title,
            message=message.strip(),
            icon=icon,
            is_read=False,
            created_at=_now_utc(),
        )
        # If already exists with same ID, replace it; else append to front
        for idx, existing in enumerate(self._items):
            if existing.id == item_id:
                self._items[idx] = item
                return item

        self._items.insert(0, item)
        return item

    def mark_read(self, notification_id: str) -> None:
        """Mark a specific notification as read."""
        for item in self._items:
            if item.id == notification_id:
                item.is_read = True

    def mark_all_read(self) -> None:
        """Mark all notifications as read."""
        for item in self._items:
            item.is_read = True

    def dismiss(self, notification_id: str) -> CapsuleNotification | None:
        """Dismiss and remove a notification by ID."""
        for idx, item in enumerate(self._items):
            if item.id == notification_id:
                return self._items.pop(idx)
        return None

    def peek(self) -> CapsuleNotification | None:
        """Return the most recent notification without removing it."""
        return self._items[0] if self._items else None

    def list_all(self) -> list[CapsuleNotification]:
        """Return a copy of all notifications."""
        return list(self._items)

    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear all notifications."""
        self._items.clear()

    @property
    def unread_count(self) -> int:
        """Return count of unread notifications."""
        return sum(1 for item in self._items if not item.is_read)

    @property
    def has_unread(self) -> bool:
        """Return True if at least one notification is unread."""
        return self.unread_count > 0

    @property
    def count(self) -> int:
        """Return total count of notifications."""
        return len(self._items)

    @property
    def is_empty(self) -> bool:
        """Return True if there are no notifications."""
        return len(self._items) == 0

    def compute_popup_width(self, item: CapsuleNotification) -> int:
        """Compute required width for the 3-second popup capsule."""
        text = self.format_popup_text(item)
        content_len = len(text) + 6  # icon + padding
        estimated_w = 40 + (content_len * self.char_width_px)
        return max(200, min(360, estimated_w))

    def format_popup_text(
        self, item: CapsuleNotification, max_chars: int = 42
    ) -> str:
        """Format and truncate notification for the popup banner."""
        full_text = item.title
        if item.message:
            full_text = f"{item.title}: {item.message}"

        if len(full_text) <= max_chars:
            return full_text
        return full_text[: max_chars - 1].rstrip() + "…"


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/repositories/app_state_repository.py ==========
"""Data access operations for local machine-written runtime state."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3

from plus_ultra.database.connection import transaction


class AppStateRepository:
    """Data access operations for local machine-written runtime state."""

    def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        self.conn = conn

    def get(self, key: str, default: str | None = None) -> str | None:
        """Retrieve a machine-written state value."""
        cur = self.conn.execute(
            "SELECT value FROM app_state WHERE key = ?", (key,)
        )
        row = cur.fetchone()
        return str(row["value"]) if row else default

    def set(self, key: str, value: str) -> None:
        """Set or update a machine-written state value atomically."""
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO app_state (key, value)
                VALUES (?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value
                """,
                (key, value),
            )

    def get_widget_position(self) -> tuple[int, int] | None:
        """Retrieve saved widget screen coordinates (widget_x, widget_y)."""
        x_str = self.get("widget_x")
        y_str = self.get("widget_y")
        if x_str is not None and y_str is not None:
            try:
                return int(x_str), int(y_str)
            except ValueError:
                return None
        return None

    def set_widget_position(self, x: int, y: int) -> None:
        """Save widget screen coordinates to machine state."""
        with transaction(self.conn):
            self.set("widget_x", str(x))
            self.set("widget_y", str(y))

"""Common utilities and base functions for data repositories."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime


def _now_utc() -> str:
    """Return current UTC timestamp in ISO-8601 format."""
    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/repositories/category_repository.py ==========
"""Data access operations for Category entities."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
import uuid

from plus_ultra.database.connection import transaction
from plus_ultra.models import Category
from plus_ultra.repositories.base import _now_utc


def _row_to_category(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Category:
    """Convert an SQLite row to a Category model."""
    return Category(
        id=row["id"],
        name=row["name"],
        color=row["color"],
        created_at=row["created_at"],
        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
        synced_at=row["synced_at"],
        is_deleted=row["is_deleted"],
    )


class CategoryRepository:
    """Data access operations for Category entities."""

    def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        self.conn = conn

    def create(self, name: str, color: str = "#4a5568") -> Category:
        """Create a new category.

        Raises:
            sqlite3.IntegrityError: If active category name already exists.
        """
        cat_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO categories (
                    id, name, color, created_at, updated_at, is_deleted
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)
                """,
                (cat_id, name.strip(), color, now, now),
            )
        return Category(
            id=cat_id,
            name=name.strip(),
            color=color,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )

    def get_by_id(self, category_id: str) -> Category | None:
        """Fetch category by ID, including soft-deleted ones."""
        cur = self.conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM categories WHERE id = ?", (category_id,)
        )
        row = cur.fetchone()
        return _row_to_category(row) if row else None

    def list_active(self) -> list[Category]:
        """Return all active (non-deleted) categories ordered by name."""
        cur = self.conn.execute(
            """
            SELECT * FROM categories
            WHERE is_deleted = 0
            ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE ASC
            """
        )
        return [_row_to_category(row) for row in cur.fetchall()]

    def update(
        self, category_id: str, name: str, color: str
    ) -> Category | None:
        """Update category name and color; sets updated_at timestamp."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE categories
                SET name = ?, color = ?, updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (name.strip(), color, now, category_id),
            )
            if cur.rowcount == 0:
                return None
        return self.get_by_id(category_id)

    def soft_delete(self, category_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft-delete category and nullify linked tasks' category_id."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE categories
                SET is_deleted = 1, updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (now, category_id),
            )
            if cur.rowcount == 0:
                return False

            # Cascade: set tasks.category_id to NULL in same transaction
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE tasks
                SET category_id = NULL, updated_at = ?
                WHERE category_id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (now, category_id),
            )
        return True


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/repositories/code_repository.py ==========
"""Data access operations for CodeItem entities."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
import uuid
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.database.connection import transaction
from plus_ultra.models import CodeItem
from plus_ultra.repositories.base import _now_utc


def _row_to_code_item(row: sqlite3.Row) -> CodeItem:
    """Convert an SQLite row to a CodeItem model."""
    return CodeItem(
        id=row["id"],
        category=row["category"],
        title=row["title"],
        content=row["content"],
        language=row["language"],
        difficulty=row["difficulty"],
        is_done=row["is_done"],
        url=row["url"],
        notes=row["notes"],
        created_at=row["created_at"],
        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
        synced_at=row["synced_at"],
        is_deleted=row["is_deleted"],
    )


class CodeRepository:
    """Data access operations for CodeItem entities."""

    def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        self.conn = conn

    def list_items(
        self,
        category: str | None = None,
        search_query: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[CodeItem]:
        """Query active code items with optional category and search filters."""
        query = ["SELECT * FROM code_items WHERE is_deleted = 0"]
        params: list[Any] = []

        if category and category != "All":
            query.append("AND category = ?")
            params.append(category)

        if search_query:
            query.append("AND (title LIKE ? OR content LIKE ? OR notes LIKE ?)")
            wildcard = f"%{search_query.strip()}%"
            params.extend([wildcard, wildcard, wildcard])

        query.append("ORDER BY created_at DESC")
        cur = self.conn.execute(" ".join(query), params)
        return [_row_to_code_item(row) for row in cur.fetchall()]

    def get(self, item_id: str) -> CodeItem | None:
        """Fetch a single code item by ID."""
        cur = self.conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM code_items WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0",
            (item_id,),
        )
        row = cur.fetchone()
        return _row_to_code_item(row) if row else None

    def create(
        self,
        category: str,
        title: str,
        content: str = "",
        language: str = "python",
        difficulty: str = "Easy",
        is_done: int = 0,
        url: str = "",
        notes: str = "",
    ) -> CodeItem:
        """Create a new code item."""
        now = _now_utc()
        item_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO code_items (
                    id, category, title, content, language, difficulty,
                    is_done, url, notes, created_at, updated_at, is_deleted
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)
                """,
                (
                    item_id,
                    category,
                    title,
                    content,
                    language,
                    difficulty,
                    is_done,
                    url,
                    notes,
                    now,
                    now,
                ),
            )
        return CodeItem(
            id=item_id,
            category=category,
            title=title,
            content=content,
            language=language,
            difficulty=difficulty,
            is_done=is_done,
            url=url,
            notes=notes,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )

    def toggle_done(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Toggle is_done status for a DSA item."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE code_items
                SET is_done = CASE WHEN is_done = 1 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,
                    updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (now, item_id),
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0

    def update(
        self,
        item_id: str,
        category: str | None = None,
        title: str | None = None,
        content: str | None = None,
        language: str | None = None,
        difficulty: str | None = None,
        url: str | None = None,
        notes: str | None = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Update code item fields."""
        fields: list[str] = []
        params: list[Any] = []
        if category is not None:
            fields.append("category = ?")
            params.append(category)
        if title is not None:
            fields.append("title = ?")
            params.append(title)
        if content is not None:
            fields.append("content = ?")
            params.append(content)
        if language is not None:
            fields.append("language = ?")
            params.append(language)
        if difficulty is not None:
            fields.append("difficulty = ?")
            params.append(difficulty)
        if url is not None:
            fields.append("url = ?")
            params.append(url)
        if notes is not None:
            fields.append("notes = ?")
            params.append(notes)
        if not fields:
            return False
        fields.append("updated_at = ?")
        params.extend([_now_utc(), item_id])
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                f"UPDATE code_items SET {', '.join(fields)} "
                "WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0",
                params,
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0

    def soft_delete(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft delete a code item."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE code_items
                SET is_deleted = 1, updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (now, item_id),
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/repositories/course_repository.py ==========
"""Data access operations for Course entities."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
import uuid
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.database.connection import transaction
from plus_ultra.models import Course
from plus_ultra.repositories.base import _now_utc


def _row_to_course(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Course:
    """Convert an SQLite row to a Course model."""
    return Course(
        id=row["id"],
        title=row["title"],
        platform=row["platform"],
        instructor=row["instructor"],
        total_modules=row["total_modules"],
        completed_modules=row["completed_modules"],
        url=row["url"],
        notes=row["notes"],
        created_at=row["created_at"],
        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
        synced_at=row["synced_at"],
        is_deleted=row["is_deleted"],
    )


class CourseRepository:
    """Data access operations for Course entities."""

    def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        self.conn = conn

    def list_courses(
        self,
        platform: str | None = None,
        search_query: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[Course]:
        """List active courses with platform and search filters."""
        query = ["SELECT * FROM courses WHERE is_deleted = 0"]
        params: list[Any] = []

        if platform and platform != "All":
            query.append("AND platform = ?")
            params.append(platform)

        if search_query:
            query.append(
                "AND (title LIKE ? OR instructor LIKE ? OR notes LIKE ?)"
            )
            wildcard = f"%{search_query.strip()}%"
            params.extend([wildcard, wildcard, wildcard])

        query.append("ORDER BY created_at DESC")
        cur = self.conn.execute(" ".join(query), params)
        return [_row_to_course(row) for row in cur.fetchall()]

    def get(self, course_id: str) -> Course | None:
        """Fetch course by ID."""
        cur = self.conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM courses WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0",
            (course_id,),
        )
        row = cur.fetchone()
        return _row_to_course(row) if row else None

    def create(
        self,
        title: str,
        platform: str = "Coursera",
        instructor: str = "",
        total_modules: int = 10,
        completed_modules: int = 0,
        url: str = "",
        notes: str = "",
    ) -> Course:
        """Insert a new course."""
        now = _now_utc()
        course_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO courses (
                    id, title, platform, instructor, total_modules,
                    completed_modules, url, notes, created_at, updated_at,
                    is_deleted
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)
                """,
                (
                    course_id,
                    title,
                    platform,
                    instructor,
                    total_modules,
                    completed_modules,
                    url,
                    notes,
                    now,
                    now,
                ),
            )
        return Course(
            id=course_id,
            title=title,
            platform=platform,
            instructor=instructor,
            total_modules=total_modules,
            completed_modules=completed_modules,
            url=url,
            notes=notes,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )

    def increment_module(self, course_id: str) -> bool:
        """Increment completed_modules count up to total_modules."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE courses
                SET completed_modules = MIN(
                    total_modules, completed_modules + 1
                ),
                updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (now, course_id),
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0

    def update(
        self,
        course_id: str,
        title: str | None = None,
        platform: str | None = None,
        instructor: str | None = None,
        total_modules: int | None = None,
        completed_modules: int | None = None,
        url: str | None = None,
        notes: str | None = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Update course fields."""
        fields: list[str] = []
        params: list[Any] = []
        if title is not None:
            fields.append("title = ?")
            params.append(title)
        if platform is not None:
            fields.append("platform = ?")
            params.append(platform)
        if instructor is not None:
            fields.append("instructor = ?")
            params.append(instructor)
        if total_modules is not None:
            fields.append("total_modules = ?")
            params.append(total_modules)
        if completed_modules is not None:
            fields.append("completed_modules = ?")
            params.append(completed_modules)
        if url is not None:
            fields.append("url = ?")
            params.append(url)
        if notes is not None:
            fields.append("notes = ?")
            params.append(notes)
        if not fields:
            return False
        fields.append("updated_at = ?")
        params.extend([_now_utc(), course_id])
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                f"UPDATE courses SET {', '.join(fields)} "
                "WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0",
                params,
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0

    def soft_delete(self, course_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft delete a course."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE courses
                SET is_deleted = 1, updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (now, course_id),
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0

def _row_to_idea_doubt(row: sqlite3.Row) -> IdeaDoubt:

        id=row["id"],

        description=row["description"],
        tech_stack_or_ref=row["tech_stack_or_ref"],

        created_at=row["created_at"],
        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
        synced_at=row["synced_at"],
        is_deleted=row["is_deleted"],
    )


class IdeaDoubtRepository:
    """Data access operations for IdeaDoubt entities."""

    def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        self.conn = conn

    def list_items(
        self,
        kind: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
        search_query: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[IdeaDoubt]:
        """List active ideas and doubts."""
        query = ["SELECT * FROM ideas_doubts WHERE is_deleted = 0"]
        params: list[Any] = []

        if kind and kind != "All":
            query.append("AND kind = ?")
            params.append(kind)

        if status and status != "All":
            query.append("AND status = ?")
            params.append(status)

        if search_query:
            query.append(
                "AND (title LIKE ? OR description LIKE ? OR "
                "tech_stack_or_ref LIKE ?)"
            )
            wildcard = f"%{search_query.strip()}%"
            params.extend([wildcard, wildcard, wildcard])

        query.append("ORDER BY created_at DESC")
        cur = self.conn.execute(" ".join(query), params)
        return [_row_to_idea_doubt(row) for row in cur.fetchall()]

    def get(self, item_id: str) -> IdeaDoubt | None:
        """Fetch item by ID."""
        cur = self.conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM ideas_doubts WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0",
            (item_id,),
        )
        row = cur.fetchone()
        return _row_to_idea_doubt(row) if row else None

    def create(
        self,
        kind: str,
        title: str,
        description: str = "",
        tech_stack_or_ref: str = "",
        status: str = "Idea",
    ) -> IdeaDoubt:
        """Create a new idea or doubt."""
        now = _now_utc()
        item_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO ideas_doubts (
                    id, kind, title, description, tech_stack_or_ref,
                    status, created_at, updated_at, is_deleted
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)
                """,
                (
                    item_id,
                    kind,
                    title,
                    description,
                    tech_stack_or_ref,
                    status,
                    now,
                    now,
                ),
            )
        return IdeaDoubt(
            id=item_id,
            kind=kind,
            title=title,
            description=description,
            tech_stack_or_ref=tech_stack_or_ref,
            status=status,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )

    def update_status(self, item_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
        """Update status of an idea or doubt."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE ideas_doubts
                SET status = ?, updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (new_status, now, item_id),
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0

    def update(
        self,
        item_id: str,
        title: str | None = None,
        kind: str | None = None,
        description: str | None = None,
        tech_stack_or_ref: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Update idea/doubt fields."""

        params: list[Any] = []
        if title is not None:
            fields.append("title = ?")
            params.append(title)
        if kind is not None:
            fields.append("kind = ?")
            params.append(kind)
        if description is not None:
            fields.append("description = ?")
            params.append(description)
        if tech_stack_or_ref is not None:
            fields.append("tech_stack_or_ref = ?")
            params.append(tech_stack_or_ref)
        if status is not None:
            fields.append("status = ?")
            params.append(status)
        if not fields:
            return False
        fields.append("updated_at = ?")
        params.extend([_now_utc(), item_id])
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                f"UPDATE ideas_doubts SET {', '.join(fields)} "
                "WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0",
                params,
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0

    def soft_delete(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft delete an idea or doubt."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE ideas_doubts
                SET is_deleted = 1, updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (now, item_id),
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
import uuid
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.database.connection import transaction
