from plus_ultra.repositories.base import _now_utc


def _row_to_opportunity(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Opportunity:
    """Convert an SQLite row to an Opportunity model."""
    return Opportunity(
        id=row["id"],
        title=row["title"],
        organization=row["organization"],
        kind=row["kind"],
        url=row["url"],
        deadline=row["deadline"],
        status=row["status"],
        notes=row["notes"],
        created_at=row["created_at"],
        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
        synced_at=row["synced_at"],
        is_deleted=row["is_deleted"],
    )


class OpportunityRepository:
    """Data access operations for Opportunity entities."""

    def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        self.conn = conn

    def list_opportunities(
        self,
        kind: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
        search_query: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[Opportunity]:
        """List active opportunities with filters."""
        query = ["SELECT * FROM opportunities WHERE is_deleted = 0"]
        params: list[Any] = []

        if kind and kind != "All":
            query.append("AND kind = ?")
            params.append(kind)

        if status and status != "All":
            query.append("AND status = ?")
            params.append(status)

        if search_query:
            query.append(
                "AND (title LIKE ? OR organization LIKE ? OR notes LIKE ?)"
            )
            wildcard = f"%{search_query.strip()}%"
            params.extend([wildcard, wildcard, wildcard])

        query.append("ORDER BY created_at DESC")
        cur = self.conn.execute(" ".join(query), params)
        return [_row_to_opportunity(row) for row in cur.fetchall()]

    def get(self, opp_id: str) -> Opportunity | None:
        """Fetch opportunity by ID."""
        cur = self.conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM opportunities WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0",
            (opp_id,),
        )
        row = cur.fetchone()
        return _row_to_opportunity(row) if row else None

    def create(
        self,
        title: str,
        organization: str,
        kind: str = "Internship",
        url: str = "",
        deadline: str = "",
        status: str = "Saved",
        notes: str = "",
    ) -> Opportunity:
        """Create a new opportunity."""
        now = _now_utc()
        opp_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO opportunities (
                    id, title, organization, kind, url, deadline,
                    status, notes, created_at, updated_at, is_deleted
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)
                """,
                (
                    opp_id,
                    title,
                    organization,
                    kind,
                    url,
                    deadline,
                    status,
                    notes,
                    now,
                    now,
                ),
            )
        return Opportunity(
            id=opp_id,
            title=title,
            organization=organization,
            kind=kind,
            url=url,
            deadline=deadline,
            status=status,
            notes=notes,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )

    def update_status(self, opp_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
        """Update opportunity status."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE opportunities
                SET status = ?, updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (new_status, now, opp_id),
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0

    def update(
        self,
        opp_id: str,
        title: str | None = None,
        organization: str | None = None,
        kind: str | None = None,
        url: str | None = None,
        deadline: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
        notes: str | None = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Update opportunity fields."""
        fields: list[str] = []
        params: list[Any] = []
        if title is not None:
            fields.append("title = ?")
            params.append(title)
        if organization is not None:
            fields.append("organization = ?")
            params.append(organization)
        if kind is not None:
            fields.append("kind = ?")
            params.append(kind)
        if url is not None:
            fields.append("url = ?")
            params.append(url)
        if deadline is not None:
            fields.append("deadline = ?")
            params.append(deadline)
        if status is not None:
            fields.append("status = ?")
            params.append(status)
        if notes is not None:
            fields.append("notes = ?")
            params.append(notes)
        if not fields:
            return False
        fields.append("updated_at = ?")
        params.extend([_now_utc(), opp_id])
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                f"UPDATE opportunities SET {', '.join(fields)} "
                "WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0",
                params,
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0

    def soft_delete(self, opp_id: str) -> bool:

                UPDATE opportunities

========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/repositories/quick_link_repository.py ==========
"""Data access operations for QuickLink entities."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
import uuid
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.database.connection import transaction
from plus_ultra.models import QuickLink
from plus_ultra.repositories.base import _now_utc


def _row_to_quick_link(row: sqlite3.Row) -> QuickLink:
    """Convert an SQLite row to a QuickLink model."""
    return QuickLink(
        id=row["id"],
        name=row["name"],
        target_uri=row["target_uri"],
        category=row["category"],
        click_count=row["click_count"],
        created_at=row["created_at"],
        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
        synced_at=row["synced_at"],
        is_deleted=row["is_deleted"],
    )


class QuickLinkRepository:
    """Data access operations for QuickLink entities."""

    def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        self.conn = conn

    def list_links(
        self,
        category: str | None = None,
        search_query: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[QuickLink]:
        """List active quick links."""
        query = ["SELECT * FROM quick_links WHERE is_deleted = 0"]
        params: list[Any] = []

        if category and category != "All":
            query.append("AND category = ?")
            params.append(category)

        if search_query:
            query.append("AND (name LIKE ? OR target_uri LIKE ?)")
            wildcard = f"%{search_query.strip()}%"
            params.extend([wildcard, wildcard])

        query.append("ORDER BY click_count DESC, created_at DESC")
        cur = self.conn.execute(" ".join(query), params)
        return [_row_to_quick_link(row) for row in cur.fetchall()]

    def get(self, link_id: str) -> QuickLink | None:
        """Fetch quick link by ID."""
        cur = self.conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM quick_links WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0",
            (link_id,),
        )
        row = cur.fetchone()
        return _row_to_quick_link(row) if row else None

    def create(
        self,
        name: str,
        target_uri: str,
        category: str = "Web",
    ) -> QuickLink:
        """Create a new quick link."""
        now = _now_utc()
        link_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO quick_links (
                    id, name, target_uri, category, click_count,
                    created_at, updated_at, is_deleted
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?, 0)
                """,
                (link_id, name, target_uri, category, now, now),
            )
        return QuickLink(
            id=link_id,
            name=name,
            target_uri=target_uri,
            category=category,
            click_count=0,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )

    def increment_click(self, link_id: str) -> bool:
        """Increment click count."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE quick_links
                SET click_count = click_count + 1, updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (now, link_id),
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0

    def update(
        self,
        link_id: str,
        name: str | None = None,
        target_uri: str | None = None,
        category: str | None = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Update quick link fields."""
        fields: list[str] = []
        params: list[Any] = []
        if name is not None:
            fields.append("name = ?")
            params.append(name)
        if target_uri is not None:
            fields.append("target_uri = ?")
            params.append(target_uri)
        if category is not None:
            fields.append("category = ?")
            params.append(category)
        if not fields:
            return False
        fields.append("updated_at = ?")
        params.extend([_now_utc(), link_id])
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                f"UPDATE quick_links SET {', '.join(fields)} "
                "WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0",
                params,
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0

    def soft_delete(self, link_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft delete a quick link."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE quick_links
                SET is_deleted = 1, updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (now, link_id),
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/repositories/reminder_repository.py ==========
"""Data access operations for Reminder alerts."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
import uuid

from plus_ultra.database.connection import transaction
from plus_ultra.models import Reminder
from plus_ultra.repositories.base import _now_utc


def _row_to_reminder(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Reminder:
    """Convert an SQLite row to a Reminder model."""
    return Reminder(
        id=row["id"],
        task_id=row["task_id"],
        remind_at=row["remind_at"],
        created_at=row["created_at"],
        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
        synced_at=row["synced_at"],
        is_deleted=row["is_deleted"],
    )


class ReminderRepository:
    """Data access operations for Reminder alerts."""

    def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        self.conn = conn

    def create(self, task_id: str, remind_at: str) -> Reminder:
        """Create and schedule a reminder for an existing task."""
        rem_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO reminders (
                    id, task_id, remind_at, created_at, updated_at, is_deleted
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)
                """,
                (rem_id, task_id, remind_at, now, now),
            )
        return Reminder(
            id=rem_id,
            task_id=task_id,
            remind_at=remind_at,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )

    def get_by_id(self, reminder_id: str) -> Reminder | None:
        """Fetch reminder by ID."""
        cur = self.conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM reminders WHERE id = ?", (reminder_id,)
        )
        row = cur.fetchone()
        return _row_to_reminder(row) if row else None

    def list_due(self, before_timestamp: str) -> list[Reminder]:
        """Query active reminders due at or before a given timestamp.

        Enforces:
        - reminder is_deleted = 0.
        - linked task is_deleted = 0 (excludes reminders of deleted tasks).
        - reminder has not already fired (not in reminder_fires table).
        """
        cur = self.conn.execute(
            """
            SELECT r.* FROM reminders r
            JOIN tasks t ON r.task_id = t.id
            WHERE r.is_deleted = 0
              AND t.is_deleted = 0
              AND r.remind_at <= ?
              AND NOT EXISTS (
                  SELECT 1 FROM reminder_fires rf WHERE rf.reminder_id = r.id
              )
            ORDER BY r.remind_at ASC
            """,
            (before_timestamp,),
        )
        return [_row_to_reminder(row) for row in cur.fetchall()]

    def mark_fired(self, reminder_id: str) -> None:
        """Record that a reminder alert has been dispatched on this device."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT OR IGNORE INTO reminder_fires (reminder_id, fired_at)
                VALUES (?, ?)
                """,
                (reminder_id, now),
            )

    def is_fired(self, reminder_id: str) -> bool:
        """Check if reminder was already dispatched on this device."""
        cur = self.conn.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM reminder_fires WHERE reminder_id = ?",
            (reminder_id,),
        )
        return cur.fetchone() is not None

    def soft_delete(self, reminder_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft-delete an individual reminder."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE reminders
                SET is_deleted = 1, updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (now, reminder_id),
            )
            return cur.rowcount > 0


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/repositories/task_repository.py ==========
"""Data access operations for Task entities."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
import uuid
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.database.connection import transaction
from plus_ultra.models import Task
from plus_ultra.repositories.base import _now_utc


def _row_to_task(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Task:
    """Convert an SQLite row to a Task model."""
    return Task(
        id=row["id"],
        category_id=row["category_id"],
        title=row["title"],
        description=row["description"],
        due_date=row["due_date"],
        status=row["status"],
        task_label=row["task_label"],
        critical_level=row["critical_level"],
        pace_level=row["pace_level"],
        is_google_task=bool(row["is_google_task"]),
        created_at=row["created_at"],
        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
        synced_at=row["synced_at"],
        is_deleted=row["is_deleted"],
    )


class TaskRepository:
    """Data access operations for Task entities."""

    def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        self.conn = conn

    def create(
        self,
        title: str,
        category_id: str | None = None,
        description: str = "",
        due_date: str | None = None,
        status: str = "Pending",
        task_label: str = "College",
        critical_level: str = "Medium",
        pace_level: str = "Short-term",
        is_google_task: bool = False,
    ) -> Task:
        """Create and persist a new task record."""
        clean_title = title.strip()
        task_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        now = _now_utc()
        google_flag = 1 if is_google_task else 0

        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO tasks (
                    id, category_id, title, description, due_date, status,
                    task_label, critical_level, pace_level, is_google_task,
                    created_at, updated_at, is_deleted
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)
                """,
                (
                    task_id,
                    category_id,
                    clean_title,
                    description,
                    due_date,
                    status,
                    task_label,
                    critical_level,
                    pace_level,
                    google_flag,
                    now,
                    now,
                ),
            )

        return Task(
            id=task_id,
            category_id=category_id,
            title=clean_title,
            description=description,
            due_date=due_date,
            status=status,
            task_label=task_label,
            critical_level=critical_level,
            pace_level=pace_level,
            is_google_task=is_google_task,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )

    def get_by_id(self, task_id: str) -> Task | None:
        """Fetch task by ID."""
        cur = self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE id = ?", (task_id,))
        row = cur.fetchone()
        return _row_to_task(row) if row else None

    def list_active(
        self,
        category_id: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[Task]:
        """List active non-deleted tasks with optional filters."""
        query = "SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE is_deleted = 0"
        params: list[Any] = []

        if category_id is not None:
            query += " AND category_id = ?"
            params.append(category_id)
        if status is not None:
            query += " AND status = ?"
            params.append(status)

        query += " ORDER BY created_at DESC"
        cur = self.conn.execute(query, params)
        return [_row_to_task(row) for row in cur.fetchall()]

    def update(
        self,
        task_id: str,
        title: str | None = None,
        category_id: str | None = None,
        description: str | None = None,
        due_date: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
        task_label: str | None = None,
        critical_level: str | None = None,
        pace_level: str | None = None,
        is_google_task: bool | None = None,
    ) -> Task | None:
        """Update task fields; automatically bumps updated_at timestamp."""
        existing = self.get_by_id(task_id)
        if not existing or existing.is_deleted:
            return None

        now = _now_utc()
        new_title = title.strip() if title is not None else existing.title
        new_category_id = (
            category_id if category_id is not None else existing.category_id
        )
        new_description = (
            description if description is not None else existing.description
        )
        new_due_date = due_date if due_date is not None else existing.due_date
        new_status = status if status is not None else existing.status
        new_label = (
            task_label if task_label is not None else existing.task_label
        )
        new_critical = (
            critical_level
            if critical_level is not None
            else existing.critical_level
        )
        new_pace = pace_level if pace_level is not None else existing.pace_level
        new_google = (
            is_google_task
            if is_google_task is not None
            else existing.is_google_task
        )

        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE tasks SET
                    title = ?, category_id = ?, description = ?,
                    due_date = ?, status = ?, task_label = ?,
                    critical_level = ?, pace_level = ?, is_google_task = ?,
                    updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (
                    new_title,
                    new_category_id,
                    new_description,
                    new_due_date,
                    new_status,
                    new_label,
                    new_critical,
                    new_pace,
                    1 if new_google else 0,
                    now,
                    task_id,
                ),
            )
        return self.get_by_id(task_id)

    def soft_delete(self, task_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft-delete task and cascade soft-delete to its reminders."""
        now = _now_utc()
        with transaction(self.conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE tasks
                SET is_deleted = 1, updated_at = ?
                WHERE id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (now, task_id),
            )
            if cur.rowcount == 0:
                return False

            # Cascade: soft-delete task's reminders in the same transaction
            cur.execute(
                """
                UPDATE reminders
                SET is_deleted = 1, updated_at = ?
                WHERE task_id = ? AND is_deleted = 0
                """,
                (now, task_id),
            )
        return True

========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/scheduler.py ==========
"""Background reminder evaluation scheduler for PLUS-ULTRA.

Applies OS concepts:
- Thread Isolation & Concurrency: Periodic reminder evaluation executes on a
  dedicated daemon thread without blocking the Tkinter UI event loop.
  The worker thread creates its own thread-isolated SQLite connection in WAL
  mode, preventing SQLite check_same_thread concurrency errors.
- Producer-Consumer Flow: The scheduler queries SQLite for due reminders,
  dispatches desktop notifications via subprocess, and enqueues in-app alerts
  to the main thread via thread-safe callbacks.
- Clean Lifecycle & Shutdown: Controlled cancellation using threading.Event.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import sqlite3
import threading
from collections.abc import Callable
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

from plus_ultra import desktop, settings
from plus_ultra.database import get_connection
from plus_ultra.models import Reminder, Task
from plus_ultra.repositories import ReminderRepository, TaskRepository
from plus_ultra.services import ReminderService

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL_S: float = 10.0  # 10,000 ms


class ReminderScheduler:
    """Background polling worker for evaluating due reminders."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        reminder_service: ReminderService,
        poll_interval_s: float = DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL_S,
        on_reminder_due: Callable[[Task, Reminder], None] | None = None,
        desktop_notify: bool = True,
        now_provider: Callable[[], str] | None = None,
        db_path: Path | None = None,
        conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.reminder_service = reminder_service
        self.poll_interval_s = poll_interval_s
        self.on_reminder_due = on_reminder_due
        self.desktop_notify = desktop_notify
        self.now_provider = now_provider
        self.db_path = db_path
        self.conn_factory = conn_factory

        self._stop_event = threading.Event()
        self._thread: threading.Thread | None = None

    @property
    def is_running(self) -> bool:
        """Return True if background worker thread is active."""
        return self._thread is not None and self._thread.is_alive()

    def tick(
        self,
        now_iso: str | None = None,
        service: ReminderService | None = None,
    ) -> list[tuple[Reminder, Task]]:
        """Evaluate and dispatch reminders due at or before current time.

        Returns list of (Reminder, Task) pairs that were successfully fired.
        """
        # Respect user settings flag
        eff_settings = settings.load_effective_settings()
        if not eff_settings.get("reminders_enabled", True):
            logger.debug("Reminders disabled in settings; tick skipped.")
            return []

        active_service = service or self.reminder_service

        if now_iso is not None:
            now = now_iso
        elif self.now_provider is not None:
            now = self.now_provider()
        else:
            now = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()

        due_items = active_service.get_due_reminders(now)
        fired: list[tuple[Reminder, Task]] = []

        for reminder, task in due_items:
            logger.info(
                "Firing reminder %s for task '%s' (due %s)",
                reminder.id,
                task.title,
                reminder.remind_at,
            )

            # 1. Native OS desktop notification via notify-send
            if self.desktop_notify:
                urgency = (
                    "critical" if task.critical_level == "High" else "normal"
                )
                body = (
                    f"Priority: {task.critical_level} | "
                    f"{task.description or 'Due now'}"
                )
                desktop.send_desktop_notification(
                    title=f"Reminder: {task.title}",
                    message=body,
                    urgency=urgency,
                    timeout_ms=5000,
                )

            # 2. In-app notification callback to UI thread
            if self.on_reminder_due is not None:
                try:
                    self.on_reminder_due(task, reminder)
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.warning("Error in on_reminder_due callback: %s", exc)

            # 3. Mark fired in SQLite to prevent re-firing
            active_service.mark_fired(reminder.id)
            fired.append((reminder, task))

        return fired

    def _run_loop(self) -> None:
        """Internal background thread worker loop with isolated DB session."""
        logger.info(
            "Reminder scheduler started (polling every %.1fs)",
            self.poll_interval_s,
        )

        # Open thread-isolated connection if factory/path available
        thread_conn: sqlite3.Connection | None = None
        thread_service: ReminderService = self.reminder_service

        try:
            if self.conn_factory is not None:
                thread_conn = self.conn_factory()
            else:
                thread_conn = get_connection(self.db_path)

            rem_repo = ReminderRepository(thread_conn)
            task_repo = TaskRepository(thread_conn)
            thread_service = ReminderService(rem_repo, task_repo)
        except Exception as err:
            logger.debug(
                "Thread connection fallback to shared service: %s", err
            )

        try:
            while not self._stop_event.is_set():
                try:
                    self.tick(service=thread_service)
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.error(
                        "Error during reminder scheduler tick: %s", exc
                    )

                # Sleep responsiveness: wakes immediately on stop_event set
                self._stop_event.wait(self.poll_interval_s)
        finally:
            if thread_conn is not None:
                thread_conn.close()

        logger.info("Reminder scheduler worker thread stopped.")

    def start(self) -> None:
        """Start the background daemon thread if not already running."""
        if self.is_running:
            logger.warning("Scheduler already running.")
            return

        self._stop_event.clear()
        self._thread = threading.Thread(
            target=self._run_loop,
            name="ReminderSchedulerThread",
            daemon=True,
        )
        self._thread.start()

    def stop(self, timeout: float = 2.0) -> None:
        """Signal worker thread to stop and wait for termination."""
        if not self.is_running:
            return

        logger.info("Stopping reminder scheduler...")
        self._stop_event.set()
        if self._thread is not None:
            self._thread.join(timeout=timeout)
            self._thread = None
        logger.info("Reminder scheduler cleanly stopped.")


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/services/category_service.py ==========
"""Business operations for category management."""

from __future__ import annotations

from plus_ultra.models import Category
from plus_ultra.repositories import CategoryRepository


class CategoryService:
    """Business operations for category management."""

    def __init__(self, category_repo: CategoryRepository) -> None:
        self.category_repo = category_repo

    def create_category(self, name: str, color: str = "#4a5568") -> Category:
        """Create a new category."""
        clean_name = name.strip()
        if not clean_name:
            raise ValueError("Category name cannot be empty.")
        return self.category_repo.create(name=clean_name, color=color)

    def get_category(self, category_id: str) -> Category | None:
        """Fetch category by ID if active."""
        cat = self.category_repo.get_by_id(category_id)
        return cat if cat and not cat.is_deleted else None

    def update_category(
        self, category_id: str, name: str, color: str
    ) -> Category | None:
        """Update category name and color."""
        clean_name = name.strip()
        if not clean_name:
            raise ValueError("Category name cannot be empty.")
        return self.category_repo.update(
            category_id=category_id, name=clean_name, color=color
        )

    def soft_delete_category(self, category_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft-delete category and nullify linked tasks' category_id."""
        return self.category_repo.soft_delete(category_id)

    def list_active(self) -> list[Category]:
        """List all active categories ordered alphabetically."""
        return self.category_repo.list_active()

    def list_categories(self) -> list[Category]:
        """Alias for list_active."""
        return self.list_active()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/services/code_service.py ==========
"""Business operations for Code snippets and DSA tracking."""

from __future__ import annotations

from plus_ultra.models import CodeItem
from plus_ultra.repositories import CodeRepository


class CodeService:
    """Business operations for Code snippets and DSA tracking."""

    def __init__(self, code_repo: CodeRepository) -> None:
        self.code_repo = code_repo

    def list_items(
        self,
        category: str | None = None,
        search_query: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[CodeItem]:
        """List active code items."""
        return self.code_repo.list_items(category, search_query)

    def get_item(self, item_id: str) -> CodeItem | None:
        """Get code item by ID."""
        return self.code_repo.get(item_id)

    def create_item(
        self,
        category: str,
        title: str,
        content: str = "",
        language: str = "python",
        difficulty: str = "Easy",
        is_done: int = 0,
        url: str = "",
        notes: str = "",
    ) -> CodeItem:
        """Validate and create code item."""
        clean_title = title.strip()
        if not clean_title:
            raise ValueError("Title cannot be empty.")
        return self.code_repo.create(
            category=category,
            title=clean_title,
            content=content,
            language=language,
            difficulty=difficulty,
            is_done=is_done,
            url=url,
            notes=notes,
        )

    def toggle_done(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Toggle DSA problem solved status."""
        return self.code_repo.toggle_done(item_id)

    def update_code_item(
        self,
        item_id: str,
        category: str | None = None,
        title: str | None = None,
        content: str | None = None,
        language: str | None = None,
        difficulty: str | None = None,
        url: str | None = None,
        notes: str | None = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Update code item attributes."""
        clean_title = title.strip() if title is not None else None
        if clean_title is not None and not clean_title:
            raise ValueError("Title cannot be empty.")
        return self.code_repo.update(
            item_id=item_id,
            category=category,
            title=clean_title,
            content=content,
            language=language,
            difficulty=difficulty,
            url=url,
            notes=notes,
        )

    def soft_delete_item(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft delete code item."""
        return self.code_repo.soft_delete(item_id)


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/services/course_service.py ==========
"""Business operations for Course and certification tracking."""

from __future__ import annotations

from plus_ultra.models import Course
from plus_ultra.repositories import CourseRepository


class CourseService:
    """Business operations for Course and certification tracking."""

    def __init__(self, course_repo: CourseRepository) -> None:
        self.course_repo = course_repo

    def list_courses(
        self,
        platform: str | None = None,
        search_query: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[Course]:
        """List active courses."""
        return self.course_repo.list_courses(platform, search_query)

    def get_course(self, course_id: str) -> Course | None:
        """Get course by ID."""
        return self.course_repo.get(course_id)

    def create_course(
        self,
        title: str,
        platform: str = "Coursera",
        instructor: str = "",
        total_modules: int = 10,
        completed_modules: int = 0,
        url: str = "",
        notes: str = "",
    ) -> Course:
        """Validate and create course."""
        clean_title = title.strip()
        if not clean_title:
            raise ValueError("Course title cannot be empty.")
        if total_modules < 1:
            raise ValueError("Total modules must be at least 1.")
        return self.course_repo.create(
            title=clean_title,
            platform=platform,
            instructor=instructor,
            total_modules=total_modules,
            completed_modules=max(0, min(completed_modules, total_modules)),
            url=url,
            notes=notes,
        )

    def increment_module(self, course_id: str) -> bool:
        """Increment completed module count."""
        return self.course_repo.increment_module(course_id)

    def update_course(
        self,
        course_id: str,
        title: str | None = None,
        platform: str | None = None,
        instructor: str | None = None,
        total_modules: int | None = None,
        completed_modules: int | None = None,
        url: str | None = None,
        notes: str | None = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Update course attributes."""
        clean_title = title.strip() if title is not None else None
        if clean_title is not None and not clean_title:
            raise ValueError("Course title cannot be empty.")
        if total_modules is not None and total_modules < 1:
            raise ValueError("Total modules must be at least 1.")
        return self.course_repo.update(
            course_id=course_id,
            title=clean_title,
            platform=platform,
            instructor=instructor,
            total_modules=total_modules,
            completed_modules=completed_modules,
            url=url,
            notes=notes,
        )

    def soft_delete_course(self, course_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft delete course."""
        return self.course_repo.soft_delete(course_id)


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/services/idea_doubt_service.py ==========
"""Business operations for Ideas and Doubts tracking."""

from __future__ import annotations

from plus_ultra.models import IdeaDoubt
from plus_ultra.repositories import IdeaDoubtRepository


class IdeaDoubtService:
    """Business operations for Ideas and Doubts tracking."""

    IDEA_STATUSES = ("Idea", "Planned", "In Progress", "Completed")
    DOUBT_STATUSES = ("Open", "Resolved")

    def __init__(self, idea_doubt_repo: IdeaDoubtRepository) -> None:
        self.idea_doubt_repo = idea_doubt_repo

    def list_items(
        self,
        kind: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
        search_query: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[IdeaDoubt]:
        """List active ideas and doubts."""
        return self.idea_doubt_repo.list_items(kind, status, search_query)

    def get_item(self, item_id: str) -> IdeaDoubt | None:
        """Get item by ID."""
        return self.idea_doubt_repo.get(item_id)

    def create_item(
        self,
        kind: str,
        title: str,
        description: str = "",
        tech_stack_or_ref: str = "",
        status: str = "Idea",
    ) -> IdeaDoubt:
        """Validate and create idea or doubt."""
        clean_title = title.strip()
        if not clean_title:
            raise ValueError("Title cannot be empty.")
        return self.idea_doubt_repo.create(
            kind=kind,
            title=clean_title,
            description=description,
            tech_stack_or_ref=tech_stack_or_ref,
            status=status,
        )

    def advance_status(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Advance status to next stage or toggle open/resolved."""
        item = self.idea_doubt_repo.get(item_id)
        if not item:
            return False

        if item.kind == "Idea":
            try:
                curr_idx = self.IDEA_STATUSES.index(item.status)
                next_idx = (curr_idx + 1) % len(self.IDEA_STATUSES)
                new_status = self.IDEA_STATUSES[next_idx]
            except ValueError:
                new_status = "In Progress"
        else:
            new_status = "Resolved" if item.status == "Open" else "Open"

        return self.idea_doubt_repo.update_status(item_id, new_status)

    def update_idea_doubt(
        self,
        item_id: str,
        title: str | None = None,
        kind: str | None = None,
        description: str | None = None,
        tech_stack_or_ref: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Update idea or doubt details."""
        clean_title = title.strip() if title is not None else None
        if clean_title is not None and not clean_title:
            raise ValueError("Title cannot be empty.")
        return self.idea_doubt_repo.update(
            item_id=item_id,
            title=clean_title,
            kind=kind,
            description=description,
            tech_stack_or_ref=tech_stack_or_ref,
            status=status,
        )

    def soft_delete_item(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft delete item."""
        return self.idea_doubt_repo.soft_delete(item_id)

"""Business operations for career and academic opportunities."""

from __future__ import annotations

from plus_ultra.models import Opportunity
from plus_ultra.repositories import OpportunityRepository


class OpportunityService:
    """Business operations for career and academic opportunities."""

    STATUSES = ("Saved", "Applied", "In Progress", "Accepted", "Rejected")

    def __init__(self, opp_repo: OpportunityRepository) -> None:
        self.opp_repo = opp_repo

    def list_opportunities(
        self,
        kind: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
        search_query: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[Opportunity]:
        """List active opportunities."""
        return self.opp_repo.list_opportunities(kind, status, search_query)

    def get_opportunity(self, opp_id: str) -> Opportunity | None:
        """Get opportunity by ID."""
        return self.opp_repo.get(opp_id)

    def create_opportunity(
        self,
        title: str,
        organization: str,
        kind: str = "Internship",
        url: str = "",
        deadline: str = "",
        status: str = "Saved",
        notes: str = "",
    ) -> Opportunity:
        """Validate and create opportunity."""
        clean_title = title.strip()
        clean_org = organization.strip()
        if not clean_title:
            raise ValueError("Title cannot be empty.")
        if not clean_org:
            raise ValueError("Organization cannot be empty.")
        return self.opp_repo.create(
            title=clean_title,
            organization=clean_org,
            kind=kind,
            url=url,
            deadline=deadline,
            status=status,
            notes=notes,
        )

    def advance_status(self, opp_id: str) -> bool:
        """Advance opportunity status through standard lifecycle."""
        item = self.opp_repo.get(opp_id)
        if not item:
            return False

        try:
            curr_idx = self.STATUSES.index(item.status)
            next_idx = (curr_idx + 1) % len(self.STATUSES)
            new_status = self.STATUSES[next_idx]
        except ValueError:
            new_status = "Applied"

        return self.opp_repo.update_status(opp_id, new_status)

    def update_opportunity(
        self,
        opp_id: str,
        title: str | None = None,
        organization: str | None = None,
        kind: str | None = None,
        url: str | None = None,
        deadline: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
        notes: str | None = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Update opportunity details."""
        clean_title = title.strip() if title is not None else None
        clean_org = organization.strip() if organization is not None else None
        if clean_title is not None and not clean_title:
            raise ValueError("Title cannot be empty.")
        if clean_org is not None and not clean_org:
            raise ValueError("Organization cannot be empty.")
        return self.opp_repo.update(
            opp_id=opp_id,
            title=clean_title,
            organization=clean_org,
            kind=kind,
            url=url,
            deadline=deadline,
            status=status,
            notes=notes,
        )

    def soft_delete_opportunity(self, opp_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft delete opportunity."""
        return self.opp_repo.soft_delete(opp_id)

========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/services/reminder_service.py ==========
"""Business operations for reminder scheduling and due evaluation."""

from __future__ import annotations

from plus_ultra.models import Reminder, Task
from plus_ultra.repositories import ReminderRepository, TaskRepository


class ReminderService:
    """Business operations for reminder scheduling and due evaluation."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        reminder_repo: ReminderRepository,
        task_repo: TaskRepository,
    ) -> None:
        self.reminder_repo = reminder_repo
        self.task_repo = task_repo

    def create_reminder(self, task_id: str, remind_at: str) -> Reminder:
        """Create and schedule a reminder for an active task."""
        clean_task_id = task_id.strip()
        task = self.task_repo.get_by_id(clean_task_id)
        if not task or task.is_deleted:
            raise ValueError(f"Task '{task_id}' does not exist or is deleted.")

        clean_remind_at = remind_at.strip()
        if not clean_remind_at:
            raise ValueError("remind_at timestamp cannot be empty.")

        return self.reminder_repo.create(clean_task_id, clean_remind_at)

    def get_due_reminders(
        self, before_timestamp: str
    ) -> list[tuple[Reminder, Task]]:
        """Query active due reminders and pair with active tasks.

        Excludes reminders where the linked task is deleted or the reminder
        has already been dispatched in reminder_fires.
        """
        reminders = self.reminder_repo.list_due(before_timestamp)
        result: list[tuple[Reminder, Task]] = []
        for rem in reminders:
            task = self.task_repo.get_by_id(rem.task_id)
            if task and not task.is_deleted:
                result.append((rem, task))
        return result

    def mark_fired(self, reminder_id: str) -> None:
        """Record reminder dispatch in reminder_fires table."""
        self.reminder_repo.mark_fired(reminder_id)

    def is_fired(self, reminder_id: str) -> bool:
        """Check if reminder was already dispatched on this device."""
        return self.reminder_repo.is_fired(reminder_id)

    def soft_delete_reminder(self, reminder_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft delete a reminder."""
        return self.reminder_repo.soft_delete(reminder_id)


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/services/task_service.py ==========
"""Business operations for task management."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging

from plus_ultra.models import Task
from plus_ultra.repositories import CategoryRepository, TaskRepository

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

VALID_STATUSES = ("Pending", "In Progress", "Completed", "Overdue")
VALID_CRITICAL_LEVELS = ("High", "Medium", "Low")
VALID_PACE_LEVELS = ("Long-term", "Short-term")


class TaskService:
    """Business operations for task management."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        task_repo: TaskRepository,
        category_repo: CategoryRepository,
    ) -> None:
        self.task_repo = task_repo
        self.category_repo = category_repo

    def create_task(
        self,
        title: str,
        category_id: str | None = None,
        description: str = "",
        due_date: str | None = None,
        status: str = "Pending",
        task_label: str = "College",
        critical_level: str = "Medium",
        pace_level: str = "Short-term",
        is_google_task: bool = False,
    ) -> Task:
        """Validate input and create a new active task."""
        clean_title = title.strip()
        if not clean_title:
            raise ValueError("Task title cannot be empty or whitespace-only.")

        if status not in VALID_STATUSES:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Invalid status: {status}. Expected {VALID_STATUSES}"
            )

        if critical_level not in VALID_CRITICAL_LEVELS:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Invalid critical level: {critical_level}. Expected "
                f"{VALID_CRITICAL_LEVELS}"
            )

        if pace_level not in VALID_PACE_LEVELS:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Invalid pace level: {pace_level}. Expected "
                f"{VALID_PACE_LEVELS}"
            )

        # Verify category exists if provided
        if category_id is not None:
            cat = self.category_repo.get_by_id(category_id)
            if not cat or cat.is_deleted:
                raise ValueError(f"Category '{category_id}' does not exist.")

        return self.task_repo.create(
            title=clean_title,
            category_id=category_id,
            description=description.strip(),
            due_date=due_date,
            status=status,
            task_label=task_label.strip(),
            critical_level=critical_level,
            pace_level=pace_level,
            is_google_task=is_google_task,
        )

    def get_task(self, task_id: str) -> Task | None:
        """Fetch task by ID if not deleted."""
        task = self.task_repo.get_by_id(task_id)
        return task if task and not task.is_deleted else None

    def update_task(
        self,
        task_id: str,
        title: str | None = None,
        category_id: str | None = None,
        description: str | None = None,
        due_date: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
        task_label: str | None = None,
        critical_level: str | None = None,
        pace_level: str | None = None,
        is_google_task: bool | None = None,
    ) -> Task | None:
        """Validate updates and persist changes."""
        clean_title = title.strip() if title is not None else None
        if clean_title is not None and not clean_title:
            raise ValueError("Task title cannot be empty.")

        if status is not None and status not in VALID_STATUSES:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid status: {status}.")

        if (
            critical_level is not None
            and critical_level not in VALID_CRITICAL_LEVELS
        ):
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid critical level: {critical_level}.")

        if pace_level is not None and pace_level not in VALID_PACE_LEVELS:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid pace level: {pace_level}.")

        if category_id is not None:
            cat = self.category_repo.get_by_id(category_id)
            if not cat or cat.is_deleted:
                raise ValueError(f"Category '{category_id}' does not exist.")

        return self.task_repo.update(
            task_id=task_id,
            title=clean_title,
            category_id=category_id,
            description=description.strip() if description else None,
            due_date=due_date,
            status=status,
            task_label=task_label.strip() if task_label else None,
            critical_level=critical_level,
            pace_level=pace_level,
            is_google_task=is_google_task,
        )

    def toggle_status(self, task_id: str) -> Task | None:
        """Toggle task status between Pending and Completed."""
        task = self.get_task(task_id)
        if not task:
            return None

        new_status = "Pending" if task.status == "Completed" else "Completed"
        return self.update_task(task_id, status=new_status)

    def soft_delete_task(self, task_id: str) -> bool:
        """Soft delete a task and cascade to its reminders."""
        return self.task_repo.soft_delete(task_id)

    def list_tasks(
        self,
        category_id: str | None = None,
        status: str | None = None,
        critical_level: str | None = None,
        search_query: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[Task]:
        """List active tasks with filters and search query."""
        tasks = self.task_repo.list_active(
            category_id=category_id, status=status
        )

        if critical_level:
            tasks = [t for t in tasks if t.critical_level == critical_level]

        if search_query:
            query = search_query.strip().lower()
            tasks = [
                t
                for t in tasks
                if query in t.title.lower() or query in t.description.lower()
            ]

        return tasks


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/settings.py ==========
"""Settings management for PLUS-ULTRA.

Applies OS concepts:
- Defense in depth and filesystem security: User settings stored in private
  XDG config directory with mode 0o600 (owner read/write only).
- Crash consistency and atomic file updates: Writing via temporary file in
  the same filesystem directory followed by atomic replace (POSIX rename).
- Fault tolerance and defensive recovery: Corrupted settings files are
  quarantined with a timestamped suffix (.bad-<timestamp>) rather than
  crashing or silently deleting user data.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import importlib.resources
import json
import logging
import os
import threading
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra import config

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_lock = threading.Lock()
_cached_effective: dict[str, Any] | None = None


def load_default_settings() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Load default settings packaged with the application."""
    res = importlib.resources.files("plus_ultra.resources")
    data_ref = res.joinpath("data", "default-settings.json")
    if data_ref.is_file():
        ref = data_ref
    else:
        ref = res.joinpath("default-settings.json")
    with ref.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data: dict[str, Any] = json.load(f)
        return data


def _quarantine_corrupt_file(path: Path) -> Path | None:
    """Quarantine a corrupted settings file by renaming with timestamp."""
    timestamp = datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d%H%M%S")
    bad_path = path.with_name(f"{path.name}.bad-{timestamp}")
    if bad_path.exists():
        timestamp = datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d%H%M%S%f")
        bad_path = path.with_name(f"{path.name}.bad-{timestamp}")

    try:
        path.rename(bad_path)
        logger.warning("Corrupt settings file quarantined to %s", bad_path)
        return bad_path
    except OSError as err:
        logger.error("Failed to quarantine corrupt file %s: %s", path, err)
        return None


def _load_user_overrides() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Read user overrides from disk, recovering safely on error."""
    path = config.get_user_settings_path()
    if not path.is_file():
        return {}

    try:
        content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        data = json.loads(content)
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            raise ValueError("Settings content is not a JSON object")
        return data
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("Failed to read user settings from %s: %s", path, exc)
        _quarantine_corrupt_file(path)
        return {}


def _merge_settings(
    defaults: dict[str, Any], overrides: dict[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Merge user overrides onto defaults with strict type checking."""
    effective = dict(defaults)
    for key, val in overrides.items():
        if key not in defaults:
            logger.warning("Ignoring unknown user setting key: %s", key)
            continue

        default_val = defaults[key]
        if type(val) is not type(default_val):
            logger.warning(
                "Ignoring setting '%s' due to type mismatch: "
                "expected %s, got %s",
                key,
                type(default_val).__name__,
                type(val).__name__,
            )
            continue

        effective[key] = val
    return effective


def _save_user_overrides(overrides: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Write user overrides atomically with mode 0o600."""
    path = config.get_user_settings_path()
    config.get_config_dir()  # Ensures parent exists with 0o700
    parent = path.parent
    temp_path = parent / f".{path.name}.tmp.{os.getpid()}"

    payload = json.dumps(overrides, indent=2) + "\n"
    try:
        fd = os.open(
            temp_path,
            os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC,
            0o600,
        )
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(payload)
            f.flush()
            os.fsync(f.fileno())

        temp_path.replace(path)
        with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
            path.chmod(0o600)
    except Exception:
        with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
            temp_path.unlink()
        raise


def reload() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Force reload settings from disk and return effective settings."""
    global _cached_effective
    with _lock:
        defaults = load_default_settings()
        overrides = _load_user_overrides()
        _cached_effective = _merge_settings(defaults, overrides)
        return dict(_cached_effective)


def load_effective_settings() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return effective settings (defaults merged with user overrides)."""
    global _cached_effective
    with _lock:
        if _cached_effective is None:
            defaults = load_default_settings()
            overrides = _load_user_overrides()
            _cached_effective = _merge_settings(defaults, overrides)
        return dict(_cached_effective)


def update_user_setting(key: str, value: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Update a setting, persist atomically to disk, and return effective."""
    global _cached_effective
    defaults = load_default_settings()
    if key not in defaults:
        raise KeyError(f"Unknown setting key: {key}")

    expected_type = type(defaults[key])
    if type(value) is not expected_type:
        raise TypeError(
            f"Type mismatch for setting '{key}': "
            f"expected {expected_type.__name__}, got {type(value).__name__}"
        )

    with _lock:
        overrides = _load_user_overrides()
        overrides[key] = value
        _save_user_overrides(overrides)
        _cached_effective = _merge_settings(defaults, overrides)
        return dict(_cached_effective)


def reset_setting(key: str) -> None:
    """Reset a single setting back to its default value."""
    global _cached_effective
    defaults = load_default_settings()
    if key not in defaults:
        raise KeyError(f"Unknown setting key: {key}")

    with _lock:
        overrides = _load_user_overrides()
        if key in overrides:
            del overrides[key]
            _save_user_overrides(overrides)
        _cached_effective = _merge_settings(defaults, overrides)


def reset_all_settings() -> None:
    """Reset all settings to default values and remove user overrides."""
    global _cached_effective
    path = config.get_user_settings_path()
    with _lock:
        if path.is_file():
            with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                path.unlink()
        _cached_effective = dict(load_default_settings())


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/components/datetime_picker.py ==========
"""Interactive reusable Date and Time picker component for PLUS-ULTRA."""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import datetime
import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable

from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class DateTimePicker(tk.Frame):
    """Interactive date and time selector with presets and quick actions."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        parent: tk.Widget,
        initial_value: str = "",
        include_time: bool = True,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
        on_change: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent, bg="#12151c")
        self.include_time = include_time
        self.theme = theme
        self.on_change = on_change

        self._build_ui()
        if initial_value:
            self.set_value(initial_value)

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        # 1. Main Display / Entry field with "Now" button
        entry_row = tk.Frame(self, bg="#12151c")
        entry_row.pack(fill="x")

        self.entry = tk.Entry(
            entry_row,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            insertbackground="#00e5ff",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.entry.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, ipady=4)

        now_btn = tk.Button(
            entry_row,
            text="⚡ Now",
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 9, "bold"),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=8,
            pady=3,
            command=self.set_now,
        )
        now_btn.pack(side="left", padx=(6, 0))

        # 2. Date Quick Presets: [Today] [Tomorrow] [+3 Days] [+1 Wk]
        preset_row = tk.Frame(self, bg="#12151c")
        preset_row.pack(fill="x", pady=(6, 0))

        date_presets = [
            ("Today", 0),
            ("Tomorrow", 1),
            ("+3 Days", 3),
            ("+1 Wk", 7),
        ]
        for label, days_offset in date_presets:
            tk.Button(
                preset_row,
                text=label,
                bg="#1c212c",
                fg="#94a3b8",
                activebackground="#2b3448",
                activeforeground="#00e5ff",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 8),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=6,
                pady=2,
                command=lambda d=days_offset: self.add_days_from_today(d),
            ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 4))

        # Stepper buttons: [-1d] [+1d]
        tk.Button(
            preset_row,
            text="-1d",
            bg="#161922",
            fg="#64748b",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 8),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=5,
            pady=2,
            command=lambda: self.step_days(-1),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(4, 2))
        tk.Button(
            preset_row,
            text="+1d",
            bg="#161922",
            fg="#64748b",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 8),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=5,
            pady=2,
            command=lambda: self.step_days(1),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 2))

        # 3. Time Quick Presets (if include_time is enabled)
        if self.include_time:
            time_row = tk.Frame(self, bg="#12151c")
            time_row.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

            time_presets = [
                "09:00",
                "12:00",
                "15:00",
                "18:00",
                "21:00",
                "23:59",
            ]
            for t_str in time_presets:
                tk.Button(
                    time_row,
                    text=t_str,
                    bg="#161922",
                    fg="#94a3b8",
                    activebackground="#2b3448",
                    activeforeground="#00e5ff",
                    relief="flat",
                    bd=0,
                    font=(self.theme.font_family, 8),
                    cursor="hand2",
                    padx=5,
                    pady=2,
                    command=lambda t=t_str: self.set_time(t),
                ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 3))

    def get_value(self) -> str:
        """Return the current date/time string from the entry field."""
        return self.entry.get().strip()

    def set_value(self, val: str) -> None:
        """Set the text inside the entry field."""
        self.entry.delete(0, tk.END)
        self.entry.insert(0, val.strip())
        if self.on_change:
            self.on_change(val.strip())

    def insert(self, index: int | str, string: str) -> None:
        """Delegate insert to the internal entry widget."""
        self.entry.insert(index, string)

    def delete(self, first: int | str, last: int | str | None = None) -> None:
        """Delegate delete to the internal entry widget."""
        self.entry.delete(first, last)

    def set_now(self) -> None:
        """Insert the current date and time automatically."""
        now = datetime.datetime.now()
        fmt = "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M" if self.include_time else "%Y-%m-%d"
        self.set_value(now.strftime(fmt))

    def add_days_from_today(self, offset_days: int) -> None:
        """Set date relative to today, preserving time if present."""
        target_date = datetime.date.today() + datetime.timedelta(
            days=offset_days
        )
        date_str = target_date.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

        current = self.get_value()
        time_part = "18:00"
        if " " in current:
            parts = current.split(maxsplit=1)
            time_part = parts[1]

        if self.include_time:
            self.set_value(f"{date_str} {time_part}")
        else:
            self.set_value(date_str)

    def set_time(self, time_str: str) -> None:
        """Update or append the time component to the existing date."""
        current = self.get_value()
        date_part = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        if current:
            parts = current.split(maxsplit=1)
            date_part = parts[0]

        self.set_value(f"{date_part} {time_str}")

    def step_days(self, delta_days: int) -> None:
        """Increment or decrement the current date value by delta_days."""
        current = self.get_value()
        base_date = datetime.date.today()
        time_part = "18:00"

        if current:
            parts = current.split(maxsplit=1)
            with contextlib.suppress(ValueError):
                base_date = datetime.date.fromisoformat(parts[0])
            if len(parts) > 1:
                time_part = parts[1]

        new_date = base_date + datetime.timedelta(days=delta_days)
        date_str = new_date.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

        if self.include_time:
            self.set_value(f"{date_str} {time_part}")
        else:
            self.set_value(date_str)

"""Reusable UI components for PLUS-ULTRA."""
