========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/components/notifications_panel.py ==========
"""Right drawer component with Notifications and Network Status."""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable

from plus_ultra.network import NetworkStatusMonitor
from plus_ultra.notifications import CapsuleNotificationQueue
from plus_ultra.services import CodeService
from plus_ultra.ui.icons import get_icon
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class NotificationsPanel:
    """Right-hand panel containing Notifications and Network Status monitor."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        container: tk.Widget,
        notification_queue: CapsuleNotificationQueue,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
        on_notifications_cleared: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
        code_service: CodeService | None = None,
        parent_window: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.container = container
        self.notification_queue = notification_queue
        self.theme = theme
        self.on_notifications_cleared = on_notifications_cleared
        self.code_service = code_service
        self.parent_window = parent_window

        self.active_tab: str = "Notifications"
        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        # 1. Main Header Bar
        head_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#12151c", padx=16, pady=16)
        head_bar.pack(fill="x")

        icon = get_icon("fallback", 18)
        title_lbl = tk.Label(
            head_bar,
            text=" Recent Alerts",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 13, "bold"),
            image=icon,
            compound="left",
        )
        title_lbl.image = icon  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        title_lbl.pack(anchor="w")

        btn_box = tk.Frame(head_bar, bg="#12151c")
        btn_box.pack(fill="x", pady=(10, 0))

        tk.Button(
            btn_box,
            text="Mark All Read",
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#00e5ff",
            activebackground="#2b3448",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 9, "bold"),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=10,
            pady=5,
            command=self.mark_all_read,
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))

        tk.Button(
            btn_box,
            text="Clear",
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            activebackground="#2b3448",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 9),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=10,
            pady=5,
            command=self.clear_all,
        ).pack(side="left")

        # 2. Notifications Listbox
        list_box_frame = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#12151c", padx=16)
        list_box_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=(6, 12))

        self.notif_listbox = tk.Listbox(
            list_box_frame,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            selectbackground="#2b3448",
            selectforeground="#00e5ff",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.notif_listbox.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        # 3. Bottom Network Status Monitor Card
        net_card = tk.Frame(
            self.container,
            bg="#161922",
            padx=14,
            pady=12,
            highlightbackground="#2b3448",
            highlightthickness=1,
        )
        net_card.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(0, 16))

        net_top = tk.Frame(net_card, bg="#161922")
        net_top.pack(fill="x")

        tk.Label(
            net_top,
            text="Network Status",
            bg="#161922",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
        ).pack(side="left")

        tk.Button(
            net_top,
            text="Probe",
            bg="#1e2433",
            fg="#00e5ff",
            activebackground="#2b3448",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 8),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=6,
            pady=2,
            command=self.probe_network,
        ).pack(side="right")

        self.net_status_label = tk.Label(
            net_card,
            text="● Probing connectivity...",
            bg="#161922",
            fg="#64748b",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
            anchor="w",
        )
        self.net_status_label.pack(fill="x", pady=(6, 0))

        self.refresh()
        self._net_monitor = NetworkStatusMonitor()
        self.probe_network()

    def probe_network(self) -> None:
        """Trigger asynchronous non-blocking network probe."""
        self.net_status_label.configure(
            text="● Checking connection...", fg="#eab308"
        )
        self._net_monitor.probe_async()
        self.container.after(300, self._poll_network)

    def _poll_network(self) -> None:
        """Poll network monitor result queue on main Tk thread."""
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            if not self.container.winfo_exists():
                return
            res = self._net_monitor.get_result()
            if res is not None:
                color = "#10b981" if res else "#ef4444"
                text = "● Online (Connected)" if res else "● Offline (No Route)"
                if (
                    hasattr(self, "net_status_label")
                    and self.net_status_label.winfo_exists()
                ):
                    self.net_status_label.configure(text=text, fg=color)
            else:
                self.container.after(300, self._poll_network)

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        """Update notification items displayed in the listbox."""
        self.notif_listbox.delete(0, tk.END)
        all_notifs = self.notification_queue.list_all()
        if not all_notifs:
            self.notif_listbox.insert(tk.END, "No recent alerts.")
            self.notif_listbox.itemconfig(0, fg="#64748b")
            return

        for idx, item in enumerate(all_notifs):
            prefix = "[Read] " if item.is_read else "[New] "
            line = f"{prefix}{item.title}"
            self.notif_listbox.insert(tk.END, line)
            if not item.is_read:
                self.notif_listbox.itemconfig(idx, fg="#00e5ff")
            else:
                self.notif_listbox.itemconfig(idx, fg="#94a3b8")

    def refresh_dsa(self) -> None:
        """Compatibility no-op method for DSA tab removal."""

    def mark_all_read(self) -> None:
        """Mark all notifications as read and fire callback."""
        self.notification_queue.mark_all_read()
        self.refresh()
        if self.on_notifications_cleared:
            self.on_notifications_cleared()

    def clear_all(self) -> None:
        """Clear all notifications from queue and fire callback."""
        self.notification_queue.clear()
        self.refresh()
        if self.on_notifications_cleared:
            self.on_notifications_cleared()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/components/scrollable.py ==========
"""Reusable scrollable dark frame for card-based sub-views."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk
from typing import Any


class ScrollableFrame(tk.Frame):
    """Reusable scrollable dark frame for card-based sub-views."""

    def __init__(self, parent: tk.Widget, bg: str = "#161922") -> None:
        super().__init__(parent, bg=bg)
        self.canvas = tk.Canvas(self, bg=bg, highlightthickness=0, bd=0)
        self.scrollbar = ttk.Scrollbar(
            self, orient="vertical", command=self.canvas.yview
        )
        self.interior = tk.Frame(self.canvas, bg=bg)

        self.canvas_win = self.canvas.create_window(
            (0, 0), window=self.interior, anchor="nw"
        )
        self.canvas.configure(yscrollcommand=self.scrollbar.set)

        self.scrollbar.pack(side="right", fill="y")
        self.canvas.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)

        self.interior.bind("<Configure>", self._on_interior_configure)
        self.canvas.bind("<Configure>", self._on_canvas_configure)

        self.canvas.bind_all("<Button-4>", self._on_mousewheel_up, add="+")
        self.canvas.bind_all("<Button-5>", self._on_mousewheel_down, add="+")

    def _on_interior_configure(self, _event: tk.Event[Any]) -> None:
        self.canvas.configure(scrollregion=self.canvas.bbox("all"))

    def _on_canvas_configure(self, event: tk.Event[Any]) -> None:
        self.canvas.itemconfig(self.canvas_win, width=event.width)

    def _on_mousewheel_up(self, _event: tk.Event[Any]) -> None:
        if self.winfo_ismapped():
            self.canvas.yview_scroll(-1, "units")

    def _on_mousewheel_down(self, _event: tk.Event[Any]) -> None:
        if self.winfo_ismapped():
            self.canvas.yview_scroll(1, "units")


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/components/sidebar.py ==========
"""Sidebar navigation component for PLUS-ULTRA workspace panel."""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable

from plus_ultra.network import NetworkStatusMonitor
from plus_ultra.ui.icons import get_icon
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class Sidebar:
    """Left navigation sidebar with draggable tabs and clean styling."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        container: tk.Widget,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
        on_switch_tab: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
        on_close: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.container = container
        self.theme = theme
        self.on_switch_tab = on_switch_tab
        self.on_close = on_close
        self.tab_buttons: dict[str, tk.Button] = {}

        self.nav_items: list[tuple[str, str]] = [
            ("Tasks", "Tasks"),
            ("Code", "Code c-p"),
            ("DSA", "DSA Tracker"),
            ("Courses", "Courses"),
            ("Ideas-Doubt", "PS/I"),
            ("Opportunities", "Opportunities"),
            ("Quick Links", "Quick Links"),
        ]

        self._drag_tab_id: str | None = None
        self._drag_start_y: int = 0
        self._is_dragging: bool = False
        self._active_tab: str = "Tasks"

        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        # App brand header
        brand_frame = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#12151c", padx=20, pady=24)
        brand_frame.pack(fill="x")

        icon = get_icon("fallback", 24)
        brand_label = tk.Label(
            brand_frame,
            text=" PLUS-ULTRA",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#00e5ff",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 16, "bold"),
            image=icon,
            compound="left",
        )
        brand_label.image = icon  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        brand_label.pack(anchor="w")

        tk.Label(
            brand_frame,
            text="Workspace & System Assistant",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#64748b",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 0))

        # Nav items container
        self.nav_frame = tk.Frame(
            self.container, bg="#12151c", padx=12, pady=10
        )
        self.nav_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        self._render_tabs()

        # Bottom section: Icon-only Settings on left, Network status on right
        bottom_frame = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#12151c", padx=16, pady=16)
        bottom_frame.pack(side="bottom", fill="x")

        # Fallback to fallback icon if settings icon is missing
        settings_icon = get_icon("settings", 24) or get_icon("fallback", 24)
        self.settings_btn = tk.Button(
            bottom_frame,
            image=settings_icon,
            bg="#12151c",
            activebackground="#1e2433",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=8,
            pady=8,
            command=lambda: self._on_tab_click("Settings"),
        )
        self.settings_btn.image = settings_icon  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        self.settings_btn.pack(side="left")

        self.net_status_lbl = tk.Label(
            bottom_frame,
            text="● Online",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#10b981",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 9, "bold"),
        )
        self.net_status_lbl.pack(side="right", padx=(0, 4))

        self._net_monitor = NetworkStatusMonitor()
        self._net_monitor.probe_async()
        self.container.after(300, self._poll_network_status)

    def _render_tabs(self) -> None:
        """Render navigation tab buttons in current order."""
        for child in self.nav_frame.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()
        self.tab_buttons.clear()

        for tab_id, tab_label in self.nav_items:
            is_active = tab_id == self._active_tab
            btn = tk.Button(
                self.nav_frame,
                text=tab_label,
                anchor="w",
                bg="#1e2433" if is_active else "#12151c",
                fg="#00e5ff" if is_active else "#94a3b8",
                activebackground="#1e2433",
                activeforeground="#00e5ff",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                highlightthickness=0,
                font=(
                    self.theme.font_family,
                    13,
                    "bold" if is_active else "normal",
                ),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=20,
                pady=16,
            )
            btn.pack(fill="x", pady=3)
            self.tab_buttons[tab_id] = btn

            btn.bind(
                "<ButtonPress-1>",
                lambda e, tid=tab_id: self._on_press(e, tid),
            )
            btn.bind("<B1-Motion>", self._on_motion)
            btn.bind(
                "<ButtonRelease-1>",
                lambda e, tid=tab_id: self._on_release(e, tid),
            )

    def _on_press(self, event: tk.Event, tab_id: str) -> None:
        self._drag_tab_id = tab_id
        self._drag_start_y = event.y_root
        self._is_dragging = False

    def _on_motion(self, event: tk.Event) -> None:
        if abs(event.y_root - self._drag_start_y) > 12:
            self._is_dragging = True

    def _on_release(self, event: tk.Event, tab_id: str) -> None:
        if self._is_dragging and self._drag_tab_id:
            self._reorder_tab(self._drag_tab_id, event.y_root)
        else:
            self._on_tab_click(tab_id)
        self._drag_tab_id = None
        self._is_dragging = False

    def _reorder_tab(self, tab_id: str, release_y: int) -> None:
        """Move dragged tab to the slot corresponding to release_y."""
        if not self.tab_buttons:
            return

        current_idx = next(
            (i for i, (tid, _) in enumerate(self.nav_items) if tid == tab_id),
            None,
        )
        if current_idx is None:
            return

        target_idx = current_idx
        for idx, (tid, _) in enumerate(self.nav_items):
            btn = self.tab_buttons.get(tid)
            if btn and btn.winfo_exists() and btn.winfo_rooty() > 0:
                y_center = btn.winfo_rooty() + (btn.winfo_height() // 2)
                if release_y < y_center:
                    target_idx = idx
                    break
        else:
            target_idx = len(self.nav_items) - 1

        if target_idx != current_idx:
            item = self.nav_items.pop(current_idx)
            self.nav_items.insert(target_idx, item)
            self._render_tabs()

    def _on_tab_click(self, tab_id: str) -> None:
        self._active_tab = tab_id
        self.highlight_tab(tab_id)
        if self.on_switch_tab:
            self.on_switch_tab(tab_id)

    def _poll_network_status(self) -> None:
        """Poll network monitor result queue on main Tk thread."""
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            if not self.container.winfo_exists():
                return
            res = self._net_monitor.get_result()
            if res is not None:
                color = "#10b981" if res else "#ef4444"
                text = "● Online" if res else "● Offline"
                if (
                    hasattr(self, "net_status_lbl")
                    and self.net_status_lbl.winfo_exists()
                ):
                    self.net_status_lbl.configure(text=text, fg=color)
            else:
                self.container.after(300, self._poll_network_status)

    def highlight_tab(self, active_tab: str) -> None:
        """Update active button styling across navigation items."""
        self._active_tab = active_tab
        for name, btn in self.tab_buttons.items():
            if name == active_tab:
                btn.configure(
                    bg="#1e2433",
                    fg="#00e5ff",
                    font=(self.theme.font_family, 13, "bold"),
                )
            else:
                btn.configure(
                    bg="#12151c",
                    fg="#94a3b8",
                    font=(self.theme.font_family, 13),
                )
        if hasattr(self, "settings_btn"):
            self.settings_btn.configure(
                bg="#1e2433" if active_tab == "Settings" else "#12151c"
            )


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/dialogs/base.py ==========
"""Base helpers and utilities for modal dialogs."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk


def center_modal(dialog: tk.Toplevel, parent: tk.Widget) -> None:
    """Center modal dialog over parent window."""
    dialog.update_idletasks()
    pw = parent.winfo_width()
    ph = parent.winfo_height()
    px = parent.winfo_x()
    py = parent.winfo_y()
    w = dialog.winfo_width()
    h = dialog.winfo_height()
    x = max(20, px + (pw - w) // 2)
    y = max(20, py + (ph - h) // 2)
    dialog.geometry(f"+{x}+{y}")


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/dialogs/category_dialog.py ==========
"""Modal dialog for creating a new Category."""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable

from plus_ultra.services import CategoryService
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs.base import center_modal
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class CategoryDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    """Modal dialog for creating new task categories."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        parent: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        category_service: CategoryService,
        on_success: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.category_service = category_service
        self.on_success = on_success
        self.theme = theme

        self.title("Add Category")
        self.geometry("520x280")
        self.minsize(440, 240)
        self.configure(bg="#12151c")
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()

        self._build_ui()
        center_modal(self, parent)

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        footer = tk.Frame(self, bg="#0d1117", padx=24, pady=16)
        footer.pack(side="bottom", fill="x")

        body = tk.Frame(self, bg="#12151c", padx=24, pady=20)
        body.pack(side="top", fill="both", expand=True)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Category Name *",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 12),
        )
        self.entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(6, 0), ipady=6)

        tk.Button(
            footer,
            text="Save Category",
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self._save,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=18,
            pady=8,
        ).pack(side="right", padx=(8, 0))

        tk.Button(
            footer,
            text="Cancel",
            bg="#2b3448",
            fg="#e2e8f0",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
            command=self.destroy,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=14,
            pady=8,
        ).pack(side="right")

    def _save(self) -> None:
        name = self.entry.get().strip()
        if not name:
            return
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            self.category_service.create_category(name)
        if self.on_success:
            self.on_success()
        self.destroy()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/dialogs/code_dialog.py ==========
"""Modal dialog for creating or editing code snippets and DSA problems."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable
from tkinter import messagebox, ttk
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import CodeItem
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs.base import center_modal
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class CodeDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    """Modal dialog for creating or editing code snippets and DSA problems."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        parent: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        item: CodeItem | None = None,
        on_save: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], None] | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.item = item
        self.on_save = on_save
        self.theme = theme

        self.title("Edit Code Item" if item else "Add Code Item")
        self.geometry("680x680")
        self.minsize(580, 580)
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()

        self._build_ui()
        center_modal(self, parent)

    @property
    def lang_entry(self) -> ttk.Combobox:
        """Compatibility property for test assertions."""
        return self.lang_combo

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        self.configure(bg="#12151c")

        footer = tk.Frame(self, bg="#0d1117", padx=28, pady=18)
        footer.pack(side="bottom", fill="x")

        save_label = "Update Item" if self.item else "Create Item"
        tk.Button(
            footer,
            text=save_label,
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self._handle_save,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=20,
            pady=10,
        ).pack(side="right", padx=(10, 0))

        tk.Button(
            footer,
            text="Cancel",
            bg="#2b3448",
            fg="#e2e8f0",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
            command=self.destroy,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=16,
            pady=10,
        ).pack(side="right")

        body = tk.Frame(self, bg="#12151c", padx=24, pady=20)
        body.pack(side="top", fill="both", expand=True)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Title / Problem Name *",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.title_entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.title_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 12), ipady=5)
        if self.item:
            self.title_entry.insert(0, self.item.title)

        r1 = tk.Frame(body, bg="#12151c")
        r1.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

        cat_box = tk.Frame(r1, bg="#12151c")
        cat_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 8))
        tk.Label(
            cat_box,
            text="Category",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.cat_combo = ttk.Combobox(
            cat_box,
            values=[
                "Snippets",
                "CLI Commands",
                "Syntax Reference",
                "DSA Problem Tracker",
            ],
            state="readonly",
        )
        self.cat_combo.set(self.item.category if self.item else "Snippets")
        self.cat_combo.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        lang_box = tk.Frame(r1, bg="#12151c")
        lang_box.pack(side="right", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(8, 0))
        tk.Label(
            lang_box,
            text="Language / Tool",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        predefined_langs = [
            "Python",
            "JavaScript",
            "TypeScript",
            "C",
            "C++",
            "Java",
            "Rust",
            "Go",
            "Bash",
            "SQL",
            "HTML/CSS",
            "JSON",
            "Markdown",
            "Other",
        ]
        self.lang_combo = ttk.Combobox(
            lang_box,
            values=predefined_langs,
            state="normal",
        )
        init_lang = (
            self.item.language if self.item and self.item.language else "Python"
        )
        self.lang_combo.set(init_lang)
        self.lang_combo.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        r2 = tk.Frame(body, bg="#12151c")
        r2.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

        diff_box = tk.Frame(r2, bg="#12151c")
        diff_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 8))
        tk.Label(
            diff_box,
            text="Difficulty (DSA)",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.diff_combo = ttk.Combobox(
            diff_box, values=["Easy", "Medium", "Hard"], state="readonly"
        )
        self.diff_combo.set(self.item.difficulty if self.item else "Easy")
        self.diff_combo.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        url_box = tk.Frame(r2, bg="#12151c")
        url_box.pack(side="right", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(8, 0))
        tk.Label(
            url_box,
            text="Problem / Reference URL (optional)",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.url_entry = tk.Entry(
            url_box,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        if self.item and self.item.url:
            self.url_entry.insert(0, self.item.url)
        self.url_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0), ipady=4)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Code Snippet / Command Content",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.code_text = tk.Text(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=("courier", 10),
            height=6,
        )
        self.code_text.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=(4, 0))
        if self.item and self.item.content:
            self.code_text.insert("1.0", self.item.content)

    def _handle_save(self) -> None:
        title = self.title_entry.get().strip()
        if not title:
            messagebox.showerror(
                "Validation Error", "Title cannot be empty.", parent=self
            )
            return

        payload = {
            "title": title,
            "category": self.cat_combo.get(),
            "language": self.lang_combo.get().strip() or "Python",
            "difficulty": self.diff_combo.get(),
            "url": self.url_entry.get().strip(),
            "content": self.code_text.get("1.0", "end-1c").strip(),
        }
        if self.on_save:
            self.on_save(payload)
        self.destroy()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/dialogs/course_dialog.py ==========
"""Modal dialog for creating or editing academic/skill courses."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable
from tkinter import messagebox, ttk
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import Course
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs.base import center_modal
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class CourseDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    """Modal dialog for creating or editing academic/skill courses."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        parent: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        course: Course | None = None,
        on_save: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], None] | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.course = course
        self.on_save = on_save
        self.theme = theme

        self.title("Edit Course" if course else "Add Course")
        self.geometry("640x580")
        self.minsize(560, 520)
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()

        self._build_ui()
        center_modal(self, parent)

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        self.configure(bg="#12151c")

        footer = tk.Frame(self, bg="#0d1117", padx=28, pady=18)
        footer.pack(side="bottom", fill="x")

        save_label = "Update Course" if self.course else "Create Course"
        tk.Button(
            footer,
            text=save_label,
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self._handle_save,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=20,
            pady=10,
        ).pack(side="right", padx=(10, 0))

        tk.Button(
            footer,
            text="Cancel",
            bg="#2b3448",
            fg="#e2e8f0",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
            command=self.destroy,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=16,
            pady=10,
        ).pack(side="right")

        body = tk.Frame(self, bg="#12151c", padx=28, pady=24)
        body.pack(side="top", fill="both", expand=True)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Course Title *",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.title_entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.title_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 12), ipady=5)
        if self.course:
            self.title_entry.insert(0, self.course.title)

        r1 = tk.Frame(body, bg="#12151c")
        r1.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

        plat_box = tk.Frame(r1, bg="#12151c")
        plat_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 8))
        tk.Label(
            plat_box,
            text="Platform",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.plat_combo = ttk.Combobox(
            plat_box,
            values=["University", "YouTube", "Independent", "Book", "Other"],
            state="readonly",
        )
        self.plat_combo.set(
            self.course.platform if self.course else "University"
        )
        self.plat_combo.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        inst_box = tk.Frame(r1, bg="#12151c")
        inst_box.pack(side="right", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(8, 0))
        tk.Label(
            inst_box,
            text="Instructor / Source",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.inst_entry = tk.Entry(
            inst_box,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        if self.course and self.course.instructor:
            self.inst_entry.insert(0, self.course.instructor)
        self.inst_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0), ipady=4)

        r2 = tk.Frame(body, bg="#12151c")
        r2.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

        tot_box = tk.Frame(r2, bg="#12151c")
        tot_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 8))
        tk.Label(
            tot_box,
            text="Total Modules",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.tot_entry = tk.Entry(
            tot_box,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.tot_entry.insert(
            0, str(self.course.total_modules if self.course else 10)
        )
        self.tot_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0), ipady=4)

        done_box = tk.Frame(r2, bg="#12151c")
        done_box.pack(side="right", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(8, 0))
        tk.Label(
            done_box,
            text="Completed Modules",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.done_entry = tk.Entry(
            done_box,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.done_entry.insert(
            0, str(self.course.completed_modules if self.course else 0)
        )
        self.done_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0), ipady=4)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Course / Lecture URL (optional)",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.url_entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        if self.course and self.course.url:
            self.url_entry.insert(0, self.course.url)
        self.url_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 12), ipady=4)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Notes / Topics",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.notes_entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        if self.course and self.course.notes:
            self.notes_entry.insert(0, self.course.notes)
        self.notes_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0), ipady=4)

    def _handle_save(self) -> None:
        title = self.title_entry.get().strip()
        if not title:
            messagebox.showerror(
                "Validation Error", "Course title cannot be empty.", parent=self
            )
            return

        try:
            tot = max(1, int(self.tot_entry.get().strip() or "10"))
        except ValueError:
            tot = 10

        try:
            done = max(0, min(tot, int(self.done_entry.get().strip() or "0")))
        except ValueError:
            done = 0

        payload = {
            "title": title,
            "platform": self.plat_combo.get(),
            "instructor": self.inst_entry.get().strip(),
            "total_modules": tot,
            "completed_modules": done,
            "url": self.url_entry.get().strip(),
            "notes": self.notes_entry.get().strip(),
        }
        if self.on_save:
            self.on_save(payload)
        self.destroy()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/dialogs/idea_dialog.py ==========
"""Modal dialog for creating or editing ideas and concept doubts."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable
from tkinter import messagebox, ttk
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import IdeaDoubt
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs.base import center_modal
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class IdeaDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    """Modal dialog for creating or editing ideas and concept doubts."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        parent: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        item: IdeaDoubt | None = None,
        on_save: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], None] | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
        default_kind: str = "Idea",
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.item = item
        self.on_save = on_save
        self.theme = theme
        self.default_kind = default_kind

        self.title("Edit Entry" if item else "Add Idea / Doubt")
        self.geometry("640x620")
        self.minsize(560, 540)
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()

        self._build_ui()
        center_modal(self, parent)

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        self.configure(bg="#12151c")

        footer = tk.Frame(self, bg="#0d1117", padx=28, pady=18)
        footer.pack(side="bottom", fill="x")

        save_label = "Update Entry" if self.item else "Create Entry"
        tk.Button(
            footer,
            text=save_label,
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self._handle_save,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=20,
            pady=10,
        ).pack(side="right", padx=(10, 0))

        tk.Button(
            footer,
            text="Cancel",
            bg="#2b3448",
            fg="#e2e8f0",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
            command=self.destroy,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=16,
            pady=10,
        ).pack(side="right")

        body = tk.Frame(self, bg="#12151c", padx=24, pady=20)
        body.pack(side="top", fill="both", expand=True)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Title / Question *",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.title_entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.title_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 12), ipady=5)
        if self.item:
            self.title_entry.insert(0, self.item.title)

        r1 = tk.Frame(body, bg="#12151c")
        r1.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

        kind_box = tk.Frame(r1, bg="#12151c")
        kind_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 8))
        tk.Label(
            kind_box,
            text="Entry Kind",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.kind_combo = ttk.Combobox(
            kind_box, values=["Idea", "Doubt"], state="readonly"
        )
        self.kind_combo.set(self.item.kind if self.item else self.default_kind)
        self.kind_combo.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))
        self.kind_combo.bind("<<ComboboxSelected>>", self._on_kind_change)

        status_box = tk.Frame(r1, bg="#12151c")
        status_box.pack(side="right", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(8, 0))
        tk.Label(
            status_box,
            text="Initial Status",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.status_combo = ttk.Combobox(status_box, state="readonly")
        self._update_status_options(self.kind_combo.get())
        if self.item:
            self.status_combo.set(self.item.status)
        self.status_combo.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Tech Stack / Topic Reference",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.tech_entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        if self.item and self.item.tech_stack_or_ref:
            self.tech_entry.insert(0, self.item.tech_stack_or_ref)
        self.tech_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 12), ipady=4)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Description / Notes",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.desc_text = tk.Text(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            height=5,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        )
        if self.item and self.item.description:
            self.desc_text.insert("1.0", self.item.description)
        self.desc_text.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=(4, 0))

    def _on_kind_change(self, _event: tk.Event[Any]) -> None:
        self._update_status_options(self.kind_combo.get())

    def _update_status_options(self, kind: str) -> None:
        if kind == "Doubt":
            self.status_combo["values"] = ["Open", "Resolved"]
            self.status_combo.set("Open")
        else:
            self.status_combo["values"] = [
                "Idea",
                "Planned",
                "In Progress",
                "Completed",
            ]
            self.status_combo.set("Idea")

    def _handle_save(self) -> None:
        title = self.title_entry.get().strip()
        if not title:
            messagebox.showerror(
                "Validation Error", "Title cannot be empty.", parent=self
            )
            return

        payload = {
            "title": title,
            "kind": self.kind_combo.get(),
            "status": self.status_combo.get(),
            "tech_stack_or_ref": self.tech_entry.get().strip(),
            "description": self.desc_text.get("1.0", "end-1c").strip(),
        }
        if self.on_save:
            self.on_save(payload)
        self.destroy()

"""Modal dialog for creating or editing career opportunities."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable
from tkinter import messagebox, ttk

from plus_ultra.ui.components.datetime_picker import DateTimePicker
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs.base import center_modal
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class OpportunityDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    """Modal dialog for creating or editing career opportunities."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        parent: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        item: Opportunity | None = None,

        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.item = item
        self.on_save = on_save
        self.theme = theme

        self.title("Edit Opportunity" if item else "Add Opportunity")
        self.geometry("640x640")
        self.minsize(560, 560)
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()

        self._build_ui()
        center_modal(self, parent)

    @property
    def dead_entry(self) -> tk.Entry:
        """Compatibility property for test assertions."""
        return self.datetime_picker.entry

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        self.configure(bg="#12151c")

        footer = tk.Frame(self, bg="#0d1117", padx=28, pady=18)

        ).pack(side="right", padx=(10, 0))

            footer,

            relief="flat",

            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
            command=self.destroy,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=16,
            pady=10,
        ).pack(side="right")

        body = tk.Frame(self, bg="#12151c", padx=24, pady=20)
        body.pack(side="top", fill="both", expand=True)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Role / Program Title *",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.title_entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.title_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 12), ipady=5)
        if self.item:
            self.title_entry.insert(0, self.item.title)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Company / Organization *",
            bg="#12151c",

        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.org_entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.org_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 12), ipady=5)
        if self.item:
            self.org_entry.insert(0, self.item.organization)

        r1 = tk.Frame(body, bg="#12151c")
        r1.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

        kind_box = tk.Frame(r1, bg="#12151c")
        kind_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 8))
        tk.Label(
            kind_box,
            text="Opportunity Type",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.kind_combo = ttk.Combobox(
            kind_box,
            values=[
                "Internship",
                "Full-time",
                "Hackathon",
                "Fellowship",
                "Research",
            ],
            state="readonly",
        )
        self.kind_combo.set(self.item.kind if self.item else "Internship")
        self.kind_combo.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        status_box = tk.Frame(r1, bg="#12151c")
        status_box.pack(side="right", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(8, 0))
        tk.Label(
            status_box,
            text="Application Status",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.status_combo = ttk.Combobox(
            status_box,
            values=["Saved", "Applied", "In Progress", "Accepted", "Rejected"],
            state="readonly",
        )
        self.status_combo.set(self.item.status if self.item else "Saved")
        self.status_combo.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        r2 = tk.Frame(body, bg="#12151c")
        r2.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

        dead_box = tk.Frame(r2, bg="#12151c")
        dead_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 8))
        tk.Label(
            dead_box,
            text="Deadline (Presets & 'Now')",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        init_dead = ""
        if self.item and self.item.deadline:
            init_dead = self.item.deadline
        self.datetime_picker = DateTimePicker(
            dead_box,
            initial_value=init_dead,
            include_time=False,
            theme=self.theme,
        )
        self.datetime_picker.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        url_box = tk.Frame(r2, bg="#12151c")
        url_box.pack(side="right", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(8, 0))
        tk.Label(
            url_box,
            text="Application Link / Portal URL",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.url_entry = tk.Entry(
            url_box,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        if self.item and self.item.url:
            self.url_entry.insert(0, self.item.url)
        self.url_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0), ipady=4)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Notes / Requirements",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.notes_entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        if self.item and self.item.notes:
            self.notes_entry.insert(0, self.item.notes)
        self.notes_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0), ipady=4)

    def _handle_save(self) -> None:
        title = self.title_entry.get().strip()
        org = self.org_entry.get().strip()
        if not title or not org:
            messagebox.showerror(
                "Validation Error",
                "Title and Organization are required.",
                parent=self,
            )
            return

        payload = {
            "title": title,
            "organization": org,
            "kind": self.kind_combo.get(),
            "status": self.status_combo.get(),
            "deadline": self.datetime_picker.get_value(),
            "url": self.url_entry.get().strip(),
            "notes": self.notes_entry.get().strip(),
        }
        if self.on_save:
            self.on_save(payload)
        self.destroy()

"""Modal dialog for creating or editing quick links and shortcuts."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable
from tkinter import messagebox, ttk
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import QuickLink
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs.base import center_modal
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class QuickLinkDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    """Modal dialog for creating or editing quick links and shortcuts."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        parent: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        link: QuickLink | None = None,
        on_save: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], None] | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.link = link
        self.on_save = on_save
        self.theme = theme

        self.title("Edit Shortcut" if link else "Add Quick Link")
        self.geometry("600x480")
        self.minsize(520, 420)
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()

        self._build_ui()
        center_modal(self, parent)

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        self.configure(bg="#12151c")

        footer = tk.Frame(self, bg="#0d1117", padx=28, pady=18)
        footer.pack(side="bottom", fill="x")

        save_label = "Update Shortcut" if self.link else "Create Shortcut"
        tk.Button(
            footer,
            text=save_label,
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self._handle_save,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=20,
            pady=10,
        ).pack(side="right", padx=(10, 0))

        tk.Button(
            footer,
            text="Cancel",
            bg="#2b3448",
            fg="#e2e8f0",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
            command=self.destroy,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=16,
            pady=10,
        ).pack(side="right")

        body = tk.Frame(self, bg="#12151c", padx=24, pady=20)
        body.pack(side="top", fill="both", expand=True)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Display Name *",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.name_entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.name_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 12), ipady=5)
        if self.link:
            self.name_entry.insert(0, self.link.name)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Target URI (Web URL or Local Path) *",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.uri_entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.uri_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 12), ipady=5)
        if self.link:
            self.uri_entry.insert(0, self.link.target_uri)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Category",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.cat_combo = ttk.Combobox(
            body,
            values=["Web", "Local Folder", "Local File", "Tool"],
            state="readonly",
        )
        self.cat_combo.set(self.link.category if self.link else "Web")
        self.cat_combo.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

    def _handle_save(self) -> None:
        name = self.name_entry.get().strip()
        uri = self.uri_entry.get().strip()
        if not name or not uri:
            messagebox.showerror(
                "Validation Error",
                "Display Name and Target URI are required.",
                parent=self,
            )
            return

        payload = {
            "name": name,
            "target_uri": uri,
            "category": self.cat_combo.get(),
        }
        if self.on_save:
            self.on_save(payload)
        self.destroy()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/dialogs/task_dialog.py ==========
"""Modal dialog for creating or editing tasks with interactive Date & Time."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable
from tkinter import messagebox, ttk
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import Category, Task
from plus_ultra.services import CategoryService
from plus_ultra.ui.components.datetime_picker import DateTimePicker
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs.base import center_modal
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs.category_dialog import CategoryDialog
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class TaskDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    """Modal dialog for creating or editing tasks with interactive Date/Time."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        parent: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        categories: list[Category],
        task: Task | None = None,
        on_save: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], None] | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
        category_service: CategoryService | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.task = task
        self.categories = list(categories)
        self.on_save = on_save
        self.theme = theme
        self.category_service = category_service

        self.title("Edit Task" if task else "Create New Task")
        self.geometry("680x720")
        self.minsize(600, 640)
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()

        self._build_ui()
        center_modal(self, parent)

    @property
    def due_entry(self) -> tk.Entry:
        """Compatibility property for test assertions."""
        return self.datetime_picker.entry

    @property
    def time_entry(self) -> tk.Entry:
        """Compatibility property for test assertions."""
        return self.datetime_picker.entry

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        self.configure(bg="#12151c")

        # 1. FIXED BOTTOM ACTION BAR
        footer = tk.Frame(self, bg="#0d1117", padx=28, pady=18)
        footer.pack(side="bottom", fill="x")

        save_label = "Update Task" if self.task else "Create Task"
        save_btn = tk.Button(
            footer,
            text=save_label,
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self._handle_save,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=20,
            pady=10,
        )
        save_btn.pack(side="right", padx=(10, 0))

        cancel_btn = tk.Button(
            footer,
            text="Cancel",
            bg="#2b3448",
            fg="#e2e8f0",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
            command=self.destroy,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=14,
            pady=8,
        )
        cancel_btn.pack(side="right")

        # 2. MAIN FORM BODY
        body = tk.Frame(self, bg="#12151c", padx=24, pady=20)
        body.pack(side="top", fill="both", expand=True)

        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Task Title *",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
        ).pack(anchor="w")

        self.title_entry = tk.Entry(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            insertbackground="#00e5ff",
            relief="flat",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.title_entry.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 12), ipady=5)
        if self.task:
            self.title_entry.insert(0, self.task.title)

        # Row 1: Category & Priority
        r1 = tk.Frame(body, bg="#12151c")
        r1.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

        cat_box = tk.Frame(r1, bg="#12151c")
        cat_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 8))
        tk.Label(
            cat_box,
            text="Category",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")

        cat_input_row = tk.Frame(cat_box, bg="#12151c")
        cat_input_row.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        self.cat_combo = ttk.Combobox(
            cat_input_row,
            values=self._get_cat_values(),
            state="readonly",
        )
        cur_cat = "General"
        if self.task and self.task.category_id:
            for c in self.categories:
                if c.id == self.task.category_id:
                    cur_cat = c.name
                    break
        self.cat_combo.set(cur_cat)
        self.cat_combo.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
        self.cat_combo.bind("<<ComboboxSelected>>", self._on_cat_selected)

        # Quick inline add category button
        add_cat_btn = tk.Button(
            cat_input_row,
            text="+",
            bg="#1e2433",
            fg="#00e5ff",
            activebackground="#2b3448",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=8,
            pady=2,
            command=self._prompt_new_category,
        )
        add_cat_btn.pack(side="left", padx=(6, 0))

        prio_box = tk.Frame(r1, bg="#12151c")
        prio_box.pack(side="right", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(8, 0))
        tk.Label(
            prio_box,
            text="Priority",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.prio_combo = ttk.Combobox(
            prio_box, values=["High", "Medium", "Low"], state="readonly"
        )
        self.prio_combo.set(self.task.critical_level if self.task else "Medium")
        self.prio_combo.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        # Row 2: Pace
        r2 = tk.Frame(body, bg="#12151c")
        r2.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

        pace_box = tk.Frame(r2, bg="#12151c")
        pace_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 8))
        tk.Label(
            pace_box,
            text="Pace",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.pace_combo = ttk.Combobox(
            pace_box, values=["Short-term", "Long-term"], state="readonly"
        )
        self.pace_combo.set(self.task.pace_level if self.task else "Short-term")
        self.pace_combo.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        # Row 3: Interactive Date & Time Picker
        due_section = tk.Frame(body, bg="#12151c")
        due_section.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

        tk.Label(
            due_section,
            text="Due Date & Time (Interactive Presets & 'Now')",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")

        init_due = ""
        if self.task and self.task.due_date:
            init_due = self.task.due_date
        self.datetime_picker = DateTimePicker(
            due_section,
            initial_value=init_due,
            include_time=True,
            theme=self.theme,
        )
        self.datetime_picker.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        # Row 4: Reminder Checkbox
        self.remind_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=True)
        rem_chk = tk.Checkbutton(
            body,
            text="Schedule desktop notification at Due Date/Time",
            variable=self.remind_var,
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            selectcolor="#1c212c",
            activebackground="#12151c",
            activeforeground="#00e5ff",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 9),
        )
        rem_chk.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 10))

        # Row 5: Notes / Description
        tk.Label(
            body,
            text="Description / Notes",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        self.desc_text = tk.Text(
            body,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            height=4,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        )
        self.desc_text.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=(4, 0))
        if self.task and self.task.description:
            self.desc_text.insert("1.0", self.task.description)

    def _get_cat_values(self) -> list[str]:
        names = [c.name for c in self.categories] or ["General"]
        return names + ["+ Add New Category..."]

    def _on_cat_selected(self, _event: tk.Event) -> None:
        if self.cat_combo.get() == "+ Add New Category...":
            self._prompt_new_category()

    def _prompt_new_category(self) -> None:
        if self.category_service:
            CategoryDialog(
                self,
                category_service=self.category_service,
                on_success=self._on_category_created,
                theme=self.theme,
            )
        else:
            messagebox.showinfo(
                "Category Service",
                "Category service unavailable.",
                parent=self,
            )

    def _on_category_created(self) -> None:
        if self.category_service:
            self.categories = self.category_service.list_categories()
            new_vals = self._get_cat_values()
            self.cat_combo.configure(values=new_vals)
            if self.categories:
                self.cat_combo.set(self.categories[-1].name)

    def _handle_save(self) -> None:
        title = self.title_entry.get().strip()
        if not title:
            messagebox.showerror(
                "Validation Error", "Task title cannot be empty.", parent=self
            )
            return

        cat_id = None
        chosen_cat = self.cat_combo.get()
        for c in self.categories:
            if c.name == chosen_cat:
                cat_id = c.id
                break

        full_due_str = self.datetime_picker.get_value()

        payload = {
            "title": title,
            "category_id": cat_id,
            "critical_level": self.prio_combo.get(),
            "pace_level": self.pace_combo.get(),
            "due_date": full_due_str,
            "schedule_reminder": self.remind_var.get(),
            "description": self.desc_text.get("1.0", "end-1c").strip(),
        }

        if self.on_save:
            self.on_save(payload)
        self.destroy()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/floating.py ==========
"""Floating desktop capsule widget for PLUS-ULTRA.

Applies OS and UI concepts:
- Compact Main Capsule: Top-center hovering presence with unread bell indicator.
- Orbiting Beam on Hover: Trailing animated border beam runs exclusively
  when mouse cursor hovers over the capsule contour.
- Transient Popup Capsule: 3-second temporary notification pill positioned
  directly below the main capsule.
- Unread Persistence: Orange bell remains visible on the main capsule as long
  as unread notifications exist.
- XWayland Shape Masking: Hardware clipping to eliminate rectangular corners.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import logging
import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra import settings
from plus_ultra.desktop import apply_window_shape_mask
from plus_ultra.notifications import (
    CapsuleNotification,
    CapsuleNotificationQueue,
)
from plus_ultra.ui.geometry import (
    generate_capsule_points,
    generate_perimeter_path,
)
from plus_ultra.ui.icons import get_icon
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import GEOMETRY, THEME, CapsuleGeometry, CapsuleTheme

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class FloatingWidget:
    """Always-on-top hovering capsule widget with hover beam and popups."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        root: tk.Tk | None = None,
        geometry: CapsuleGeometry = GEOMETRY,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
        on_click_callback: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
        initial_x: int | None = None,
        initial_y: int | None = None,
        on_position_change: Callable[[int, int], None] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.geometry = geometry
        self.theme = theme
        self.on_click_callback = on_click_callback
        self.initial_x = initial_x
        self.initial_y = initial_y
        self.on_position_change = on_position_change
        self.root = root or tk.Tk()

        # Notification State Queue
        self.notifications = CapsuleNotificationQueue(
            min_width=self.geometry.min_width,
            max_width=self.geometry.max_width,
        )

        # Fixed Compact Capsule Dimensions
        self.width: int = self.geometry.width
        self.height: int = self.geometry.height
        self.radius: int = self.geometry.radius

        # Mouse & State Flags
        self._drag_start_x: int = 0
        self._drag_start_y: int = 0
        self._dragged: bool = False
        self._is_hovered: bool = False
        self._active_toggle: bool = False
        self._beam_idx: int = 0
        self._beam_length: int = 56
        self._beam_timer_id: str | None = None
        self._closed: bool = False

        # Popup window reference and dismiss timer
        self._popup_window: tk.Toplevel | None = None
        self._popup_dismiss_timer_id: str | None = None

        self._load_preferences()
        self._setup_window()
        self._build_canvas()
        self._bind_events()

    def _load_preferences(self) -> None:
        """Load user preferences for window stacking and animations."""
        eff: dict[str, Any] = settings.load_effective_settings()
        self.always_on_top: bool = eff.get("always_on_top", True)
        self.glow_enabled: bool = eff.get("glow_enabled", True)

    def _setup_window(self) -> None:
        """Configure borderless window placement at top-center of screen."""
        self.root.overrideredirect(True)
        if self.always_on_top:
            self.root.attributes("-topmost", True)

        screen_w = self.root.winfo_screenwidth()
        default_x = (screen_w - self.width) // 2
        default_y = self.geometry.top_offset
        init_x = self.initial_x if self.initial_x is not None else default_x
        init_y = self.initial_y if self.initial_y is not None else default_y

        self.root.geometry(f"{self.width}x{self.height}+{init_x}+{init_y}")
        self.root.configure(bg=self.theme.window_bg)

    def _build_canvas(self) -> None:
        """Construct canvas and draw capsule polygon, beam, and text."""
        w = self.width
        h = self.height
        r = self.radius

        self.canvas = tk.Canvas(
            self.root,
            width=w,
            height=h,
            bg=self.theme.window_bg,
            highlightthickness=0,
            cursor="hand2",
        )
        self.canvas.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        # 1. Base capsule polygon (clean dark fill)
        points = generate_capsule_points(w, h, r)
        self.capsule_id = self.canvas.create_polygon(
            points,
            fill=self.theme.idle_fill,
            outline=self.theme.idle_border,
            width=2,
        )

        # 2. Animated Border Beam (runs only on hover)
        self._perimeter_path = generate_perimeter_path(w, h, r)
        self.beam_body_id = self.canvas.create_line(
            0,
            0,
            0,
            0,
            fill=self.theme.beam_body,
            width=3.5,
            capstyle="round",
            joinstyle="round",
        )
        self.beam_head_id = self.canvas.create_line(
            0,
            0,
            0,
            0,
            fill=self.theme.beam_head,
            width=4.5,
            capstyle="round",
        )

        # 3. Content items: Alert indicator icon + PLUS-ULTRA text
        self._bell_icon = get_icon("fallback", 18)
        self.bell_id = self.canvas.create_image(
            52,
            h // 2,
            image=self._bell_icon,
            state="hidden",
        )

        self.idle_text_id = self.canvas.create_text(
            w // 2,
            h // 2,
            text="PLUS-ULTRA",
            fill=self.theme.idle_text,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, self.theme.font_size, "bold"),
        )

        # Apply initial XWayland shape mask to eliminate square corners
        self.root.update_idletasks()
        apply_window_shape_mask(self.root.winfo_id(), w, h, r)

    def _bind_events(self) -> None:
        """Bind mouse drag, hover, click, and keyboard events."""
        self.canvas.bind("<Enter>", self._on_enter)
        self.canvas.bind("<Leave>", self._on_leave)
        self.canvas.bind("<Button-1>", self._on_button_press)
        self.canvas.bind("<B1-Motion>", self._on_motion)
        self.canvas.bind("<ButtonRelease-1>", self._on_button_release)
        self.root.bind("<Escape>", lambda _e: self.close())

    def _on_enter(self, _event: tk.Event[Any]) -> None:
        """Handle mouse hover entrance: start trailing beam."""
        self._is_hovered = True
        self.canvas.itemconfig(self.idle_text_id, fill=self.theme.hover_text)
        if self.glow_enabled:
            self._start_beam()

    def _on_leave(self, _event: tk.Event[Any]) -> None:
        """Handle mouse hover exit: stop trailing beam and restore border."""
        self._is_hovered = False
        self.canvas.itemconfig(self.idle_text_id, fill=self.theme.idle_text)
        self._stop_beam()
        idle_border = (
            self.theme.hover_border
            if self._active_toggle
            else self.theme.idle_border
        )
        self.canvas.itemconfig(
            self.capsule_id,
            fill=self.theme.idle_fill,
            outline=idle_border,
        )

    def _start_beam(self) -> None:
        """Start the orbiting beam animation loop on hover."""
        if self._beam_timer_id is None and not self._closed:
            self._tick_beam()

    def _stop_beam(self) -> None:
        """Stop orbiting beam and clear line coordinates when not hovered."""
        if self._beam_timer_id is not None:
            with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
                self.root.after_cancel(self._beam_timer_id)
            self._beam_timer_id = None

        # Hide beam segments
        self.canvas.coords(self.beam_body_id, 0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.canvas.coords(self.beam_head_id, 0, 0, 0, 0)

    def _tick_beam(self) -> None:
        """Advance Animated Border Beam along perimeter path (~40 FPS)."""
        if self._closed or not self._is_hovered:
            return

        total_pts = len(self._perimeter_path)
        if total_pts > 0 and self.glow_enabled:
            self._beam_idx = (self._beam_idx + 2) % total_pts

            # Body coordinates (trailing tail)
            body_coords: list[float] = []
            for k in range(self._beam_length):
                idx = (self._beam_idx - k) % total_pts
                body_coords.extend(self._perimeter_path[idx])
            self.canvas.coords(self.beam_body_id, *body_coords)

            # Head coordinates (bright leading tip)
            head_coords: list[float] = []
            for k in range(6):
                idx = (self._beam_idx - k) % total_pts
                head_coords.extend(self._perimeter_path[idx])
            self.canvas.coords(self.beam_head_id, *head_coords)

        self._beam_timer_id = self.root.after(25, self._tick_beam)

    def _on_button_press(self, event: tk.Event[Any]) -> None:
        """Record initial mouse coordinates on click."""
        self._drag_start_x = event.x_root - self.root.winfo_x()
        self._drag_start_y = event.y_root - self.root.winfo_y()
        self._dragged = False

    def _on_motion(self, event: tk.Event[Any]) -> None:
        """Track mouse dragging and update window coordinates."""
        dx = abs(event.x_root - (self._drag_start_x + self.root.winfo_x()))
        dy = abs(event.y_root - (self._drag_start_y + self.root.winfo_y()))
        if dx > 2 or dy > 2:
            self._dragged = True

        new_x = event.x_root - self._drag_start_x
        new_y = event.y_root - self._drag_start_y

        self.root.geometry(f"{self.width}x{self.height}+{new_x}+{new_y}")

        # If popup is visible, keep it anchored below main capsule
        if self._popup_window and self._popup_window.winfo_exists():
            pop_w = self._popup_window.winfo_width()
            pop_x = new_x + (self.width - pop_w) // 2
            pop_y = new_y + self.height + 8
            self._popup_window.geometry(f"+{pop_x}+{pop_y}")

    def _on_button_release(self, _event: tk.Event[Any]) -> None:
        """Handle click when mouse was released without dragging."""
        if not self._dragged:
            self._on_click()
        elif self.on_position_change:
            self.on_position_change(self.root.winfo_x(), self.root.winfo_y())

    def _on_click(self) -> None:
        """Toggle active state and trigger click callback."""
        self._active_toggle = not self._active_toggle
        logger.info("Capsule clicked (active toggle: %s)", self._active_toggle)
        if self.on_click_callback:
            self.on_click_callback()

    # --- Notification & Popup Banner Methods ---

    def post_notification(
        self,
        title: str,
        message: str = "",
        icon: str = "",
        notification_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> str:
        """Add notification: shows 3s popup below and displays orange bell."""
        item = self.notifications.push(
            title=title,
            message=message,
            icon=icon,
            notification_id=notification_id,
        )
        self.root.after_idle(lambda: self._handle_new_notification(item))
        return item.id

    def mark_all_read(self) -> None:
        """Mark all notifications read and hide orange bell indicator."""
        self.notifications.mark_all_read()
        self.root.after_idle(self._update_bell_state)

    def mark_read(self, notification_id: str) -> None:
        """Mark single notification read and update bell indicator."""
        self.notifications.mark_read(notification_id)
        self.root.after_idle(self._update_bell_state)

    def clear_notifications(self) -> None:
        """Clear all notifications and hide bell indicator."""
        self.notifications.clear()
        self.root.after_idle(self._update_bell_state)

    def _handle_new_notification(self, item: CapsuleNotification) -> None:
        """Display orange bell in main capsule and spawn 3-second popup pill."""
        if self._closed:
            return

        self._update_bell_state()
        self._spawn_popup_pill(item)

    def _update_capsule_geometry(self) -> None:
        """Dynamically adjust capsule width based on unread notifications."""
        if self._closed:
            return

        target_w = self.geometry.width
        if self.notifications.has_unread:
            target_w = self.geometry.width + 48

        if target_w != self.width:
            old_w = self.width
            self.width = target_w
            cur_x = self.root.winfo_x()
            cur_y = self.root.winfo_y()
            new_x = cur_x - (target_w - old_w) // 2
            self.root.geometry(f"{self.width}x{self.height}+{new_x}+{cur_y}")
            self.canvas.config(width=self.width)

            points = generate_capsule_points(
                self.width, self.height, self.radius
            )
            self.canvas.coords(self.capsule_id, *points)
            self._perimeter_path = generate_perimeter_path(
                self.width, self.height, self.radius
            )

            self.root.update_idletasks()
            apply_window_shape_mask(
                self.root.winfo_id(), self.width, self.height, self.radius
            )

    def _update_bell_state(self) -> None:
        """Show orange bell if unread notifications exist, else center text."""
        if self._closed:
            return

        self._update_capsule_geometry()

        w = self.width
        h = self.height

        if self.notifications.has_unread:
            # Orange bell visible on left; text offset right
            self.canvas.itemconfig(self.bell_id, state="normal")
            self.canvas.coords(self.bell_id, 48, h // 2)
            self.canvas.coords(self.idle_text_id, (w // 2) + 12, h // 2)
        else:
            # All read: bell hidden, text perfectly centered
            self.canvas.itemconfig(self.bell_id, state="hidden")
            self.canvas.coords(self.idle_text_id, w // 2, h // 2)

    def _spawn_popup_pill(self, item: CapsuleNotification) -> None:
        """Display 3s popup capsule directly below main capsule."""
        if self._closed:
            return

        # Cancel any pending dismiss timer for existing popup
        if self._popup_dismiss_timer_id is not None:
            with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
                self.root.after_cancel(self._popup_dismiss_timer_id)
            self._popup_dismiss_timer_id = None

        pop_w = max(320, self.notifications.compute_popup_width(item))
        pop_h = 52
        pop_r = 26

        # Position below main capsule
        main_x = self.root.winfo_x()
        main_y = self.root.winfo_y()
        pop_x = main_x + (self.width - pop_w) // 2
        pop_y = main_y + self.height + 10

        # Create or update popup window
        if not self._popup_window or not self._popup_window.winfo_exists():
            popup = tk.Toplevel(self.root)
            popup.overrideredirect(True)
            if self.always_on_top:
                popup.attributes("-topmost", True)
            popup.configure(bg=self.theme.window_bg)
            self._popup_window = popup
        else:
            popup = self._popup_window

        popup.geometry(f"{pop_w}x{pop_h}+{pop_x}+{pop_y}")

        # Clear canvas
        for child in popup.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()

        canvas = tk.Canvas(
            popup,
            width=pop_w,
            height=pop_h,
            bg=self.theme.window_bg,
            highlightthickness=0,
        )
        canvas.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)
