        # Capsule contour
        points = generate_capsule_points(pop_w, pop_h, pop_r)
        canvas.create_polygon(
            points,
            fill=self.theme.popup_bg,
            outline=self.theme.popup_border,
            width=2.0,
        )

        # Content text (Icon + preview text)
        preview_text = self.notifications.format_popup_text(item)
        content_str = (
            f"{item.icon}  {preview_text}" if item.icon else preview_text
        )
        canvas.create_text(
            pop_w // 2,
            pop_h // 2,
            text=content_str,
            fill=self.theme.popup_text,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
        )

        # Apply shape mask to popup
        popup.update_idletasks()
        apply_window_shape_mask(popup.winfo_id(), pop_w, pop_h, pop_r)

        # Auto-dismiss strictly after 3000ms (3.0 seconds)
        self._popup_dismiss_timer_id = self.root.after(
            3000, self._dismiss_popup_pill
        )

    def _dismiss_popup_pill(self) -> None:
        """Dismiss the 3-second popup pill window."""
        self._popup_dismiss_timer_id = None
        if self._popup_window and self._popup_window.winfo_exists():
            with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
                self._popup_window.destroy()
        self._popup_window = None

    def close(self) -> None:
        """Destroy window and popup cleanly."""
        logger.info("Closing floating widget window.")
        self._closed = True
        self._dismiss_popup_pill()

        if self._beam_timer_id is not None:
            with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
                self.root.after_cancel(self._beam_timer_id)

        if self.on_position_change:
            with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
                self.on_position_change(
                    self.root.winfo_x(), self.root.winfo_y()
                )
        self.root.destroy()

    def run(self) -> None:
        """Start the Tkinter event loop."""
        self.root.mainloop()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/geometry.py ==========
"""Capsule geometry and perimeter path calculation functions."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math


def generate_capsule_points(
    w: int, h: int, r: int, steps: int = 24
) -> list[float]:
    """Generate exact coordinates for a horizontal capsule polygon."""
    points: list[float] = []

    # 1. Top straight edge: left to right
    points.extend([float(r), 1.0, float(w - r), 1.0])

    # 2. Right semicircle: -pi/2 to pi/2
    cx, cy = float(w - r), float(h) / 2.0
    for i in range(steps + 1):
        theta = -math.pi / 2.0 + (math.pi * i / steps)
        points.extend(
            [cx + (r - 1) * math.cos(theta), cy + (r - 1) * math.sin(theta)]
        )

    # 3. Bottom straight edge: right to left
    points.extend([float(w - r), float(h - 1), float(r), float(h - 1)])

    # 4. Left semicircle: pi/2 to 3*pi/2
    cx, cy = float(r), float(h) / 2.0
    for i in range(steps + 1):
        theta = math.pi / 2.0 + (math.pi * i / steps)
        points.extend(
            [cx + (r - 1) * math.cos(theta), cy + (r - 1) * math.sin(theta)]
        )

    return points


def generate_perimeter_path(
    w: int, h: int, r: int, num_arc_points: int = 24
) -> list[tuple[float, float]]:
    """Generate ordered 2D perimeter points for the animated border beam."""
    path: list[tuple[float, float]] = []

    # 1. Top edge: left to right (step by 2px)
    for x in range(r, w - r + 1, 2):
        path.append((float(x), 1.5))

    # 2. Right semicircle: -pi/2 to pi/2
    cx, cy = float(w - r), float(h) / 2.0
    for i in range(1, num_arc_points + 1):
        theta = -math.pi / 2.0 + (math.pi * i / num_arc_points)
        path.append(
            (cx + (r - 1.5) * math.cos(theta), cy + (r - 1.5) * math.sin(theta))
        )

    # 3. Bottom edge: right to left (step by 2px)
    for x in range(w - r, r - 1, -2):
        path.append((float(x), float(h - 1.5)))

    # 4. Left semicircle: pi/2 to 3*pi/2
    cx, cy = float(r), float(h) / 2.0
    for i in range(1, num_arc_points + 1):
        theta = math.pi / 2.0 + (math.pi * i / num_arc_points)
        path.append(
            (cx + (r - 1.5) * math.cos(theta), cy + (r - 1.5) * math.sin(theta))
        )

    return path


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/icons.py ==========
"""Icon loader and image manager for PLUS-ULTRA.

Loads icons from resources/icons/, resources/base/, resources/folders/,
and resources/tabs/, falling back to fallback.png. Uses pure Python
standard-library Tkinter PhotoImage scaling with zero external runtime
dependencies.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
from pathlib import Path

# In-memory image cache keyed by (interpreter_id, icon_name, size)
_ICON_CACHE: dict[tuple[int, str, int], tk.PhotoImage] = {}


def _get_resource_dirs() -> list[Path]:
    """Return prioritized list of resource directories to search for icons."""
    base_pkg = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    res_dir = base_pkg / "resources"
    return [
        res_dir / "icons",
        res_dir / "base",
        res_dir / "folders",
        res_dir / "tabs",
    ]


def find_icon_path(icon_name: str) -> Path | None:
    """Resolve an icon file path by searching resource subdirectories."""
    dirs = _get_resource_dirs()
    candidates = [
        f"{icon_name}.png",
        f"{icon_name}.gif",
        f"{icon_name}.ppm",
        f"{icon_name}.svg",
    ]

    for d in dirs:
        if not d.exists():
            continue
        for candidate in candidates:
            cand_path = d / candidate
            if cand_path.is_file():
                return cand_path

    # Fallback path
    fallback = dirs[0] / "fallback.png"
    if fallback.is_file():
        return fallback

    return None


def get_icon(
    icon_name: str = "fallback",
    size: int = 24,
    master: tk.Misc | None = None,
) -> tk.PhotoImage | None:
    """Retrieve and scale a cached Tkinter PhotoImage for the specified icon.

    Args:
        icon_name: Name of the icon asset (without extension).
        size: Target pixel bounding box dimension.
        master: Optional Tk widget representing the active interpreter root.

    Returns:
        A cached PhotoImage scaled via subsample, or None if Tk is uninitialized
        or no image asset was found.
    """
    try:
        active_root = (
            master.winfo_toplevel() if master is not None else tk._default_root
        )
        if active_root is None or not active_root.winfo_exists():
            return None
    except Exception:
        return None

    root_id = id(active_root)
    cache_key = (root_id, icon_name, size)
    if cache_key in _ICON_CACHE:
        cached = _ICON_CACHE[cache_key]
        try:
            active_root.tk.call("image", "type", str(cached))
            return cached
        except Exception:
            _ICON_CACHE.pop(cache_key, None)

    path = find_icon_path(icon_name)
    if not path or path.suffix.lower() == ".svg":
        if path and path.suffix.lower() == ".svg":
            path = find_icon_path("fallback")
            if not path or path.suffix.lower() == ".svg":
                return None
        else:
            return None

    try:
        img = tk.PhotoImage(file=str(path), master=active_root)
        orig_w = img.width()
        orig_h = img.height()

        if orig_w > size and orig_h > size and size > 0:
            scale_x = max(1, orig_w // size)
            scale_y = max(1, orig_h // size)
            scaled = img.subsample(scale_x, scale_y)
            _ICON_CACHE[cache_key] = scaled
            return scaled

        _ICON_CACHE[cache_key] = img
        return img
    except Exception:
        return None


def clear_icon_cache() -> None:
    """Flush the in-memory icon cache."""
    _ICON_CACHE.clear()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/__init__.py ==========
"""User Interface layer for PLUS-ULTRA."""


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/panel.py ==========
"""Main desktop workspace dashboard panel for PLUS-ULTRA.

Coordinates sidebar navigation, tab views, modal dialogs, and notification
streams in a responsive, modern dark aesthetic.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable
from tkinter import ttk
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import Task
from plus_ultra.notifications import CapsuleNotificationQueue
from plus_ultra.services import (
    CategoryService,
    CodeService,
    CourseService,
    IdeaDoubtService,
    OpportunityService,
    QuickLinkService,
    ReminderService,
    TaskService,
)
from plus_ultra.ui.components import (
    NotificationsPanel,
    ScrollableFrame,
    Sidebar,
)
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs import (
    CategoryDialog,
    CodeDialog,
    CourseDialog,
    IdeaDialog,
    OpportunityDialog,
    QuickLinkDialog,
    TaskDialog,
)
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme
from plus_ultra.ui.views import (
    CodeView,
    CoursesView,
    DSAView,
    IdeasView,
    OpportunitiesView,
    QuickLinksView,
    SettingsView,
    TasksView,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

__all__ = [
    "CategoryDialog",
    "CodeDialog",
    "CourseDialog",
    "DSAView",
    "DashboardPanel",
    "IdeaDialog",
    "OpportunityDialog",
    "QuickLinkDialog",
    "ScrollableFrame",
    "Sidebar",
    "TaskDialog",
    "TreeCompatibilityFacade",
]


class TreeCompatibilityFacade:
    """Facade for backward compatibility with tests expecting panel.tree."""

    def __init__(self, panel: DashboardPanel) -> None:
        self.panel = panel

    def get_children(self) -> list[str]:
        """Return task IDs of currently filtered and rendered tasks."""
        return [t.id for t in self.panel._filtered_tasks]


class DashboardPanel(tk.Toplevel):
    """Main desktop workspace panel with modern card UI and full CRUD."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        parent: tk.Tk,
        task_service: TaskService,
        category_service: CategoryService,
        notification_queue: CapsuleNotificationQueue,
        reminder_service: ReminderService | None = None,
        code_service: CodeService | None = None,
        course_service: CourseService | None = None,
        idea_doubt_service: IdeaDoubtService | None = None,
        opportunity_service: OpportunityService | None = None,
        quick_link_service: QuickLinkService | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
        on_notifications_cleared: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
        on_open: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
        on_close: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.task_service = task_service
        self.category_service = category_service
        self.notification_queue = notification_queue
        self.reminder_service = reminder_service
        self.code_service = code_service
        self.course_service = course_service
        self.idea_doubt_service = idea_doubt_service
        self.opportunity_service = opportunity_service
        self.quick_link_service = quick_link_service
        self.theme = theme
        self.on_notifications_cleared = on_notifications_cleared
        self.on_open = on_open
        self.on_close = on_close

        self.active_tab: str = "Tasks"
        self.tab_containers: dict[str, tk.Widget] = {}
        self.tree = TreeCompatibilityFacade(self)

        self.title("PLUS-ULTRA Workspace")
        self.geometry("1380x840")
        self.minsize(1000, 640)
        self.configure(bg="#0f1218")
        self.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self.hide)
        self.bind("<Escape>", lambda _e: self.hide())

        self._setup_styles()
        self._build_layout()
        self._refresh_tasks()
        self.withdraw()

    def _setup_styles(self) -> None:
        style = ttk.Style()
        style.theme_use("clam")
        style.configure(
            "TCombobox",
            fieldbackground="#1c212c",
            background="#2b3448",
            foreground="#f8fafc",
            arrowcolor="#00e5ff",
            relief="flat",
            borderwidth=0,
        )

    def _build_layout(self) -> None:
        self.main_container = tk.Frame(self, bg="#0f1218")
        self.main_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        self.paned_window = tk.PanedWindow(
            self.main_container,
            orient="horizontal",
            bg="#2b3448",
            sashrelief="ridge",
            sashwidth=6,
            bd=0,
        )
        self.paned_window.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        # 1. Left Sidebar (Adjustable with minsize=180)
        self.sidebar_col = tk.Frame(self.paned_window, bg="#12151c", width=280)
        self.sidebar = Sidebar(
            self.sidebar_col,
            self.theme,
            on_switch_tab=self._switch_tab,
            on_close=self.hide,
        )
        self.paned_window.add(self.sidebar_col, minsize=180, width=280)

        # 2. Center Workspace Area (Adjustable with minsize=400)
        self.center_col = tk.Frame(self.paned_window, bg="#161922")
        self._build_all_tab_containers()
        self.paned_window.add(self.center_col, minsize=400)

        # 3. Right Notification Stream (Adjustable with minsize=100)
        self.notif_col = tk.Frame(self.paned_window, bg="#12151c", width=280)
        self._build_notifications_panel()
        self.paned_window.add(self.notif_col, minsize=100, width=280)

    @property
    def tab_buttons(self) -> dict[str, tk.Button]:
        return self.sidebar.tab_buttons

    @property
    def settings_btn(self) -> tk.Button:
        return self.sidebar.settings_btn

    def _build_all_tab_containers(self) -> None:
        self.placeholder_container = tk.Frame(self.center_col, bg="#161922")

        # 1. Tasks
        self.tasks_container = tk.Frame(self.center_col, bg="#161922")
        self.tasks_view = TasksView(
            self.tasks_container,
            self,
            self.task_service,
            self.category_service,
            self.reminder_service,
            self.theme,
        )
        self.tab_containers["Tasks"] = self.tasks_container

        # 2. Code
        self.code_container = tk.Frame(self.center_col, bg="#161922")
        self.code_view = CodeView(
            self.code_container, self, self.code_service, self.theme
        )
        self.tab_containers["Code"] = self.code_container

        # 3. DSA Problem Tracker
        self.dsa_container = tk.Frame(self.center_col, bg="#161922")
        self.dsa_view = DSAView(
            self.dsa_container, self, self.code_service, self.theme
        )
        self.tab_containers["DSA"] = self.dsa_container

        # 4. Courses
        self.courses_container = tk.Frame(self.center_col, bg="#161922")
        self.courses_view = CoursesView(
            self.courses_container, self, self.course_service, self.theme
        )
        self.tab_containers["Courses"] = self.courses_container

        # 5. Ideas-Doubt
        self.ideas_container = tk.Frame(self.center_col, bg="#161922")
        self.ideas_view = IdeasView(
            self.ideas_container, self, self.idea_doubt_service, self.theme
        )
        self.tab_containers["Ideas-Doubt"] = self.ideas_container

        # 6. Opportunities
        self.opps_container = tk.Frame(self.center_col, bg="#161922")
        self.opps_view = OpportunitiesView(
            self.opps_container, self, self.opportunity_service, self.theme
        )
        self.tab_containers["Opportunities"] = self.opps_container

        # 7. Quick Links
        self.links_container = tk.Frame(self.center_col, bg="#161922")
        self.links_view = QuickLinksView(
            self.links_container, self, self.quick_link_service, self.theme
        )
        self.tab_containers["Quick Links"] = self.links_container

        # 8. Settings
        self.settings_container = tk.Frame(self.center_col, bg="#161922")
        self.settings_view = SettingsView(
            self.settings_container,
            self,
            self.theme,
            on_reseed=self._refresh_tasks,
        )
        self.tab_containers["Settings"] = self.settings_container

        self.tasks_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    def _build_notifications_panel(self) -> None:
        self.notif_panel = NotificationsPanel(
            self.notif_col,
            self.notification_queue,
            self.theme,
            self.on_notifications_cleared,
            code_service=self.code_service,
            parent_window=self,
        )

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Delegated View Properties & Methods (backward compatibility)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    @property
    def search_entry(self) -> tk.Entry:
        return self.tasks_view.search_entry

    @property
    def _filtered_tasks(self) -> list[Task]:
        return self.tasks_view.filtered_tasks

    @_filtered_tasks.setter
    def _filtered_tasks(self, value: list[Task]) -> None:
        self.tasks_view.filtered_tasks = value

    def _set_status_filter(self, status: str) -> None:
        self.tasks_view.set_status_filter(status)

    def _refresh_tasks(self) -> None:
        self.tasks_view.refresh()

    def _toggle_selected_status(self, task_id: str) -> None:
        self.tasks_view.toggle_selected_status(task_id)

    def _delete_task(self, task_id: str) -> None:
        self.tasks_view.delete_task(task_id)

    def _open_create_task(self) -> None:
        self.tasks_view.open_create_task()

    def _open_edit_task(self, task: Task) -> None:
        self.tasks_view.open_edit_task(task)

    def _open_create_category(self) -> None:
        self.tasks_view.open_create_category()

    def _set_code_filter(self, cat: str) -> None:
        self.code_view.set_code_filter(cat)

    def _refresh_code(self) -> None:
        self.code_view.refresh()

    def _toggle_dsa_done(self, item_id: str) -> None:
        self.dsa_view.toggle_dsa_done(item_id)

    def _refresh_dsa(self) -> None:
        self.dsa_view.refresh()

    def _copy_to_clipboard(self, text: str, btn: tk.Button) -> None:
        self.code_view.copy_to_clipboard(text, btn)

    def _set_course_filter(self, platform: str) -> None:
        self.courses_view.set_course_filter(platform)

    def _refresh_courses(self) -> None:
        self.courses_view.refresh()

    def _inc_course(self, course_id: str) -> None:
        self.courses_view.inc_course(course_id)

    def _set_idea_filter(self, kind: str) -> None:
        self.ideas_view.set_idea_filter(kind)

    def _refresh_ideas(self) -> None:
        self.ideas_view.refresh()

    def _advance_idea(self, item_id: str) -> None:
        self.ideas_view.advance_idea(item_id)

    def _set_opp_filter(self, kind: str) -> None:
        self.opps_view.set_opp_filter(kind)

    def _refresh_opportunities(self) -> None:
        self.opps_view.refresh()

    def _advance_opp(self, opp_id: str) -> None:
        self.opps_view.advance_opp(opp_id)

    def _set_link_filter(self, category: str) -> None:
        self.links_view.set_link_filter(category)

    def _refresh_quick_links(self) -> None:
        self.links_view.refresh()

    def _launch_quick_link(self, link: Any) -> None:
        self.links_view.launch_quick_link(link)

    def _refresh_settings(self) -> None:
        self.settings_view.refresh()

    @property
    def notif_listbox(self) -> tk.Listbox:
        return self.notif_panel.notif_listbox

    def _refresh_notifications(self) -> None:
        self.notif_panel.refresh()

    def _mark_all_notifications_read(self) -> None:
        self.notif_panel.mark_all_read()

    def _clear_all_notifications(self) -> None:
        self.notif_panel.clear_all()

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Navigation & Tab Switching
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _switch_tab(self, tab_name: str) -> None:
        self.active_tab = tab_name
        self._highlight_active_tab()

        # Hide all containers
        self.tasks_container.pack_forget()
        self.placeholder_container.pack_forget()
        for container in self.tab_containers.values():
            container.pack_forget()

        if tab_name == "Tasks":
            self.tasks_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
            self._refresh_tasks()
        elif tab_name == "Code":
            if self.code_service:
                self.code_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
                self._refresh_code()
            else:
                self.placeholder_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        elif tab_name == "DSA":
            if self.code_service:
                self.dsa_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
                self._refresh_dsa()
            else:
                self.placeholder_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        elif tab_name == "Courses":
            if self.course_service:
                self.courses_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
                self._refresh_courses()
            else:
                self.placeholder_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        elif tab_name == "Ideas-Doubt":
            if self.idea_doubt_service:
                self.ideas_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
                self._refresh_ideas()
            else:
                self.placeholder_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        elif tab_name == "Opportunities":
            if self.opportunity_service:
                self.opps_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
                self._refresh_opportunities()
            else:
                self.placeholder_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        elif tab_name == "Quick Links":
            if self.quick_link_service:
                self.links_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
                self._refresh_quick_links()
            else:
                self.placeholder_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        elif tab_name == "Settings":
            self.settings_container.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
            self._refresh_settings()

    def _highlight_active_tab(self) -> None:
        self.sidebar.highlight_tab(self.active_tab)

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Window Visibility & Management
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def show(self) -> None:
        """Show workspace dashboard and notify pill to disappear."""
        self.deiconify()
        self.lift()
        self.focus_force()
        if self.on_open:
            self.on_open()
        self._refresh_tasks()
        self._refresh_notifications()

    def hide(self) -> None:
        """Hide workspace dashboard and notify pill to reappear."""
        self.withdraw()
        if self.on_close:
            self.on_close()

    def toggle(self) -> None:
        """Toggle workspace visibility."""
        if self.state() == "normal":
            self.hide()
        else:
            self.show()

    def destroy(self) -> None:
        """Cleanly destroy window and restore pill."""
        if self.on_close:
            self.on_close()
        super().destroy()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/theme.py ==========
"""Theme definitions and visual styling tokens for PLUS-ULTRA.

Encapsulates color palettes, capsule dimensions, typography, and glow
animation properties for the floating desktop widget.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import NamedTuple


class CapsuleGeometry(NamedTuple):
    """Dimensions for the top-center floating capsule."""

    min_width: int = 320
    width: int = 320
    max_width: int = 880
    height: int = 72
    radius: int = 36
    top_offset: int = 16


class CapsuleTheme(NamedTuple):
    """Color tokens for the floating capsule."""

    window_bg: str = "#12151c"
    idle_fill: str = "#1a1f2c"
    idle_border: str = "#2b3448"
    idle_text: str = "#cbd5e1"
    hover_fill: str = "#1a1f2c"  # Clean dark fill (no static glow)
    hover_border: str = "#00e5ff"
    hover_text: str = "#ffffff"
    beam_head: str = "#ffffff"  # Luminous tip of running beam
    beam_body: str = "#00e5ff"  # Core electric cyan running beam
    beam_tail: str = "#0284c7"  # Smooth gradient tail
    bell_orange: str = "#ff9800"  # Unread notification bell indicator
    notification_bg: str = "#182030"
    badge_bg: str = "#00e5ff"
    badge_fg: str = "#0a0e17"
    dismiss_fg: str = "#94a3b8"
    dismiss_hover: str = "#f43f5e"
    popup_bg: str = "#141a24"
    popup_border: str = "#2b3448"
    popup_text: str = "#f8fafc"
    font_family: str = "helvetica"
    font_size: int = 13


GEOMETRY = CapsuleGeometry()
THEME = CapsuleTheme()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/views/code_view.py ==========
"""Code snippet and workspace view component."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
import webbrowser
from collections.abc import Callable
from tkinter import messagebox
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import CodeItem
from plus_ultra.services import CodeService
from plus_ultra.ui.components.scrollable import ScrollableFrame
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs import CodeDialog
from plus_ultra.ui.icons import get_icon
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class CodeView:
    """Card-based code snippet workspace view with full CRUD."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        container: tk.Widget,
        parent_window: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        code_service: CodeService | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
    ) -> None:
        self.container = container
        self.parent_window = parent_window
        self.code_service = code_service
        self.theme = theme

        self.active_code_filter: str = "All"
        self.code_filter_btns: dict[str, tk.Button] = {}

        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        head_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28, pady=24)
        head_bar.pack(fill="x")

        icon = get_icon("fallback", 24)
        title_lbl = tk.Label(
            head_bar,
            text=" Code c-p",
            bg="#161922",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 16, "bold"),
            image=icon,
            compound="left",
        )
        title_lbl.image = icon  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        title_lbl.pack(side="left")

        tk.Button(
            head_bar,
            text="+ Add Code Item",
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self.open_create_code,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=18,
            pady=8,
        ).pack(side="right")

        # Filter Sub-Tabs: All, Snippets, CLI Commands, Syntax Reference
        filter_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28)
        filter_bar.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 16))

        for cat in [
            "All",
            "Snippets",
            "CLI Commands",
            "Syntax Reference",
        ]:
            btn = tk.Button(
                filter_bar,
                text=cat,
                bg="#1e2433" if cat == "All" else "#12151c",
                fg="#00e5ff" if cat == "All" else "#94a3b8",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                highlightthickness=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=14,
                pady=6,
                command=lambda c=cat: self.set_code_filter(c),
            )
            btn.pack(side="left", padx=3)
            self.code_filter_btns[cat] = btn

        self.code_scroll = ScrollableFrame(self.container, bg="#161922")
        self.code_scroll.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=28, pady=(0, 20))

    def set_code_filter(self, cat: str) -> None:
        """Update active code category filter and reload cards."""
        self.active_code_filter = cat
        for c, btn in self.code_filter_btns.items():
            if c == cat:
                btn.configure(bg="#1e2433", fg="#00e5ff")
            else:
                btn.configure(bg="#12151c", fg="#94a3b8")
        self.refresh()

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        """Query code items matching category and render interactive cards."""
        parent = self.code_scroll.interior
        for child in parent.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()

        if not self.code_service:
            return

        cat = (
            None
            if self.active_code_filter == "All"
            else self.active_code_filter
        )
        items = self.code_service.list_items(category=cat)
        # Exclude DSA items from Code Lab
        if self.active_code_filter == "All":
            items = [i for i in items if i.category != "DSA Problem Tracker"]

        if not items:
            empty_box = tk.Frame(parent, bg="#161922", pady=48)
            empty_box.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
            tk.Label(
                empty_box,
                text="No code items found in this category.",
                bg="#161922",
                fg="#64748b",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 12),
            ).pack()
            return

        for item in items:
            self._render_card(parent, item)

    def _render_card(self, parent: tk.Widget, item: CodeItem) -> None:
        card = tk.Frame(
            parent,
            bg="#1c212c",
            padx=24,
            pady=18,
            highlightbackground="#2b3448",
            highlightthickness=1,
        )
        card.pack(fill="x", pady=8)

        top = tk.Frame(card, bg="#1c212c")
        top.pack(fill="x")

        tk.Label(
            top,
            text=item.title,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 14, "bold"),
            anchor="w",
        ).pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)

        acts = tk.Frame(top, bg="#1c212c")
        acts.pack(side="right")

        if item.content:
            copy_btn = tk.Button(
                acts,
                text="Copy",
                bg="#2b3448",
                fg="#00e5ff",
                activebackground="#3b475e",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=12,
                pady=5,
            )
            copy_btn.configure(
                command=self._make_copy_callback(item.content, copy_btn)
            )
            copy_btn.pack(side="left", padx=4)

        if item.url:
            tk.Button(
                acts,
                text="Open Link",
                bg="#1e2433",
                fg="#cbd5e1",
                activebackground="#2b3448",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=12,
                pady=5,
                command=lambda u=item.url: webbrowser.open(u),
            ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        tk.Button(
            acts,
            text="Edit",
            bg="#2b3448",
            fg="#e2e8f0",
            activebackground="#3b475e",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda i=item: self.open_edit_code(i),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        tk.Button(
            acts,
            text="Delete",
            bg="#3f1d24",
            fg="#fca5a5",
            activebackground="#5c2630",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda iid=item.id: self.delete_code_item(iid),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        # Metadata Chips
        meta_box = tk.Frame(card, bg="#1c212c")
        meta_box.pack(fill="x", pady=(10, 0))

        tk.Label(
            meta_box,
            text=f"[{item.category}]",
            bg="#083344",
            fg="#22d3ee",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 9, "bold"),
            padx=8,
            pady=2,
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))

        if item.language:
            tk.Label(
                meta_box,
                text=item.language,
                bg="#1e2433",
                fg="#94a3b8",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 9),
                padx=8,
                pady=2,
            ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        # Code Preview Block
        if item.content:
            code_box = tk.Frame(card, bg="#12151c", padx=14, pady=12)
            code_box.pack(fill="x", pady=(10, 0))
            display_code = item.content
            if len(display_code.splitlines()) > 5:
                lines = display_code.splitlines()[:5]
                display_code = "\n".join(lines) + "\n... [truncated]"
            tk.Label(
                code_box,
                text=display_code,
                bg="#12151c",
                fg="#cbd5e1",
                font=("courier", 10),
                anchor="w",
                justify="left",
            ).pack(fill="x")

    def _make_copy_callback(
        self, text: str, btn: tk.Button
    ) -> Callable[[], None]:
        return lambda: self.copy_to_clipboard(text, btn)

    def copy_to_clipboard(self, text: str, btn: tk.Button) -> None:
        """Copy snippet text to OS clipboard and display transient feedback."""
        self.parent_window.clipboard_clear()
        self.parent_window.clipboard_append(text)
        orig_text = btn.cget("text")
        btn.configure(text="Copied!", fg="#10b981")
        self.parent_window.after(
            1500, lambda: btn.configure(text=orig_text, fg="#00e5ff")
        )

    def toggle_dsa_done(self, item_id: str) -> None:
        """Toggle DSA problem solved status."""
        if self.code_service:
            self.code_service.toggle_done(item_id)
            self.refresh()

    def delete_code_item(self, item_id: str) -> None:
        """Prompt confirmation and soft-delete code item."""
        if (
            messagebox.askyesno(
                "Delete", "Delete this code item?", parent=self.parent_window
            )
            and self.code_service
        ):
            self.code_service.soft_delete_item(item_id)
            self.refresh()

    def open_create_code(self) -> None:
        """Open CodeDialog to add a new code snippet."""

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            if self.code_service:
                self.code_service.create_item(
                    category=data["category"],
                    title=data["title"],
                    content=data["content"],
                    language=data["language"],
                    difficulty=data["difficulty"],
                    url=data["url"],
                )
                self.refresh()

        CodeDialog(self.parent_window, on_save=_on_save)

    def open_edit_code(self, item: CodeItem) -> None:
        """Open CodeDialog to edit an existing code item."""

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            if self.code_service:
                self.code_service.update_code_item(
                    item_id=item.id,
                    category=data["category"],
                    title=data["title"],
                    content=data["content"],
                    language=data["language"],
                    difficulty=data["difficulty"],
                    url=data["url"],
                )
                self.refresh()

        CodeDialog(self.parent_window, item=item, on_save=_on_save)


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/views/courses_view.py ==========
"""Courses and Certifications workspace view component."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
import webbrowser
from tkinter import messagebox
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import Course
from plus_ultra.services import CourseService
from plus_ultra.ui.components.scrollable import ScrollableFrame
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs import CourseDialog
from plus_ultra.ui.icons import get_icon
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class CoursesView:
    """Card-based courses workspace view with module progress tracking."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        container: tk.Widget,
        parent_window: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        course_service: CourseService | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
    ) -> None:
        self.container = container
        self.parent_window = parent_window
        self.course_service = course_service
        self.theme = theme

        self.active_course_filter: str = "All"
        self.course_filter_btns: dict[str, tk.Button] = {}

        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        head_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28, pady=24)
        head_bar.pack(fill="x")

        icon = get_icon("fallback", 24)
        title_lbl = tk.Label(
            head_bar,
            text="Courses & Certifications",
            bg="#161922",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 16, "bold"),
            image=icon,
            compound="left",
            padx=10,
        )
        title_lbl.image = icon  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        title_lbl.pack(side="left")

        tk.Button(
            head_bar,
            text="+ Add Course",
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self.open_create_course,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=20,
            pady=8,
        ).pack(side="right")

        # Platform Filter Pills
        filter_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28)
        filter_bar.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 16))

        for plat in [
            "All",
            "University",
            "YouTube",
            "Independent",
            "Book",
        ]:
            btn = tk.Button(
                filter_bar,
                text=plat,
                bg="#1e2433" if plat == "All" else "#12151c",
                fg="#00e5ff" if plat == "All" else "#94a3b8",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                highlightthickness=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=16,
                pady=8,
                command=lambda p=plat: self.set_course_filter(p),
            )
            btn.pack(side="left", padx=4)
            self.course_filter_btns[plat] = btn

        self.courses_scroll = ScrollableFrame(self.container, bg="#161922")
        self.courses_scroll.pack(
            fill="both", expand=True, padx=28, pady=(0, 20)
        )

    def set_course_filter(self, platform: str) -> None:
        """Update active platform filter and reload cards."""
        self.active_course_filter = platform
        for p, btn in self.course_filter_btns.items():
            if p == platform:
                btn.configure(bg="#1e2433", fg="#00e5ff")
            else:
                btn.configure(bg="#12151c", fg="#94a3b8")
        self.refresh()

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        """Query courses matching platform and render interactive cards."""
        parent = self.courses_scroll.interior
        for child in parent.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()

        if not self.course_service:
            return

        plat = (
            None
            if self.active_course_filter == "All"
            else self.active_course_filter
        )
        courses = self.course_service.list_courses(platform=plat)

        if not courses:
            tk.Label(
                parent,
                text="No courses found in this category.",
                bg="#161922",
                fg="#64748b",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
                pady=40,
            ).pack()
            return

        for c in courses:
            card = tk.Frame(parent, bg="#1c212c", padx=28, pady=20)
            card.pack(fill="x", pady=10)

            top = tk.Frame(card, bg="#1c212c")
            top.pack(fill="x")

            tk.Label(
                top,
                text=f"{c.title}",
                bg="#1c212c",
                fg="#f8fafc",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 15, "bold"),
                anchor="w",
            ).pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)

            acts = tk.Frame(top, bg="#1c212c")
            acts.pack(side="right")

            tk.Button(
                acts,
                text="+1 Module",
                bg="#00e5ff",
                fg="#0a0e17",
                activebackground="#00b4d8",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=12,
                pady=4,
                command=lambda cid=c.id: self.inc_course(cid),
            ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

            if c.url:
                tk.Button(
                    acts,
                    text="Open",
                    bg="#1e2433",
                    fg="#cbd5e1",
                    relief="flat",
                    bd=0,
                    font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
                    cursor="hand2",
                    padx=12,
                    pady=4,
                    command=lambda u=c.url: webbrowser.open(u),
                ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

            tk.Button(
                acts,
                text="Edit",
                bg="#2b3448",
                fg="#e2e8f0",
                activebackground="#3b475e",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=12,
                pady=4,
                command=lambda crs=c: self.open_edit_course(crs),
            ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

            tk.Button(
                acts,
                text="Delete",
                bg="#3f1d24",
                fg="#fca5a5",
                activebackground="#5c2630",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=12,
                pady=4,
                command=lambda cid=c.id: self.delete_course(cid),
            ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

            # Platform Tag
            meta_box = tk.Frame(card, bg="#1c212c")
            meta_box.pack(fill="x", pady=(10, 0))

            tk.Label(
                meta_box,
                text=f"[{c.platform}]",
                bg="#083344",
                fg="#22d3ee",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
                padx=10,
                pady=3,
            ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))

            if c.instructor:
                tk.Label(
                    meta_box,
                    text=f"Instructor: {c.instructor}",
                    bg="#1c212c",
                    fg="#94a3b8",
                    font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
                ).pack(side="left")

            # Progress Bar & Percentage
            pct = int((c.completed_modules / max(1, c.total_modules)) * 100)
            bar_text = (
                f"Modules: {c.completed_modules} / {c.total_modules} "
                f"({pct}% complete)"
            )
            tk.Label(
                card,
                text=bar_text,
                bg="#1c212c",
                fg="#94a3b8",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
                anchor="w",
            ).pack(fill="x", pady=(10, 4))

            prog_bg = tk.Frame(card, bg="#12151c", height=10)
            prog_bg.pack(fill="x", pady=(2, 8))
            fill_frame = tk.Frame(prog_bg, bg="#00e5ff", height=10)
            fill_frame.place(relx=0, rely=0, relwidth=min(1.0, pct / 100))

            if c.notes:
                tk.Label(
                    card,
                    text=f"Note: {c.notes}",
                    bg="#1c212c",
                    fg="#94a3b8",
                    font=(self.theme.font_family, 12),
                    anchor="w",
                ).pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

    def inc_course(self, course_id: str) -> None:
        """Increment completed module count for course."""
        if self.course_service:
            self.course_service.increment_module(course_id)
            self.refresh()

    def delete_course(self, course_id: str) -> None:
        """Prompt confirmation and soft-delete course."""
        if (
            messagebox.askyesno(
                "Delete", "Delete this course?", parent=self.parent_window
            )
            and self.course_service
        ):
            self.course_service.soft_delete_course(course_id)
            self.refresh()

    def open_create_course(self) -> None:
        """Open CourseDialog to add a new course."""

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            if self.course_service:
                self.course_service.create_course(
                    title=data["title"],
                    platform=data["platform"],
                    instructor=data["instructor"],
                    total_modules=data["total_modules"],
                    completed_modules=data["completed_modules"],
                    url=data["url"],
                    notes=data["notes"],
                )
                self.refresh()

        CourseDialog(self.parent_window, on_save=_on_save)

    def open_edit_course(self, course: Course) -> None:
        """Open CourseDialog to edit an existing course."""

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            if self.course_service:
                self.course_service.update_course(
                    course_id=course.id,
                    title=data["title"],
                    platform=data["platform"],
                    instructor=data["instructor"],
                    total_modules=data["total_modules"],
                    completed_modules=data["completed_modules"],
                    url=data["url"],
                    notes=data["notes"],
                )
                self.refresh()

        CourseDialog(self.parent_window, course=course, on_save=_on_save)


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/views/dsa_view.py ==========
"""Dedicated DSA problem tracker workspace view component."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
import webbrowser
from tkinter import messagebox
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import CodeItem
from plus_ultra.services import CodeService
from plus_ultra.ui.components.scrollable import ScrollableFrame
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs import CodeDialog
from plus_ultra.ui.icons import get_icon
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class DSAView:
    """Dedicated DSA problem tracker view with filters, metrics, and CRUD."""

    CATEGORY_NAME = "DSA Problem Tracker"

    def __init__(
        self,
        container: tk.Widget,
        parent_window: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        code_service: CodeService | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
    ) -> None:
        self.container = container
        self.parent_window = parent_window
        self.code_service = code_service
        self.theme = theme

        self.active_filter: str = "All"
        self.filter_buttons: dict[str, tk.Button] = {}

        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        head_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28, pady=24)
        head_bar.pack(fill="x")

        icon = get_icon("fallback", 24)
        title_lbl = tk.Label(
            head_bar,
            text=" DSA Problem Tracker",
            bg="#161922",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 16, "bold"),
            image=icon,
            compound="left",
        )
        title_lbl.image = icon  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        title_lbl.pack(side="left")

        tk.Button(
            head_bar,
            text="+ Add Problem",
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self.open_create_problem,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=18,
            pady=8,
        ).pack(side="right")

        # Filter Sub-Tabs: All, Easy, Medium, Hard, Solved, Todo
        filter_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28)
        filter_bar.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 16))

        for opt in ["All", "Easy", "Medium", "Hard", "Solved", "Todo"]:
            btn = tk.Button(
                filter_bar,
                text=opt,
                bg="#1e2433" if opt == "All" else "#12151c",
                fg="#00e5ff" if opt == "All" else "#94a3b8",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                highlightthickness=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=14,
                pady=6,
                command=lambda o=opt: self.set_filter(o),
            )
            btn.pack(side="left", padx=3)
            self.filter_buttons[opt] = btn

        self.scroll_frame = ScrollableFrame(self.container, bg="#161922")
        self.scroll_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=28, pady=(0, 20))

    def set_filter(self, opt: str) -> None:
        """Update active difficulty/status filter and reload cards."""
        self.active_filter = opt
        for name, btn in self.filter_buttons.items():
            if name == opt:
                btn.config(bg="#1e2433", fg="#00e5ff")
            else:
                btn.config(bg="#12151c", fg="#94a3b8")
        self.refresh()

    def _matches_filter(self, item: CodeItem) -> bool:
        diff = (item.difficulty or "").lower()
        f = self.active_filter
        if f == "All":
            return True
        if f in ("Easy", "Medium", "Hard"):
            return diff == f.lower()
        if f == "Solved":
            return bool(item.is_done)
        if f == "Todo":
            return not item.is_done
        return False

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        """Reload and render 2x DSA problem cards from code service."""
        for child in self.scroll_frame.interior.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()

        if not self.code_service:
            return

        all_items = self.code_service.list_items(category=self.CATEGORY_NAME)
        filtered = [it for it in all_items if self._matches_filter(it)]

        if not filtered:
            empty_box = tk.Frame(
                self.scroll_frame.interior, bg="#161922", pady=48
            )
            empty_box.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
            tk.Label(
                empty_box,
                text="No DSA problems found for the selected filter.",
                bg="#161922",
                fg="#64748b",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 12),
            ).pack()
            return

        for item in filtered:
            self._render_problem_card(item)

    def _render_problem_card(self, item: CodeItem) -> None:
        card = tk.Frame(
            self.scroll_frame.interior,
            bg="#1c212c",
            padx=24,
            pady=20,
            highlightbackground="#2b3448",
            highlightthickness=1,
        )
        card.pack(fill="x", pady=10)

        # Header row: Status badge + Title + Difficulty badge
        top_row = tk.Frame(card, bg="#1c212c")
        top_row.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 10))

        status_text = "[Solved]" if item.is_done else "[Todo]"
        status_fg = "#10b981" if item.is_done else "#f59e0b"
        status_bg = "#0f291e" if item.is_done else "#2a2114"

        tk.Label(
            top_row,
            text=status_text,
            bg=status_bg,
            fg=status_fg,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
            padx=8,
            pady=3,
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 12))

        title_lbl = tk.Label(
            top_row,
            text=item.title,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 14, "bold"),
            anchor="w",
        )
        title_lbl.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)

        if item.difficulty:
            diff = item.difficulty.capitalize()
            diff_colors = {
                "Easy": ("#0f291e", "#10b981"),
                "Medium": ("#2a2114", "#f59e0b"),
                "Hard": ("#2a1215", "#ef4444"),
            }
            bg_col, fg_col = diff_colors.get(diff, ("#1e2433", "#00e5ff"))
            tk.Label(
                top_row,
                text=diff,
                bg=bg_col,
                fg=fg_col,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
                padx=10,
                pady=3,
            ).pack(side="right")

        # Content / Notes
        if item.content:
            body = tk.Label(
                card,
                text=item.content,
                bg="#1c212c",
                fg="#cbd5e1",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
                wraplength=700,
                justify="left",
                anchor="w",
            )
            body.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 10))

        # Action bar: Toggle Done, Open URL, Edit, Delete
        action_row = tk.Frame(card, bg="#1c212c")
        action_row.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 0))

        done_btn_text = "Mark Todo" if item.is_done else "Mark Solved"
        done_btn_fg = "#94a3b8" if item.is_done else "#00e5ff"
        tk.Button(
            action_row,
            text=done_btn_text,
            bg="#242b3a",
            fg=done_btn_fg,
            activebackground="#2b3448",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=14,
            pady=6,
            command=lambda i=item.id: self.toggle_dsa_done(i),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 10))

        if item.url:
            tk.Button(
                action_row,
                text="Open Problem Link",
                bg="#242b3a",
                fg="#38bdf8",
                activebackground="#2b3448",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=12,
                pady=6,
                command=lambda u=item.url: webbrowser.open(u),
            ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 10))

        tk.Button(
            action_row,
            text="Delete",
            bg="#242b3a",
            fg="#ef4444",
            activebackground="#3b1d24",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=6,
            command=lambda i=item.id: self.delete_problem(i),
        ).pack(side="right")

        tk.Button(
            action_row,
            text="Edit",
            bg="#242b3a",
            fg="#cbd5e1",
            activebackground="#2b3448",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=6,
            command=lambda it=item: self.open_edit_problem(it),
        ).pack(side="right", padx=(0, 8))

    def toggle_dsa_done(self, item_id: str) -> None:
        """Toggle problem solved state."""
        if not self.code_service:
            return
        self.code_service.toggle_done(item_id)
        self.refresh()

    def open_create_problem(self) -> None:
        """Launch modal dialog to register a new DSA problem."""
        if not self.code_service:
            return

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            self.code_service.create_item(
                category=self.CATEGORY_NAME,
                title=data["title"],
                content=data["content"],
                language=data["language"],
                difficulty=data["difficulty"],
                url=data["url"],
            )
            self.refresh()

        CodeDialog(
            self.parent_window,
            mode="create",
            on_save=_on_save,
            theme=self.theme,
        )

    def open_edit_problem(self, item: CodeItem) -> None:
        """Launch modal dialog to edit an existing DSA problem."""
        if not self.code_service:
            return

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            self.code_service.update_code_item(
                item_id=item.id,
                category=self.CATEGORY_NAME,
                title=data["title"],
                content=data["content"],
                language=data["language"],
                difficulty=data["difficulty"],
                url=data["url"],
            )
            self.refresh()

        CodeDialog(
            self.parent_window,
            item=item,
            mode="edit",
            on_save=_on_save,
            theme=self.theme,
        )

    def delete_problem(self, item_id: str) -> None:
        """Delete a DSA problem after confirmation."""
        if not self.code_service:
            return
        if messagebox.askyesno(
            "Confirm Delete",
            "Delete this DSA problem?",
            parent=self.parent_window,
        ):
            self.code_service.soft_delete_item(item_id)
            self.refresh()


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/views/ideas_view.py ==========
"""PS/I (Problem Solving, Project Ideas & Concept Doubts) workspace view."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import messagebox
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import IdeaDoubt
from plus_ultra.services import IdeaDoubtService
from plus_ultra.ui.components.scrollable import ScrollableFrame
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs import IdeaDialog
from plus_ultra.ui.icons import get_icon
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class IdeasView:
    """Sub-tabbed PS/I workspace view with status lifecycles."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        container: tk.Widget,
        parent_window: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        idea_doubt_service: IdeaDoubtService | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
    ) -> None:
        self.container = container
        self.parent_window = parent_window
        self.idea_doubt_service = idea_doubt_service
        self.theme = theme

        self.active_subtab: str = "Idea"
        self._build_ui()

    @property
    def active_idea_filter(self) -> str:
        """Compatibility property for existing callers and tests."""
        return self.active_subtab

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        head_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28, pady=24)
        head_bar.pack(fill="x")

        icon = get_icon("fallback", 24)
        title_lbl = tk.Label(
            head_bar,
            text=" PS/I",
            bg="#161922",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 16, "bold"),
            image=icon,
            compound="left",
        )
        title_lbl.image = icon  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        title_lbl.pack(side="left")

        self.add_btn = tk.Button(
            head_bar,
            text="+ Add Idea",
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self.open_create_entry,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=18,
            pady=8,
        )
        self.add_btn.pack(side="right")

        # Two dedicated sub-tabs: [ Project Ideas ] and [ Concept Doubts ]
        tab_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28)
        tab_bar.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 16))

        self.btn_ideas_tab = tk.Button(
            tab_bar,
            text="Project Ideas",
            bg="#1e2433",
            fg="#00e5ff",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=18,
            pady=7,
            command=lambda: self.switch_subtab("Idea"),
        )
        self.btn_ideas_tab.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))

        self.btn_doubts_tab = tk.Button(
            tab_bar,
            text="Concept Doubts",
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=18,
            pady=7,
            command=lambda: self.switch_subtab("Doubt"),
        )
        self.btn_doubts_tab.pack(side="left")

        self.ideas_scroll = ScrollableFrame(self.container, bg="#161922")
        self.ideas_scroll.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=28, pady=(0, 20))

    def switch_subtab(self, kind: str) -> None:
        """Switch between Ideas and Doubts sub-tabs."""
        self.active_subtab = kind
        if kind == "Idea":
            self.btn_ideas_tab.configure(bg="#1e2433", fg="#00e5ff")
            self.btn_doubts_tab.configure(bg="#12151c", fg="#94a3b8")
            self.add_btn.configure(text="+ Add Idea")
        else:
            self.btn_doubts_tab.configure(bg="#1e2433", fg="#00e5ff")
            self.btn_ideas_tab.configure(bg="#12151c", fg="#94a3b8")
            self.add_btn.configure(text="+ Add Doubt")
        self.refresh()

    def set_idea_filter(self, kind: str) -> None:
        """Compatibility filter method."""
        target = "Doubt" if kind == "Doubt" else "Idea"
        self.switch_subtab(target)

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        """Query entries for active subtab and render cards."""
        parent = self.ideas_scroll.interior
        for child in parent.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()

        if not self.idea_doubt_service:
            return

        items = self.idea_doubt_service.list_items(kind=self.active_subtab)

        if not items:
            empty_msg = (
                "No project ideas recorded yet."
                if self.active_subtab == "Idea"
                else "No concept doubts recorded yet."
            )
            empty_frame = tk.Frame(parent, bg="#161922", pady=48)
            empty_frame.pack(fill="x")
            tk.Label(
                empty_frame,
                text=empty_msg,
                bg="#161922",
                fg="#64748b",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 12),
            ).pack()
            return

        for item in items:
            self._render_card(parent, item)

    def _render_card(self, parent: tk.Widget, item: IdeaDoubt) -> None:
        card = tk.Frame(
            parent,
            bg="#1c212c",
            padx=24,
            pady=18,
            highlightbackground="#2b3448",
            highlightthickness=1,
        )
        card.pack(fill="x", pady=8)

        top = tk.Frame(card, bg="#1c212c")
        top.pack(fill="x")

        kind_text = f"[{item.kind}]"
        kind_bg = "#083344" if item.kind == "Idea" else "#312e81"
        kind_fg = "#22d3ee" if item.kind == "Idea" else "#a5b4fc"

        tk.Label(
            top,
            text=kind_text,
            bg=kind_bg,
            fg=kind_fg,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
            padx=8,
            pady=3,
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 10))

        tk.Label(
            top,
            text=item.title,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 14, "bold"),
            anchor="w",
        ).pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)

        acts = tk.Frame(top, bg="#1c212c")
        acts.pack(side="right")

        # Status Cycle / Toggle Button
        status_bg = (
            "#064e3b" if item.status in ("Resolved", "Completed") else "#1e2433"
        )
        status_fg = (
            "#34d399" if item.status in ("Resolved", "Completed") else "#00e5ff"
        )
        tk.Button(
            acts,
            text=f"[{item.status}]",
            bg=status_bg,
            fg=status_fg,
            activebackground="#2b3448",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda iid=item.id: self.advance_idea(iid),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        tk.Button(
            acts,
            text="Edit",
            bg="#2b3448",
            fg="#e2e8f0",
            activebackground="#3b475e",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda it=item: self.open_edit_idea(it),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        tk.Button(
            acts,
            text="Delete",
            bg="#3f1d24",
            fg="#fca5a5",
            activebackground="#5c2630",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda iid=item.id: self.delete_idea(iid),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        if item.tech_stack_or_ref:
            tk.Label(
                card,
                text=f"Reference: {item.tech_stack_or_ref}",
                bg="#1e2433",
                fg="#94a3b8",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 9),
                padx=8,
                pady=2,
                anchor="w",
            ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(8, 2))

        if item.description:
            tk.Label(
                card,
                text=item.description,
                bg="#1c212c",
                fg="#cbd5e1",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
                anchor="w",
                justify="left",
            ).pack(fill="x", pady=(6, 0))

    def advance_idea(self, item_id: str) -> None:
        """Cycle idea status or toggle doubt open/resolved."""
        if self.idea_doubt_service:
            self.idea_doubt_service.advance_status(item_id)
            self.refresh()

    def delete_idea(self, item_id: str) -> None:
        """Prompt confirmation and soft-delete idea or doubt."""
        if (
            messagebox.askyesno(
                "Delete", "Delete this entry?", parent=self.parent_window
            )
            and self.idea_doubt_service
        ):
            self.idea_doubt_service.soft_delete_item(item_id)
            self.refresh()

    def open_create_entry(self) -> None:
        """Open IdeaDialog to create a new idea or doubt for active subtab."""

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            if self.idea_doubt_service:
                self.idea_doubt_service.create_item(
                    kind=data["kind"],
                    title=data["title"],
                    description=data["description"],
                    tech_stack_or_ref=data["tech_stack_or_ref"],
                    status=data["status"],
                )
                self.refresh()

        IdeaDialog(
            self.parent_window,
            default_kind=self.active_subtab,
            on_save=_on_save,
        )

    def open_create_idea(self) -> None:
        """Compatibility method."""
        self.open_create_entry()

    def open_edit_idea(self, item: IdeaDoubt) -> None:
        """Open IdeaDialog to edit an existing idea or doubt."""

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            if self.idea_doubt_service:
                self.idea_doubt_service.update_idea_doubt(
                    item_id=item.id,
                    title=data["title"],
                    kind=data["kind"],
                    description=data["description"],
                    tech_stack_or_ref=data["tech_stack_or_ref"],
                    status=data["status"],
                )
                self.refresh()

        IdeaDialog(self.parent_window, item=item, on_save=_on_save)
