========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/views/opportunities_view.py ==========
"""Opportunities and Career pipeline workspace view component."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
import webbrowser
from tkinter import messagebox
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import Opportunity
from plus_ultra.services import OpportunityService
from plus_ultra.ui.components.scrollable import ScrollableFrame
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs import OpportunityDialog
from plus_ultra.ui.icons import get_icon
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class OpportunitiesView:
    """Card-based opportunities workspace view with status lifecycles."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        container: tk.Widget,
        parent_window: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        opportunity_service: OpportunityService | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
    ) -> None:
        self.container = container
        self.parent_window = parent_window
        self.opportunity_service = opportunity_service
        self.theme = theme

        self.active_opp_filter: str = "All"
        self.opp_filter_btns: dict[str, tk.Button] = {}

        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        head_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28, pady=24)
        head_bar.pack(fill="x")

        icon = get_icon("fallback", 24)
        title_lbl = tk.Label(
            head_bar,
            text=" Opportunities & Pipeline",
            bg="#161922",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 16, "bold"),
            image=icon,
            compound="left",
        )
        title_lbl.image = icon  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        title_lbl.pack(side="left")

        tk.Button(
            head_bar,
            text="+ Add Opportunity",
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self.open_create_opportunity,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=18,
            pady=8,
        ).pack(side="right")

        filter_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28)
        filter_bar.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 16))

        for kind in [
            "All",
            "Internship",
            "Full-time",
            "Hackathon",
            "Fellowship",
        ]:
            btn = tk.Button(
                filter_bar,
                text=kind,
                bg="#1e2433" if kind == "All" else "#12151c",
                fg="#00e5ff" if kind == "All" else "#94a3b8",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                highlightthickness=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=14,
                pady=6,
                command=lambda k=kind: self.set_opp_filter(k),
            )
            btn.pack(side="left", padx=3)
            self.opp_filter_btns[kind] = btn

        self.opps_scroll = ScrollableFrame(self.container, bg="#161922")
        self.opps_scroll.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=28, pady=(0, 20))

    def set_opp_filter(self, kind: str) -> None:
        """Update active opportunity type filter and reload cards."""
        self.active_opp_filter = kind
        for k, btn in self.opp_filter_btns.items():
            if k == kind:
                btn.configure(bg="#1e2433", fg="#00e5ff")
            else:
                btn.configure(bg="#12151c", fg="#94a3b8")
        self.refresh()

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        """Query opportunities matching filter and render interactive cards."""
        parent = self.opps_scroll.interior
        for child in parent.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()

        if not self.opportunity_service:
            return

        kind = (
            None if self.active_opp_filter == "All" else self.active_opp_filter
        )
        opps = self.opportunity_service.list_opportunities(kind=kind)

        if not opps:
            empty_frame = tk.Frame(parent, bg="#161922", pady=48)
            empty_frame.pack(fill="x")
            tk.Label(
                empty_frame,
                text="No opportunities saved in this filter.",
                bg="#161922",
                fg="#64748b",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 12),
            ).pack()
            return

        for opp in opps:
            self._render_card(parent, opp)

    def _render_card(self, parent: tk.Widget, opp: Opportunity) -> None:
        card = tk.Frame(
            parent,
            bg="#1c212c",
            padx=24,
            pady=18,
            highlightbackground="#2b3448",
            highlightthickness=1,
        )
        card.pack(fill="x", pady=8)

        top = tk.Frame(card, bg="#1c212c")
        top.pack(fill="x")

        tk.Label(
            top,
            text=f"{opp.title}  |  {opp.organization}",
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 14, "bold"),
            anchor="w",
        ).pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)

        acts = tk.Frame(top, bg="#1c212c")
        acts.pack(side="right")

        st_colors = {
            "Saved": ("#1e2433", "#94a3b8"),
            "Applied": ("#1e2433", "#00e5ff"),
            "In Progress": ("#452a10", "#fbbf24"),
            "Accepted": ("#064e3b", "#34d399"),
            "Rejected": ("#451a1a", "#f87171"),
        }
        bg_s, fg_s = st_colors.get(opp.status, ("#1e2433", "#94a3b8"))
        tk.Button(
            acts,
            text=f"[{opp.status}]",
            bg=bg_s,
            fg=fg_s,
            activebackground="#2b3448",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda oid=opp.id: self.advance_opp(oid),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        if opp.url:
            tk.Button(
                acts,
                text="Apply Link",
                bg="#00e5ff",
                fg="#0a0e17",
                activebackground="#00b4d8",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=12,
                pady=5,
                command=lambda u=opp.url: webbrowser.open(u),
            ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        tk.Button(
            acts,
            text="Edit",
            bg="#2b3448",
            fg="#e2e8f0",
            activebackground="#3b475e",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda o=opp: self.open_edit_opportunity(o),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        tk.Button(
            acts,
            text="Delete",
            bg="#3f1d24",
            fg="#fca5a5",
            activebackground="#5c2630",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda oid=opp.id: self.delete_opp(oid),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        meta_row = tk.Frame(card, bg="#1c212c")
        meta_row.pack(fill="x", pady=(10, 0))

        tk.Label(
            meta_row,
            text=f"[{opp.kind}]",
            bg="#083344",
            fg="#22d3ee",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 9, "bold"),
            padx=8,
            pady=2,
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))

        if opp.deadline:
            tk.Label(
                meta_row,
                text=f"Deadline: {opp.deadline}",
                bg="#1e2433",
                fg="#fbbf24",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 9, "bold"),
                padx=8,
                pady=2,
            ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        if opp.notes:
            tk.Label(
                card,
                text=opp.notes,
                bg="#1c212c",
                fg="#cbd5e1",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
                anchor="w",
                justify="left",
            ).pack(fill="x", pady=(8, 0))

    def advance_opp(self, opp_id: str) -> None:
        """Advance opportunity status along its pipeline."""
        if self.opportunity_service:
            self.opportunity_service.advance_status(opp_id)
            self.refresh()

    def delete_opp(self, opp_id: str) -> None:
        """Prompt confirmation and soft-delete opportunity."""
        if (
            messagebox.askyesno(
                "Delete", "Delete this opportunity?", parent=self.parent_window
            )
            and self.opportunity_service
        ):
            self.opportunity_service.soft_delete_opportunity(opp_id)
            self.refresh()

    def open_create_opportunity(self) -> None:
        """Open OpportunityDialog to add a new opportunity."""

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            if self.opportunity_service:
                self.opportunity_service.create_opportunity(
                    title=data["title"],
                    organization=data["organization"],
                    kind=data["kind"],
                    status=data["status"],
                    deadline=data["deadline"],
                    url=data["url"],
                    notes=data["notes"],
                )
                self.refresh()

        OpportunityDialog(self.parent_window, on_save=_on_save)

    def open_edit_opportunity(self, opp: Opportunity) -> None:
        """Open OpportunityDialog to edit an existing opportunity."""

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            if self.opportunity_service:
                self.opportunity_service.update_opportunity(
                    opp_id=opp.id,
                    title=data["title"],
                    organization=data["organization"],
                    kind=data["kind"],
                    status=data["status"],
                    deadline=data["deadline"],
                    url=data["url"],
                    notes=data["notes"],
                )
                self.refresh()

        OpportunityDialog(self.parent_window, item=opp, on_save=_on_save)


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/views/quick_links_view.py ==========
"""Quick Links and Launcher Shortcuts workspace view component."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import subprocess
import tkinter as tk
import webbrowser
from tkinter import messagebox
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import QuickLink
from plus_ultra.services import QuickLinkService
from plus_ultra.ui.components.scrollable import ScrollableFrame
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs import QuickLinkDialog
from plus_ultra.ui.icons import get_icon
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class QuickLinksView:
    """Card-based launcher shortcuts workspace view with click tracking."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        container: tk.Widget,
        parent_window: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        quick_link_service: QuickLinkService | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
    ) -> None:
        self.container = container
        self.parent_window = parent_window
        self.quick_link_service = quick_link_service
        self.theme = theme

        self.active_link_filter: str = "All"
        self.link_filter_btns: dict[str, tk.Button] = {}

        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        head_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28, pady=24)
        head_bar.pack(fill="x")

        icon = get_icon("fallback", 24)
        title_lbl = tk.Label(
            head_bar,
            text=" Quick Links & Shortcuts",
            bg="#161922",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 16, "bold"),
            image=icon,
            compound="left",
        )
        title_lbl.image = icon  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        title_lbl.pack(side="left")

        tk.Button(
            head_bar,
            text="+ Add Quick Link",
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self.open_create_link,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=18,
            pady=8,
        ).pack(side="right")

        filter_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28)
        filter_bar.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 16))

        for cat in ["All", "Web", "Local Folder", "Local File", "Tool"]:
            btn = tk.Button(
                filter_bar,
                text=cat,
                bg="#1e2433" if cat == "All" else "#12151c",
                fg="#00e5ff" if cat == "All" else "#94a3b8",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                highlightthickness=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=14,
                pady=6,
                command=lambda c=cat: self.set_link_filter(c),
            )
            btn.pack(side="left", padx=3)
            self.link_filter_btns[cat] = btn

        self.links_scroll = ScrollableFrame(self.container, bg="#161922")
        self.links_scroll.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=28, pady=(0, 20))

    def set_link_filter(self, category: str) -> None:
        """Update active shortcut category filter and reload cards."""
        self.active_link_filter = category
        for c, btn in self.link_filter_btns.items():
            if c == category:
                btn.configure(bg="#1e2433", fg="#00e5ff")
            else:
                btn.configure(bg="#12151c", fg="#94a3b8")
        self.refresh()

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        """Query quick links matching category and render interactive cards."""
        parent = self.links_scroll.interior
        for child in parent.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()

        if not self.quick_link_service:
            return

        cat = (
            None
            if self.active_link_filter == "All"
            else self.active_link_filter
        )
        links = self.quick_link_service.list_links(category=cat)

        if not links:
            empty_frame = tk.Frame(parent, bg="#161922", pady=48)
            empty_frame.pack(fill="x")
            tk.Label(
                empty_frame,
                text="No quick links found in this category.",
                bg="#161922",
                fg="#64748b",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 12),
            ).pack()
            return

        for link in links:
            self._render_card(parent, link)

    def _render_card(self, parent: tk.Widget, link: QuickLink) -> None:
        card = tk.Frame(
            parent,
            bg="#1c212c",
            padx=24,
            pady=18,
            highlightbackground="#2b3448",
            highlightthickness=1,
        )
        card.pack(fill="x", pady=8)

        top = tk.Frame(card, bg="#1c212c")
        top.pack(fill="x")

        tk.Label(
            top,
            text=f"[{link.category}]",
            bg="#083344",
            fg="#22d3ee",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
            padx=8,
            pady=3,
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 10))

        tk.Label(
            top,
            text=link.name,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 14, "bold"),
            anchor="w",
        ).pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)

        acts = tk.Frame(top, bg="#1c212c")
        acts.pack(side="right")

        tk.Label(
            acts,
            text=f"Clicks: {link.click_count}",
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=10)

        tk.Button(
            acts,
            text="Open",
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=14,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda lnk=link: self.launch_quick_link(lnk),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        tk.Button(
            acts,
            text="Edit",
            bg="#2b3448",
            fg="#e2e8f0",
            activebackground="#3b475e",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda lnk=link: self.open_edit_link(lnk),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        tk.Button(
            acts,
            text="Delete",
            bg="#3f1d24",
            fg="#fca5a5",
            activebackground="#5c2630",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda lid=link.id: self.delete_link(lid),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        tk.Label(
            card,
            text=link.target_uri,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=("courier", 10),
            anchor="w",
        ).pack(fill="x", pady=(8, 0))

    def launch_quick_link(self, link: QuickLink) -> None:
        """Launch web link or open local file/folder via xdg-open."""
        uri = link.target_uri
        if uri.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
            webbrowser.open(uri)
        else:
            expanded = os.path.expanduser(uri)
            subprocess.Popen(["xdg-open", expanded])

        if self.quick_link_service:
            self.quick_link_service.record_click(link.id)
            self.refresh()

    def delete_link(self, link_id: str) -> None:
        """Prompt confirmation and soft-delete quick link."""
        if (
            messagebox.askyesno(
                "Delete", "Delete quick link?", parent=self.parent_window
            )
            and self.quick_link_service
        ):
            self.quick_link_service.soft_delete_link(link_id)
            self.refresh()

    def open_create_link(self) -> None:
        """Open QuickLinkDialog to add a new link."""

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            if self.quick_link_service:
                self.quick_link_service.create_link(
                    name=data["name"],
                    target_uri=data["target_uri"],
                    category=data["category"],
                )
                self.refresh()

        QuickLinkDialog(self.parent_window, on_save=_on_save)

    def open_edit_link(self, link: QuickLink) -> None:
        """Open QuickLinkDialog to edit an existing link."""

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            if self.quick_link_service:
                self.quick_link_service.update_quick_link(
                    link_id=link.id,
                    name=data["name"],
                    target_uri=data["target_uri"],
                    category=data["category"],
                )
                self.refresh()

        QuickLinkDialog(self.parent_window, link=link, on_save=_on_save)

"""Preferences and Diagnostics workspace view component."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tkinter as tk
from collections.abc import Callable
from tkinter import messagebox

from plus_ultra.database import get_connection, seed_demo_workspace_data

class SettingsView:
    """Preferences and Diagnostics view displaying status and maintenance."""

    def __init__(
        self,

            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            anchor="w",
        ).pack(fill="x", pady=3)

        card3 = tk.Frame(
            content,

            padx=24,
            pady=20,
            highlightbackground="#2b3448",

        pass

        """Refresh settings state."""
        pass

    def reseed_demo_data(self) -> None:
        """Trigger workspace demo data reseed and notify user."""
        conn = get_connection()
        try:
            seed_demo_workspace_data(conn)
            messagebox.showinfo(
                "Demo Data",
                "Successfully reseeded demo workspace data across all tabs!",
                parent=self.parent_window,
            )
            if self.on_reseed:
                self.on_reseed()
        except Exception as exc:
            messagebox.showerror(
                "Reseed Error",
                f"Failed to reseed workspace data: {exc}",
                parent=self.parent_window,
            )


========== FILE: ./src/plus_ultra/ui/views/tasks_view.py ==========
"""Tasks and Deadlines workspace view component."""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import datetime
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import messagebox, ttk
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.models import Task
from plus_ultra.services import CategoryService, ReminderService, TaskService
from plus_ultra.ui.components.scrollable import ScrollableFrame
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs import CategoryDialog, TaskDialog
from plus_ultra.ui.icons import get_icon
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import THEME, CapsuleTheme


class TasksView:
    """Card-based tasks workspace view with filtering, searching, and CRUD."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        container: tk.Widget,
        parent_window: tk.Tk | tk.Toplevel,
        task_service: TaskService,
        category_service: CategoryService,
        reminder_service: ReminderService | None = None,
        theme: CapsuleTheme = THEME,
    ) -> None:
        self.container = container
        self.parent_window = parent_window
        self.task_service = task_service
        self.category_service = category_service
        self.reminder_service = reminder_service
        self.theme = theme

        self.current_status_filter: str = "All"
        self.current_priority_filter: str = "All Priority"
        self.filtered_tasks: list[Task] = []
        self.status_buttons: dict[str, tk.Button] = {}

        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        head_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28, pady=24)
        head_bar.pack(fill="x")

        icon = get_icon("fallback", 24)
        title_lbl = tk.Label(
            head_bar,
            text=" Tasks",
            bg="#161922",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 16, "bold"),
            image=icon,
            compound="left",
        )
        title_lbl.image = icon  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        title_lbl.pack(side="left")

        tk.Button(
            head_bar,
            text="+ New Task",
            bg="#00e5ff",
            fg="#0a0e17",
            activebackground="#00b4d8",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11, "bold"),
            command=self.open_create_task,
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=18,
            pady=8,
        ).pack(side="right")

        # Filters Bar
        filter_bar = tk.Frame(self.container, bg="#161922", padx=28)
        filter_bar.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 16))

        search_box = tk.Frame(filter_bar, bg="#161922")
        search_box.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0, 16))
        tk.Label(
            search_box,
            text="Search:",
            bg="#161922",
            fg="#94a3b8",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        self.search_entry = tk.Entry(
            search_box,
            bg="#12151c",
            fg="#f8fafc",
            relief="flat",
            insertbackground="#00e5ff",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
        )
        self.search_entry.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, ipady=5)
        self.search_entry.bind("<KeyRelease>", lambda _e: self.refresh())

        status_box = tk.Frame(filter_bar, bg="#161922")
        status_box.pack(side="right")
        for st in ["All", "Pending", "Completed"]:
            btn = tk.Button(
                status_box,
                text=st,
                bg="#1e2433" if st == "All" else "#12151c",
                fg="#00e5ff" if st == "All" else "#94a3b8",
                relief="flat",
                bd=0,
                highlightthickness=0,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
                cursor="hand2",
                padx=12,
                pady=5,
                command=lambda s=st: self.set_status_filter(s),
            )
            btn.pack(side="left", padx=3)
            self.status_buttons[st] = btn

        self.prio_filter_combo = ttk.Combobox(
            status_box,
            values=["All Priority", "High", "Medium", "Low"],
            width=13,
            state="readonly",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
        )
        self.prio_filter_combo.set("All Priority")
        self.prio_filter_combo.pack(side="left", padx=(8, 0))
        self.prio_filter_combo.bind(
            "<<ComboboxSelected>>", lambda _e: self._on_prio_filter_change()
        )

        # Scrollable Task Cards Frame
        self.tasks_scroll = ScrollableFrame(self.container, bg="#161922")
        self.tasks_scroll.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=28, pady=(0, 20))

    def set_status_filter(self, status: str) -> None:
        """Update active status filter button and reload cards."""
        self.current_status_filter = status
        for st, btn in self.status_buttons.items():
            if st == status:
                btn.configure(bg="#1e2433", fg="#00e5ff")
            else:
                btn.configure(bg="#12151c", fg="#94a3b8")
        self.refresh()

    def _on_prio_filter_change(self) -> None:
        self.current_priority_filter = self.prio_filter_combo.get()
        self.refresh()

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        """Query tasks matching filters and render interactive 2x cards."""
        parent = self.tasks_scroll.interior
        for child in parent.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()

        query = self.search_entry.get().strip() or None
        stat = (
            None
            if self.current_status_filter == "All"
            else self.current_status_filter
        )
        prio = (
            None
            if self.current_priority_filter == "All Priority"
            else self.current_priority_filter
        )

        tasks = self.task_service.list_tasks(
            status=stat, critical_level=prio, search_query=query
        )
        self.filtered_tasks = tasks

        if not tasks:
            empty_frame = tk.Frame(parent, bg="#161922", pady=48)
            empty_frame.pack(fill="x")
            tk.Label(
                empty_frame,
                text="No tasks match your filters.",
                bg="#161922",
                fg="#64748b",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 12),
            ).pack()
            return

        cat_map = {
            c.id: c.name for c in self.category_service.list_categories()
        }

        for task in tasks:
            self._render_task_card(parent, task, cat_map)

    def _render_task_card(
        self, parent: tk.Widget, task: Task, cat_map: dict[str, str]
    ) -> None:
        card = tk.Frame(
            parent,
            bg="#1c212c",
            padx=24,
            pady=18,
            highlightbackground="#2b3448",
            highlightthickness=1,
        )
        card.pack(fill="x", pady=8)

        top_row = tk.Frame(card, bg="#1c212c")
        top_row.pack(fill="x")

        is_done = task.status == "Completed"
        status_label = "[Done]" if is_done else "[Pending]"
        status_fg = "#10b981" if is_done else "#00e5ff"
        status_bg = "#0f291e" if is_done else "#162032"

        status_btn = tk.Button(
            top_row,
            text=status_label,
            bg=status_bg,
            fg=status_fg,
            activebackground=status_bg,
            activeforeground=status_fg,
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10, "bold"),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=10,
            pady=4,
            command=lambda tid=task.id: self.toggle_selected_status(tid),
        )
        status_btn.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 12))

        title_fg = "#64748b" if is_done else "#f8fafc"
        tk.Label(
            top_row,
            text=task.title,
            bg="#1c212c",
            fg=title_fg,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 14, "bold"),
            anchor="w",
        ).pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)

        act_box = tk.Frame(top_row, bg="#1c212c")
        act_box.pack(side="right")

        tk.Button(
            act_box,
            text="Edit",
            bg="#2b3448",
            fg="#e2e8f0",
            activebackground="#3b475e",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda t=task: self.open_edit_task(t),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        tk.Button(
            act_box,
            text="Delete",
            bg="#3f1d24",
            fg="#fca5a5",
            activebackground="#5c2630",
            relief="flat",
            bd=0,
            highlightthickness=0,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 10),
            cursor="hand2",
            padx=12,
            pady=5,
            command=lambda tid=task.id: self.delete_task(tid),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        meta_row = tk.Frame(card, bg="#1c212c")
        meta_row.pack(fill="x", pady=(10, 0))

        cat_name = cat_map.get(task.category_id or "", "General")
        tk.Label(
            meta_row,
            text=f"[{cat_name}]",
            bg="#083344",
            fg="#22d3ee",
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 9, "bold"),
            padx=8,
            pady=2,
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))

        p_colors = {
            "High": ("#451a1a", "#f87171"),
            "Medium": ("#452a10", "#fbbf24"),
            "Low": ("#1e293b", "#94a3b8"),
        }
        bg_c, fg_c = p_colors.get(task.critical_level, ("#1e293b", "#94a3b8"))
        tk.Label(
            meta_row,
            text=f"[{task.critical_level}]",
            bg=bg_c,
            fg=fg_c,
            font=(self.theme.font_family, 9, "bold"),
            padx=8,
            pady=2,
        ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        if task.pace_level:
            tk.Label(
                meta_row,
                text=f"Pace: {task.pace_level}",
                bg="#1e2433",
                fg="#cbd5e1",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 9),
                padx=8,
                pady=2,
            ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        if task.due_date:
            due_text = f"Due: {task.due_date}"
            due_bg = "#1e2433"
            due_fg = "#cbd5e1"
            now_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
            due_cutoff = now_str[: len(task.due_date)]
            if not is_done and task.due_date < due_cutoff:
                due_text = f"Overdue: {task.due_date}"
                due_bg = "#451a1a"
                due_fg = "#f87171"

            tk.Label(
                meta_row,
                text=due_text,
                bg=due_bg,
                fg=due_fg,
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 9, "bold"),
                padx=8,
                pady=2,
            ).pack(side="left", padx=4)

        if task.description:
            tk.Label(
                card,
                text=task.description,
                bg="#1c212c",
                fg="#94a3b8",
                font=(self.theme.font_family, 11),
                anchor="w",
                justify="left",
            ).pack(fill="x", pady=(8, 0))

    def toggle_selected_status(self, task_id: str) -> None:
        """Toggle task status between Pending and Completed."""
        self.task_service.toggle_status(task_id)
        self.refresh()

    def delete_task(self, task_id: str) -> None:
        """Prompt confirmation and soft-delete a task."""
        if messagebox.askyesno(
            "Delete Task", "Delete this task?", parent=self.parent_window
        ):
            self.task_service.soft_delete_task(task_id)
            self.refresh()

    def open_create_task(self) -> None:
        """Open TaskDialog to add a new task."""
        cats = self.category_service.list_categories()

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            new_task = self.task_service.create_task(
                title=data["title"],
                category_id=data["category_id"],
                critical_level=data["critical_level"],
                pace_level=data["pace_level"],
                due_date=data["due_date"],
                description=data["description"],
            )
            if (
                data.get("schedule_reminder")
                and data["due_date"]
                and self.reminder_service
            ):
                with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
                    self.reminder_service.create_reminder(
                        new_task.id, data["due_date"]
                    )
            self.refresh()

        TaskDialog(
            self.parent_window,
            categories=cats,
            on_save=_on_save,
            category_service=self.category_service,
        )

    def open_edit_task(self, task: Task) -> None:
        """Open TaskDialog to edit an existing task."""
        cats = self.category_service.list_categories()

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            self.task_service.update_task(
                task_id=task.id,
                title=data["title"],
                category_id=data["category_id"],
                critical_level=data["critical_level"],
                pace_level=data["pace_level"],
                due_date=data["due_date"],
                description=data["description"],
            )
            if (
                data.get("schedule_reminder")
                and data["due_date"]
                and self.reminder_service
            ):
                with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
                    self.reminder_service.create_reminder(
                        task.id, data["due_date"]
                    )
            self.refresh()

        TaskDialog(
            self.parent_window,
            categories=cats,
            task=task,
            on_save=_on_save,
            category_service=self.category_service,
        )

    def open_create_category(self) -> None:
        """Open CategoryDialog to create a new category."""
        CategoryDialog(
            self.parent_window,
            category_service=self.category_service,
            on_success=self.refresh,
        )


========== FILE: ./.tests/test_config.py ==========
"""Tests for plus_ultra.config and initial application startup.

Validates:
- Happy paths: default XDG directory resolution and environment overrides.
- Security: directory permissions mode 0o700 (owner read, write, execute only).
- Edge cases: empty environment variables, unicode paths, and long paths.
- Integration: main() startup logging to launcher.log.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import stat
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from plus_ultra import config
from plus_ultra.main import main as app_main


def test_default_xdg_paths(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: Path
) -> None:
    """When no XDG env vars are set, fallback to ~/.local/share and state."""
    fake_home = tmp_path / "home"
    monkeypatch.setattr(Path, "home", lambda: fake_home)
    monkeypatch.delenv("XDG_DATA_HOME", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.delenv("XDG_STATE_HOME", raising=False)

    data_dir = config.get_data_dir()
    state_dir = config.get_state_dir()

    expected_data = fake_home / ".local" / "share" / config.APP_NAME
    expected_state = fake_home / ".local" / "state" / config.APP_NAME

    assert data_dir == expected_data
    assert state_dir == expected_state
    assert data_dir.is_dir()
    assert state_dir.is_dir()


def test_custom_xdg_env_override(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: Path
) -> None:
    """When XDG env vars are set, use them."""
    custom_data = tmp_path / "custom_data"
    custom_state = tmp_path / "custom_state"

    monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_DATA_HOME", str(custom_data))
    monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_STATE_HOME", str(custom_state))

    data_dir = config.get_data_dir()
    state_dir = config.get_state_dir()

    assert data_dir == custom_data / config.APP_NAME
    assert state_dir == custom_state / config.APP_NAME
    assert config.get_db_path() == data_dir / config.DB_FILENAME
    assert config.get_log_path() == state_dir / config.LOG_FILENAME


def test_directory_permissions_mode_0700(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: Path
) -> None:
    """Security check: Directories created with mode 0o700 (rwx------)."""
    target = tmp_path / "secure_env"
    monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_DATA_HOME", str(target / "data"))
    monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_STATE_HOME", str(target / "state"))

    data_dir = config.get_data_dir()
    state_dir = config.get_state_dir()

    data_mode = stat.S_IMODE(data_dir.stat().st_mode)
    state_mode = stat.S_IMODE(state_dir.stat().st_mode)

    assert data_mode == 0o700, f"Expected 0o700, got {oct(data_mode)}"
    assert state_mode == 0o700, f"Expected 0o700, got {oct(state_mode)}"

========== FILE: ./.tests/test_db.py ==========
"""Tests for database initialization, pragmas, transactions, and constraints.

Validates DBMS concepts:
- Concurrency and durability via WAL mode and busy timeout pragmas.
- ACID transaction atomicity and automatic rollback on failure.
- Schema constraints (check constraints, foreign key enforcement).
- Partial indexes and migration idempotency.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from plus_ultra.database import (
    CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    get_connection,
    get_schema_version,
    init_db,
    set_schema_version,
    transaction,
)


@pytest.fixture
def test_db_path(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
    """Provide a path to a temporary SQLite database."""
    return tmp_path / "test_plus_ultra.db"


def test_init_db_creates_schema_and_version(test_db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify init_db initializes tables and updates user_version."""
    init_db(test_db_path)
    conn = get_connection(test_db_path)
    try:
        assert get_schema_version(conn) == CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION

        cur = conn.execute(
            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' ORDER BY name;"
        )
        table_names = {row[0] for row in cur.fetchall()}
        expected_tables = {
            "categories",
            "tasks",
            "reminders",
            "reminder_fires",
            "app_state",
            "code_items",
            "courses",
            "ideas_doubts",
            "opportunities",
            "quick_links",
        }
        assert expected_tables.issubset(table_names)

    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_init_db_is_idempotent(test_db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify init_db can run repeatedly without error or schema loss."""
    init_db(test_db_path)
    # Second run
    init_db(test_db_path)
    conn = get_connection(test_db_path)
    try:
        assert get_schema_version(conn) == CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_connection_pragmas(test_db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify foreign_keys, journal_mode, and busy_timeout pragmas."""
    init_db(test_db_path)
    conn = get_connection(test_db_path)
    try:
        fk = conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys;").fetchone()[0]
        assert fk == 1

        journal = conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode;").fetchone()[0]
        assert journal.lower() == "wal"

        timeout = conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout;").fetchone()[0]
        assert timeout == 5000

        # Verify row_factory returns dict-accessible rows
        row = conn.execute("SELECT 1 AS num, 'alpha' AS text;").fetchone()
        assert row["num"] == 1
        assert row["text"] == "alpha"
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_transaction_commits_atomically(test_db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify transaction context manager commits successfully on exit."""
    init_db(test_db_path)
    conn = get_connection(test_db_path)
    try:
        with transaction(conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                "INSERT INTO app_state (key, value) VALUES (?, ?);",
                ("theme", "dracula"),
            )

        cur2 = conn.execute("SELECT value FROM app_state WHERE key = 'theme';")
        assert cur2.fetchone()["value"] == "dracula"
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_transaction_rolls_back_on_exception(test_db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify transaction rolls back all changes when an exception is raised."""
    init_db(test_db_path)
    conn = get_connection(test_db_path)
    try:
        with (
            pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="Simulated crash"),
            transaction(conn) as cur,
        ):
            cur.execute(
                "INSERT INTO app_state (key, value) VALUES (?, ?);",
                ("temp_flag", "should_rollback"),
            )
            raise RuntimeError("Simulated crash")

        cur = conn.execute(
            "SELECT value FROM app_state WHERE key = 'temp_flag';"
        )
        assert cur.fetchone() is None
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_task_title_check_constraint(test_db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify CHECK constraint rejects empty or whitespace-only task titles."""
    init_db(test_db_path)
    conn = get_connection(test_db_path)
    try:
        # Empty title
        with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError), transaction(conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO tasks (
                    id, title, created_at, updated_at
                ) VALUES ('t1', '', '2026-10-16', '2026-10-16');
                """
            )

        # Whitespace-only title
        with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError), transaction(conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO tasks (
                    id, title, created_at, updated_at
                ) VALUES ('t2', '   ', '2026-10-16', '2026-10-16');
                """
            )
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_task_status_check_constraint(test_db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify CHECK constraint rejects invalid task statuses."""
    init_db(test_db_path)
    conn = get_connection(test_db_path)
    try:
        with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError), transaction(conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO tasks (
                    id, title, status, created_at, updated_at
                ) VALUES ('t1', 'Valid', 'InvalidStatus', '2026', '2026');
                """
            )
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_task_critical_level_check_constraint(test_db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify CHECK constraint rejects invalid critical levels."""
    init_db(test_db_path)
    conn = get_connection(test_db_path)
    try:
        with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError), transaction(conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO tasks (
                    id, title, critical_level, created_at, updated_at
                ) VALUES ('t1', 'Valid', 'Urgent', '2026', '2026');
                """
            )
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_foreign_key_enforcement(test_db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify foreign key constraint blocks orphaned reminders."""
    init_db(test_db_path)
    conn = get_connection(test_db_path)
    try:
        with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError), transaction(conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO reminders (
                    id, task_id, remind_at, created_at, updated_at
                ) VALUES (
                    'r1', 'non_existent_task', '2026-10-17', '2026', '2026'
                );
                """
            )
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_partial_unique_index_categories_active(test_db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify categories unique index only applies when is_deleted = 0."""
    init_db(test_db_path)
    conn = get_connection(test_db_path)
    try:
        with transaction(conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO categories (
                    id, name, created_at, updated_at, is_deleted
                ) VALUES ('c1', 'Work', '2026', '2026', 0);
                """
            )

        # Attempt duplicate active category name -> must fail
        with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError), transaction(conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO categories (
                    id, name, created_at, updated_at, is_deleted
                ) VALUES ('c2', 'Work', '2026', '2026', 0);
                """
            )

        # Soft-delete the first category
        with transaction(conn) as cur:
            cur.execute("UPDATE categories SET is_deleted = 1 WHERE id = 'c1';")

        # Now creating another active category with the same name must succeed
        with transaction(conn) as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO categories (
                    id, name, created_at, updated_at, is_deleted
                ) VALUES ('c3', 'Work', '2026', '2026', 0);
                """
            )

        row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM categories WHERE name = 'Work';"
        ).fetchone()
        assert row[0] == 2
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_explain_query_plan_uses_index(test_db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify idx_reminders_due index is utilized by SQLite query planner."""
    init_db(test_db_path)
    conn = get_connection(test_db_path)
    try:
        cur = conn.execute(
            """
            EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN
            SELECT * FROM reminders
            WHERE is_deleted = 0 AND remind_at <= '2026-10-17T00:00:00Z';
            """
        )
        plan = " ".join(row[3] for row in cur.fetchall())
        assert "idx_reminders_due" in plan
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_set_schema_version(test_db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify setting and reading arbitrary schema version."""
    conn = get_connection(test_db_path)
    try:
        assert get_schema_version(conn) == 0
        set_schema_version(conn, 42)
        assert get_schema_version(conn) == 42
    finally:
        conn.close()


========== FILE: ./.tests/test_desktop.py ==========
"""Tests for plus_ultra.desktop module (POSIX single instance lock).

Validates:
- Mutual Exclusion: First process acquires lock; second process fails.
- Re-acquisition: Lock can be acquired again after release.
- Context manager protocol support.
- File security: Lock file created with mode 0o600.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import stat
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from plus_ultra.desktop import SingleInstanceLock


def test_lock_acquire_and_release(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify exclusive lock acquisition and clean release."""
    lock_file = tmp_path / "test.lock"
    lock1 = SingleInstanceLock(lock_file)

    assert lock1.acquire() is True
    assert lock_file.is_file()

    # Verify mode 0o600
    file_mode = stat.S_IMODE(lock_file.stat().st_mode)
    assert file_mode == 0o600, f"Expected 0o600, got {oct(file_mode)}"

    # Second instance attempting same lock must fail (contention)
    lock2 = SingleInstanceLock(lock_file)
    assert lock2.acquire() is False

    # Release first lock
    lock1.release()

    # Now second instance should successfully acquire
    assert lock2.acquire() is True
    lock2.release()


def test_lock_context_manager(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify lock functions cleanly as a context manager."""
    lock_file = tmp_path / "ctx.lock"
    lock1 = SingleInstanceLock(lock_file)

    with lock1 as acquired:
        assert acquired is True
        # Contention while inside context
        lock2 = SingleInstanceLock(lock_file)
        assert lock2.acquire() is False

    # After exiting context, lock is released
    lock3 = SingleInstanceLock(lock_file)
    assert lock3.acquire() is True
    lock3.release()


def test_lock_stores_current_pid(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify lock file records process ID of holding process."""
    lock_file = tmp_path / "pid.lock"
    lock = SingleInstanceLock(lock_file)

    assert lock.acquire() is True
    pid_text = lock_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    assert pid_text.isdigit()
    lock.release()


def test_send_desktop_notification_success(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """Verify notify-send called with argument list and shell=False."""
    from unittest.mock import MagicMock

    from plus_ultra.desktop import send_desktop_notification

    monkeypatch.setattr("shutil.which", lambda _cmd: "/usr/bin/notify-send")
    mock_run = MagicMock()
    monkeypatch.setattr("subprocess.run", mock_run)

    result = send_desktop_notification(
        title="Lab Deadline",
        message="Operating Systems Assignment 2",
        urgency="critical",
        timeout_ms=3000,
    )
    assert result is True
    mock_run.assert_called_once()
    args, kwargs = mock_run.call_args
    cmd = args[0]
    assert cmd[0] == "/usr/bin/notify-send"
    assert "-u" in cmd and "critical" in cmd
    assert "-t" in cmd and "3000" in cmd
    assert "Lab Deadline" in cmd
    assert "Operating Systems Assignment 2" in cmd
    assert kwargs.get("check") is True


def test_send_desktop_notification_missing_executable(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """Verify missing notify-send returns False gracefully."""
    from plus_ultra.desktop import send_desktop_notification

    monkeypatch.setattr("shutil.which", lambda _cmd: None)
    result = send_desktop_notification(title="Test", message="Body")
    assert result is False


def test_send_desktop_notification_subprocess_error(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """Verify subprocess error is caught without crashing."""
    import subprocess

    from plus_ultra.desktop import send_desktop_notification

    monkeypatch.setattr("shutil.which", lambda _cmd: "/usr/bin/notify-send")

    def _mock_raise(*_args: object, **_kwargs: object) -> None:
        raise subprocess.SubprocessError("Command failed")

    monkeypatch.setattr("subprocess.run", _mock_raise)
    result = send_desktop_notification(title="Test", message="Body")
    assert result is False


========== FILE: ./.tests/test_notifications.py ==========
"""Unit tests for notification queue, read/unread states, and popup formatting.

Validates:
- Pure data structure isolation (zero Tkinter dependencies).
- Read/unread status tracking (unread_count, has_unread, mark_all_read).
- Popup width computation and text truncation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from plus_ultra.notifications import CapsuleNotificationQueue


def test_notification_queue_idle_defaults() -> None:
    """Verify queue starts empty and reports zero unread."""
    queue = CapsuleNotificationQueue(min_width=160, max_width=440)
    assert queue.is_empty is True
    assert queue.count == 0
    assert queue.unread_count == 0
    assert queue.has_unread is False
    assert queue.peek() is None


def test_notification_push_and_unread_tracking() -> None:
    """Verify pushing notifications sets unread status and marks read."""
    queue = CapsuleNotificationQueue(min_width=160, max_width=440)

    n1 = queue.push("System Alert", "Kernel updated", icon="⚡")
    assert queue.count == 1
    assert queue.unread_count == 1
    assert queue.has_unread is True
    assert n1.is_read is False

    # Mark single read
    queue.mark_read(n1.id)
    assert queue.unread_count == 0
    assert queue.has_unread is False
    assert n1.is_read is True


def test_notification_multiple_and_mark_all_read() -> None:
    """Verify multiple unread notifications and mark_all_read."""
    queue = CapsuleNotificationQueue(min_width=160, max_width=440)

    queue.push("Task Due", "OS assignment due in 15m", icon="📅")
    queue.push("DSA Practice", "LeetCode daily challenge ready", icon="💻")

    assert queue.count == 2
    assert queue.unread_count == 2
    assert queue.has_unread is True

    queue.mark_all_read()
    assert queue.unread_count == 0
    assert queue.has_unread is False


def test_popup_width_and_text_formatting() -> None:
    """Verify popup width calculation and preview text truncation."""
    queue = CapsuleNotificationQueue()
    n = queue.push("Task 1", "Very detailed reminder text")

    w = queue.compute_popup_width(n)
    assert 200 <= w <= 360

    formatted = queue.format_popup_text(n, max_chars=20)
    assert formatted.endswith("…")
    assert len(formatted) <= 20


def test_notification_queue_update_existing_id() -> None:
    """Verify pushing with existing ID replaces content in-place."""
    queue = CapsuleNotificationQueue(min_width=160, max_width=440)
    queue.push("Old Title", notification_id="fixed_id")
    queue.push("Updated Title", notification_id="fixed_id")

    assert queue.count == 1
    assert queue.peek().title == "Updated Title"


def test_notification_queue_dismiss_and_clear() -> None:
    """Verify dismiss by ID and clear resets all notifications."""
    queue = CapsuleNotificationQueue()
    n1 = queue.push("Msg 1")
    queue.push("Msg 2")
    assert queue.count == 2

    dismissed = queue.dismiss(n1.id)
    assert dismissed == n1
    assert queue.count == 1

    queue.clear()
    assert queue.is_empty is True
    assert queue.count == 0


========== FILE: ./.tests/test_panel.py ==========
"""Unit tests for DashboardPanel, TaskDialog, and UI panel interactions.

Validates:
- 3-column dashboard construction and layout verification.
- Tab switching across 6 workspace categories and placeholders.
- Treeview task rendering, searching, priority filtering, and status filtering.
- Status toggle and soft-delete via context actions.
- TaskDialog prefill, validation, and on_save callback.
- Notification panel Mark All Read and Clear operations triggering callbacks.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import tkinter as tk
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.database import get_connection, init_db
from plus_ultra.notifications import CapsuleNotificationQueue
from plus_ultra.repositories import (
    CategoryRepository,
    CodeRepository,
    CourseRepository,
    IdeaDoubtRepository,
    OpportunityRepository,
    QuickLinkRepository,
    TaskRepository,
)
from plus_ultra.services import (
    CategoryService,
    CodeService,
    CourseService,
    IdeaDoubtService,
    OpportunityService,
    QuickLinkService,
    TaskService,
)
from plus_ultra.ui.panel import DashboardPanel, TaskDialog


def setup_test_services(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> tuple[TaskService, CategoryService, CapsuleNotificationQueue]:
    """Create test services with an isolated SQLite DB."""
    db_path = tmp_path / "panel_test.db"
    init_db(db_path)
    conn = get_connection(db_path)
    cat_repo = CategoryRepository(conn)
    task_repo = TaskRepository(conn)
    task_svc = TaskService(task_repo, cat_repo)
    cat_svc = CategoryService(cat_repo)
    queue = CapsuleNotificationQueue()
    return task_svc, cat_svc, queue


def test_dashboard_panel_layout_and_tabs(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify 3-column construction, tab buttons, and tab switching."""
    task_svc, cat_svc, queue = setup_test_services(tmp_path)
    root = tk.Tk()
    try:
        panel = DashboardPanel(
            parent=root,
            task_service=task_svc,
            category_service=cat_svc,
            notification_queue=queue,
        )

        assert panel.sidebar_col is not None
        assert panel.center_col is not None
        assert panel.notif_col is not None
        assert len(panel.tab_buttons) == 7

        # Default tab is Tasks
        assert panel.active_tab == "Tasks"

        # Switch to Code tab
        panel._switch_tab("Code")
        assert panel.active_tab == "Code"
        assert panel.placeholder_container.winfo_manager() == "pack"
        assert panel.tasks_container.winfo_manager() == ""

        # Switch back to Tasks
        panel._switch_tab("Tasks")
        assert panel.active_tab == "Tasks"
        assert panel.tasks_container.winfo_manager() == "pack"
        assert panel.placeholder_container.winfo_manager() == ""

        # Toggle visibility
        panel.show()
        assert panel.state() == "normal"
        panel.toggle()
        assert panel.state() == "withdrawn"
        panel.toggle()
        assert panel.state() == "normal"

        panel.destroy()
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()


def test_dashboard_treeview_tasks_and_filters(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify task population, searching, and filtering in the Treeview."""
    task_svc, cat_svc, queue = setup_test_services(tmp_path)
    cat = cat_svc.create_category("Academics")
    t1 = task_svc.create_task(
        title="DBMS Assignment",
        category_id=cat.id,
        critical_level="High",
        status="Pending",
    )
    t2 = task_svc.create_task(
        title="Network Security Lab",
        category_id=cat.id,
        critical_level="Low",
        status="Completed",
    )

    root = tk.Tk()
    try:
        panel = DashboardPanel(
            parent=root,
            task_service=task_svc,
            category_service=cat_svc,
            notification_queue=queue,
        )

        # Initial refresh contains both tasks
        children = panel.tree.get_children()
        assert len(children) == 2
        assert t1.id in children
        assert t2.id in children

        # Filter by status: Completed
        panel._set_status_filter("Completed")
        completed_children = panel.tree.get_children()
        assert len(completed_children) == 1
        assert t2.id in completed_children

        # Reset filter
        panel._set_status_filter("All")
        assert len(panel.tree.get_children()) == 2

        # Search query filter
        panel.search_entry.insert(0, "security")
        panel._refresh_tasks()
        searched = panel.tree.get_children()
        assert len(searched) == 1
        assert t2.id in searched

        panel.destroy()
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()

    cat = cat_svc.create_category("Engineering")

    root = tk.Tk()

        saved_data: dict[str, Any] = {}

        def _on_save(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            saved_data.update(data)

        dialog = TaskDialog(
            parent=root,
            categories=[cat],
            on_save=_on_save,
        )

        dialog.title_entry.insert(0, "New Compiler Optimization")
        dialog.cat_combo.set("Engineering")
        dialog.prio_combo.set("High")
        dialog.pace_combo.set("Long-term")

        dialog._handle_save()

        assert saved_data["category_id"] == cat.id
        assert saved_data["critical_level"] == "High"
        assert saved_data["pace_level"] == "Long-term"
        assert saved_data["due_date"] == "2026-11-01"
        assert "LLVM Backend IR pass" in saved_data["description"]
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()


def test_notification_panel_actions(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify notification actions mark all read and clear items."""
    task_svc, cat_svc, queue = setup_test_services(tmp_path)
    queue.push(title="Alert 1", message="Details 1")
    queue.push(title="Alert 2", message="Details 2")
    assert queue.unread_count == 2

    root = tk.Tk()
    try:
        cleared_called = False

        def _on_cleared() -> None:
            nonlocal cleared_called
            cleared_called = True

        panel = DashboardPanel(
            parent=root,
            task_service=task_svc,
            category_service=cat_svc,
            notification_queue=queue,
            on_notifications_cleared=_on_cleared,
        )

        assert panel.notif_listbox.size() == 2

        # Mark all read
        panel._mark_all_notifications_read()
        assert queue.unread_count == 0
        assert cleared_called is True

        # Clear all
        cleared_called = False
        panel._clear_all_notifications()
        assert len(queue.list_all()) == 0
        assert cleared_called is True
        assert panel.notif_listbox.get(0) == "No recent alerts."

        panel.destroy()
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()


def setup_all_test_services(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> tuple[
    TaskService,
    CategoryService,
    CodeService,
    CourseService,
    IdeaDoubtService,
    OpportunityService,
    QuickLinkService,
    CapsuleNotificationQueue,
]:
    """Create all feature services with an isolated SQLite DB."""
    db_path = tmp_path / "panel_all_test.db"
    init_db(db_path)
    conn = get_connection(db_path)

    cat_repo = CategoryRepository(conn)
    task_repo = TaskRepository(conn)
    code_repo = CodeRepository(conn)
    course_repo = CourseRepository(conn)
    idea_repo = IdeaDoubtRepository(conn)
    opp_repo = OpportunityRepository(conn)
    link_repo = QuickLinkRepository(conn)

    task_svc = TaskService(task_repo, cat_repo)
    cat_svc = CategoryService(cat_repo)
    code_svc = CodeService(code_repo)
    course_svc = CourseService(course_repo)
    idea_svc = IdeaDoubtService(idea_repo)
    opp_svc = OpportunityService(opp_repo)
    link_svc = QuickLinkService(link_repo)
    queue = CapsuleNotificationQueue()

    return (
        task_svc,
        cat_svc,
        code_svc,
        course_svc,
        idea_svc,
        opp_svc,
        link_svc,
        queue,
    )

def test_dashboard_course_and_opportunity_actions(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify course module increment and opportunity lifecycle progression."""
    (
        task_svc,
        cat_svc,
        code_svc,
        course_svc,
        idea_svc,
        opp_svc,
        link_svc,
        queue,
    ) = setup_all_test_services(tmp_path)

    course = course_svc.create_course(
        title="Dist Systems", total_modules=10, completed_modules=2
    )
    opp = opp_svc.create_opportunity(
        title="Fellowship", organization="Linux Foundation", status="Saved"
    )
    link = link_svc.create_link(name="Doc", target_uri="https://docs.org")

    root = tk.Tk()
    try:
        panel = DashboardPanel(
            parent=root,
            task_service=task_svc,
            category_service=cat_svc,
            notification_queue=queue,
            code_service=code_svc,
            course_service=course_svc,
            idea_doubt_service=idea_svc,
            opportunity_service=opp_svc,
            quick_link_service=link_svc,
        )

        # Increment course
        panel._inc_course(course.id)
        c_updated = course_svc.get_course(course.id)
        assert c_updated is not None
        assert c_updated.completed_modules == 3

        # Advance opportunity
        panel._advance_opp(opp.id)
        o_updated = opp_svc.get_opportunity(opp.id)
        assert o_updated is not None
        assert o_updated.status == "Applied"

        # Record link click
        link_svc.record_click(link.id)
        l_updated = link_svc.get_link(link.id)
        assert l_updated is not None
        assert l_updated.click_count == 1

        panel.destroy()
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()


========== FILE: ./.tests/test_refinements.py ==========
"""Unit tests for UI refinements: DateTimePicker, PS/I subtabs, and capsule."""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import datetime
import tkinter as tk
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.database import get_connection, init_db
from plus_ultra.network import NetworkStatusMonitor, is_network_online
from plus_ultra.repositories import (
    IdeaDoubtRepository,
)
from plus_ultra.services import (
    IdeaDoubtService,
)
from plus_ultra.ui.components.datetime_picker import DateTimePicker
from plus_ultra.ui.dialogs import CodeDialog, OpportunityDialog, TaskDialog
from plus_ultra.ui.floating import FloatingWidget
from plus_ultra.ui.views.ideas_view import IdeasView


def test_datetime_picker_interactive_actions() -> None:
    """Verify DateTimePicker Now, presets, time updates, and day steppers."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    try:
        picker = DateTimePicker(root, include_time=True)

        # 1. Test set_now
        picker.set_now()
        val = picker.get_value()
        today_prefix = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        assert val.startswith(today_prefix)
        assert len(val.split()) == 2

        # 2. Test set_time
        picker.set_time("15:30")
        assert picker.get_value() == f"{today_prefix} 15:30"

        # 3. Test add_days_from_today
        picker.add_days_from_today(2)
        target = (
            datetime.date.today() + datetime.timedelta(days=2)
        ).isoformat()
        assert picker.get_value().startswith(target)

        # 4. Test step_days
        picker.set_value(f"{today_prefix} 18:00")
        picker.step_days(1)
        expected_next = (
            datetime.date.today() + datetime.timedelta(days=1)
        ).isoformat()
        assert picker.get_value() == f"{expected_next} 18:00"

        picker.destroy()
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()


def test_datetime_picker_date_only_mode() -> None:
    """Verify DateTimePicker date-only mode for deadlines."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    try:
        picker = DateTimePicker(root, include_time=False)
        picker.set_now()
        val = picker.get_value()
        today = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        assert val == today
        assert " " not in val

        picker.add_days_from_today(5)
        expected = (
            datetime.date.today() + datetime.timedelta(days=5)
        ).isoformat()
        assert picker.get_value() == expected

        picker.destroy()
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()

        assert view.add_btn.cget("text") == "+ Add Idea"
