def test_floating_widget_dynamic_capsule_expansion() -> None:
    """Verify FloatingWidget expands width on unread alerts and restores."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    try:
        widget = FloatingWidget(root=root)
        initial_w = widget.width

        # Post an unread notification -> triggers expansion
        widget.post_notification(title="System Alert", message="Disk space low")
        widget.root.update_idletasks()
        widget._update_bell_state()

        assert widget.width > initial_w
        assert widget.notifications.has_unread is True

        # Mark all read -> restores default compact width
        widget.mark_all_read()
        widget.root.update_idletasks()
        widget._update_bell_state()

        assert widget.width == initial_w
        assert widget.notifications.has_unread is False

        widget.close()
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()


def test_network_monitor_probe() -> None:
    """Verify NetworkStatusMonitor puts probe results in queue without crash."""
    monitor = NetworkStatusMonitor()
    assert monitor.get_result() is None
    # Synchronous probe returns boolean
    res = is_network_online(timeout=0.8)
    assert isinstance(res, bool)


========== FILE: ./.tests/test_repository.py ==========
"""Unit tests for repository data access layer and DBMS integrity rules.

Validates DBMS concepts:
- Parameterized SQL and protection against SQL injection.
- Atomic cascading soft deletes (task -> reminders, category -> tasks nullify).
- Query optimization and index filtering (active only, due reminders).
- Runtime machine state isolation in app_state.
- Edge cases (Unicode, very long strings, whitespace, foreign keys).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from plus_ultra.database import get_connection, init_db
from plus_ultra.repositories import (
    AppStateRepository,
    CategoryRepository,
    ReminderRepository,
    TaskRepository,
)


@pytest.fixture
def repos(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> tuple[
    CategoryRepository,
    TaskRepository,
    ReminderRepository,
    AppStateRepository,
    sqlite3.Connection,
]:
    """Initialize a test database and return instantiated repositories."""
    db_path = tmp_path / "test_repo.db"
    init_db(db_path)
    conn = get_connection(db_path)
    yield (
        CategoryRepository(conn),
        TaskRepository(conn),
        ReminderRepository(conn),
        AppStateRepository(conn),
        conn,
    )
    conn.close()


def test_category_crud_and_trimming(
    repos: tuple[
        CategoryRepository,
        TaskRepository,
        ReminderRepository,
        AppStateRepository,
        sqlite3.Connection,
    ],
) -> None:
    """Test category creation, trimming, lookup, updating, and listing."""
    cat_repo = repos[0]
    cat = cat_repo.create("  Academics 🎓  ", color="#3b82f6")
    assert cat.name == "Academics 🎓"
    assert cat.color == "#3b82f6"
    assert cat.is_deleted == 0

    fetched = cat_repo.get_by_id(cat.id)
    assert fetched is not None
    assert fetched.name == "Academics 🎓"

    updated = cat_repo.update(cat.id, "Studies", "#10b981")
    assert updated is not None
    assert updated.name == "Studies"
    assert updated.color == "#10b981"

    active_cats = cat_repo.list_active()
    assert len(active_cats) == 1
    assert active_cats[0].id == cat.id


def test_category_soft_delete_and_name_reuse(
    repos: tuple[
        CategoryRepository,
        TaskRepository,
        ReminderRepository,
        AppStateRepository,
        sqlite3.Connection,
    ],
) -> None:
    """Verify unique constraint on active categories and name reuse."""
    cat_repo = repos[0]
    c1 = cat_repo.create("Work")

    # Creating duplicate active name must raise IntegrityError
    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
        cat_repo.create("Work")

    # Soft-delete the category
    assert cat_repo.soft_delete(c1.id) is True
    assert cat_repo.list_active() == []

    # Can now reuse the name for a new active category
    c2 = cat_repo.create("Work")
    assert c2.id != c1.id
    assert len(cat_repo.list_active()) == 1


def test_category_soft_delete_cascades_null_to_tasks(
    repos: tuple[
        CategoryRepository,
        TaskRepository,
        ReminderRepository,
        AppStateRepository,
        sqlite3.Connection,
    ],
) -> None:
    """Verify soft-deleting category sets linked tasks' category_id to NULL."""
    cat_repo, task_repo = repos[0], repos[1]
    cat = cat_repo.create("Operating Systems")
    task = task_repo.create("Paging Lab", category_id=cat.id)
    assert task.category_id == cat.id

    # Soft delete category
    assert cat_repo.soft_delete(cat.id) is True

    # Linked task must have category_id nullified
    reloaded_task = task_repo.get_by_id(task.id)
    assert reloaded_task is not None
    assert reloaded_task.category_id is None


def test_task_crud_and_custom_attributes(
    repos: tuple[
        CategoryRepository,
        TaskRepository,
        ReminderRepository,
        AppStateRepository,
        sqlite3.Connection,
    ],
) -> None:
    """Verify task creation with custom fields and updating."""
    task_repo = repos[1]
    task = task_repo.create(
        title="Implement WAL Mode",
        description="Verify concurrency with PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL",
        due_date="2026-10-20T12:00:00Z",
        status="Pending",
        task_label="College",
        critical_level="High",
        pace_level="Short-term",
        is_google_task=True,
    )

    assert task.title == "Implement WAL Mode"
    assert task.critical_level == "High"
    assert task.pace_level == "Short-term"
    assert task.is_google_task is True

    updated = task_repo.update(
        task.id,
        status="Completed",
        critical_level="Low",
        description="Updated description",
    )
    assert updated is not None
    assert updated.status == "Completed"
    assert updated.critical_level == "Low"
    assert updated.description == "Updated description"


def test_task_filtering(
    repos: tuple[
        CategoryRepository,
        TaskRepository,
        ReminderRepository,
        AppStateRepository,
        sqlite3.Connection,
    ],
) -> None:
    """Verify filtering tasks by category and status."""
    cat_repo, task_repo = repos[0], repos[1]
    cat1 = cat_repo.create("Cat1")
    cat2 = cat_repo.create("Cat2")

    task_repo.create("T1", category_id=cat1.id, status="Pending")
    task_repo.create("T2", category_id=cat1.id, status="Completed")
    task_repo.create("T3", category_id=cat2.id, status="Pending")

    # Filter by category
    cat1_tasks = task_repo.list_active(category_id=cat1.id)
    assert len(cat1_tasks) == 2

    # Filter by status
    pending_tasks = task_repo.list_active(status="Pending")
    assert len(pending_tasks) == 2

    # Filter by both
    cat1_pending = task_repo.list_active(category_id=cat1.id, status="Pending")
    assert len(cat1_pending) == 1
    assert cat1_pending[0].title == "T1"


def test_task_soft_delete_cascades_to_reminders(
    repos: tuple[
        CategoryRepository,
        TaskRepository,
        ReminderRepository,
        AppStateRepository,
        sqlite3.Connection,
    ],
) -> None:
    """Verify soft-deleting a task also soft-deletes its reminders."""
    task_repo, reminder_repo = repos[1], repos[2]
    task = task_repo.create("Study DBMS Normalization")
    rem1 = reminder_repo.create(task.id, "2026-10-18T09:00:00Z")
    rem2 = reminder_repo.create(task.id, "2026-10-18T10:00:00Z")

    assert reminder_repo.get_by_id(rem1.id).is_deleted == 0
    assert reminder_repo.get_by_id(rem2.id).is_deleted == 0

    # Soft delete task
    assert task_repo.soft_delete(task.id) is True

    # Check task is marked deleted
    assert task_repo.get_by_id(task.id).is_deleted == 1
    assert task_repo.list_active() == []

    # Check reminders are also soft-deleted
    assert reminder_repo.get_by_id(rem1.id).is_deleted == 1
    assert reminder_repo.get_by_id(rem2.id).is_deleted == 1


def test_reminder_list_due_filtering(
    repos: tuple[
        CategoryRepository,
        TaskRepository,
        ReminderRepository,
        AppStateRepository,
        sqlite3.Connection,
    ],
) -> None:
    """Verify list_due filters by time, task deletion, and fired state."""
    task_repo, reminder_repo = repos[1], repos[2]
    task1 = task_repo.create("Task 1")
    task2 = task_repo.create("Task 2")

    r1 = reminder_repo.create(task1.id, "2026-10-17T08:00:00Z")
    r2 = reminder_repo.create(task1.id, "2026-10-17T09:00:00Z")
    r3 = reminder_repo.create(task2.id, "2026-10-17T08:30:00Z")
    r_future = reminder_repo.create(task2.id, "2026-10-17T12:00:00Z")

    # Due at 09:30: r1, r2, r3 (r_future excluded by timestamp)
    due = reminder_repo.list_due("2026-10-17T09:30:00Z")
    due_ids = [r.id for r in due]
    assert due_ids == [r1.id, r3.id, r2.id]  # Ordered by remind_at ASC
    assert r_future.id not in due_ids

    # Mark r1 as fired -> excluded from subsequent queries
    reminder_repo.mark_fired(r1.id)
    assert reminder_repo.is_fired(r1.id) is True
    due_after_fire = reminder_repo.list_due("2026-10-17T09:30:00Z")
    assert r1.id not in [r.id for r in due_after_fire]

    # Soft delete task2 -> r3 must be excluded because parent task is deleted
    task_repo.soft_delete(task2.id)
    due_after_task_del = reminder_repo.list_due("2026-10-17T09:30:00Z")
    assert [r.id for r in due_after_task_del] == [r2.id]


def test_app_state_repository(
    repos: tuple[
        CategoryRepository,
        TaskRepository,
        ReminderRepository,
        AppStateRepository,
        sqlite3.Connection,
    ],
) -> None:
    """Verify key-value persistence and widget position saving/retrieval."""
    app_state = repos[3]

    assert app_state.get("non_existent", "default_val") == "default_val"
    assert app_state.get_widget_position() is None

    app_state.set("sync_token", "abc-123")
    assert app_state.get("sync_token") == "abc-123"

    # Upsert existing key
    app_state.set("sync_token", "def-456")
    assert app_state.get("sync_token") == "def-456"

    # Widget position
    app_state.set_widget_position(650, 42)
    pos = app_state.get_widget_position()
    assert pos == (650, 42)


def test_app_state_handles_corrupt_coordinates(
    repos: tuple[
        CategoryRepository,
        TaskRepository,
        ReminderRepository,
        AppStateRepository,
        sqlite3.Connection,
    ],
) -> None:
    """Verify get_widget_position returns None if coordinates are corrupt."""
    app_state = repos[3]
    app_state.set("widget_x", "not_a_number")
    app_state.set("widget_y", "50")
    assert app_state.get_widget_position() is None


def test_unicode_and_long_strings(
    repos: tuple[
        CategoryRepository,
        TaskRepository,
        ReminderRepository,
        AppStateRepository,
        sqlite3.Connection,
    ],
) -> None:
    """Verify handling of Unicode, emojis, and very long text."""
    cat_repo, task_repo = repos[0], repos[1]
    long_desc = "X" * 10000
    unicode_title = "こんにちは 世界 🚀🔥"

    cat = cat_repo.create("プロジェクト 📁")
    task = task_repo.create(
        title=unicode_title,
        category_id=cat.id,
        description=long_desc,
    )

    reloaded = task_repo.get_by_id(task.id)
    assert reloaded is not None
    assert reloaded.title == unicode_title
    assert reloaded.description == long_desc


========== FILE: ./.tests/test_scheduler.py ==========
"""Unit tests for ReminderScheduler and ReminderService.

Validates:
- Pure reminder evaluation logic using deterministic fake clocks.
- Due reminder filtering excludes reminders due in the future.
- Due reminder filtering excludes reminders belonging to soft-deleted tasks.
- Atomic fire marking prevents duplicate dispatches (exactly-once semantics).
- Respects reminders_enabled configuration flag.
- Background thread lifecycle: start, thread execution, and clean shutdown.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest

from plus_ultra.database import get_connection, init_db
from plus_ultra.repositories import (
    CategoryRepository,
    ReminderRepository,
    TaskRepository,
)
from plus_ultra.scheduler import ReminderScheduler
from plus_ultra.services import ReminderService, TaskService


@pytest.fixture
def test_setup(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> tuple[TaskService, ReminderService]:
    """Initialize DB and return services for testing."""
    db_path = tmp_path / "scheduler_test.db"
    init_db(db_path)
    conn = get_connection(db_path)
    cat_repo = CategoryRepository(conn)
    task_repo = TaskRepository(conn)
    rem_repo = ReminderRepository(conn)
    task_svc = TaskService(task_repo, cat_repo)
    rem_svc = ReminderService(rem_repo, task_repo)
    return task_svc, rem_svc


def test_scheduler_evaluates_due_reminders(
    test_setup: tuple[TaskService, ReminderService],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """Verify reminders due at or before tick time are evaluated and fired."""
    task_svc, rem_svc = test_setup
    task = task_svc.create_task("OS Memory Lab", critical_level="High")

    # Schedule one past reminder and one future reminder
    r_past = rem_svc.create_reminder(task.id, "2026-10-16T10:00:00Z")
    r_future = rem_svc.create_reminder(task.id, "2026-10-16T12:00:00Z")

    mock_notify = MagicMock()
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "plus_ultra.desktop.send_desktop_notification", mock_notify
    )

    mock_callback = MagicMock()
    scheduler = ReminderScheduler(
        reminder_service=rem_svc,
        on_reminder_due=mock_callback,
        desktop_notify=True,
    )

    # Evaluate at 11:00:00Z: only past reminder should fire
    fired = scheduler.tick(now_iso="2026-10-16T11:00:00Z")
    assert len(fired) == 1
    assert fired[0][0].id == r_past.id
    assert fired[0][1].id == task.id

    # Verify desktop notification called with task info
    mock_notify.assert_called_once()
    assert "OS Memory Lab" in mock_notify.call_args[1]["title"]
    assert mock_notify.call_args[1]["urgency"] == "critical"

    # Verify in-app UI callback called
    mock_callback.assert_called_once_with(task, r_past)

    # Verify fired reminder is recorded in reminder_fires
    assert rem_svc.is_fired(r_past.id) is True
    assert rem_svc.is_fired(r_future.id) is False

    # Second tick at 11:30:00Z: r_past must NOT fire again (exactly once)
    fired_second = scheduler.tick(now_iso="2026-10-16T11:30:00Z")
    assert len(fired_second) == 0


def test_scheduler_excludes_soft_deleted_tasks(
    test_setup: tuple[TaskService, ReminderService],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """Verify reminders for soft-deleted tasks are excluded from evaluation."""
    task_svc, rem_svc = test_setup
    task = task_svc.create_task("Dropped Course Project")
    reminder = rem_svc.create_reminder(task.id, "2026-10-16T09:00:00Z")

    # Soft delete the task
    assert task_svc.soft_delete_task(task.id) is True

    mock_notify = MagicMock()
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "plus_ultra.desktop.send_desktop_notification", mock_notify
    )

    scheduler = ReminderScheduler(
        reminder_service=rem_svc,
        desktop_notify=True,
    )

    fired = scheduler.tick(now_iso="2026-10-16T10:00:00Z")
    assert len(fired) == 0
    mock_notify.assert_not_called()
    assert rem_svc.is_fired(reminder.id) is False


def test_scheduler_respects_settings_disabled(
    test_setup: tuple[TaskService, ReminderService],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """Verify tick skips firing when reminders_enabled is False in settings."""
    task_svc, rem_svc = test_setup
    task = task_svc.create_task("Study Compiler Pass")
    rem_svc.create_reminder(task.id, "2026-10-16T08:00:00Z")

    # Mock settings with reminders_enabled=False
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "plus_ultra.settings.load_effective_settings",
        lambda: {"reminders_enabled": False},
    )

    mock_notify = MagicMock()
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "plus_ultra.desktop.send_desktop_notification", mock_notify
    )

    scheduler = ReminderScheduler(
        reminder_service=rem_svc,
        desktop_notify=True,
    )

    fired = scheduler.tick(now_iso="2026-10-16T10:00:00Z")
    assert len(fired) == 0
    mock_notify.assert_not_called()


def test_scheduler_thread_lifecycle(
    test_setup: tuple[TaskService, ReminderService],
) -> None:
    """Verify background worker thread starts and shuts down cleanly."""
    _, rem_svc = test_setup
    scheduler = ReminderScheduler(
        reminder_service=rem_svc,
        poll_interval_s=0.05,
    )

    assert not scheduler.is_running
    scheduler.start()
    assert scheduler.is_running

    # Idempotent start
    scheduler.start()
    assert scheduler.is_running

    # Clean stop
    scheduler.stop(timeout=1.0)
    assert not scheduler.is_running


========== FILE: ./.tests/test_services.py ==========
"""Unit tests for TaskService and CategoryService.

Validates:
- Pure business logic and validation rules (empty title, invalid status/levels).
- Status toggling state transitions.
- Category lifecycle and cascading nullification to tasks.
- Advanced querying: filtering by category, status, priority, and text search.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from plus_ultra.database import get_connection, init_db
from plus_ultra.repositories import (
    CategoryRepository,
    ReminderRepository,
    TaskRepository,
)
from plus_ultra.services import (
    CategoryService,
    ReminderService,
    TaskService,
)


@pytest.fixture
def services(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> tuple[TaskService, CategoryService]:
    """Provide initialized TaskService and CategoryService with a temp DB."""
    db_path = tmp_path / "test_services.db"
    init_db(db_path)
    conn = get_connection(db_path)
    cat_repo = CategoryRepository(conn)
    task_repo = TaskRepository(conn)
    return TaskService(task_repo, cat_repo), CategoryService(cat_repo)


def test_task_service_create_and_validation(
    services: tuple[TaskService, CategoryService],
) -> None:
    """Verify task creation and input validation rules."""
    task_svc, cat_svc = services
    cat = cat_svc.create_category("Academics")

    task = task_svc.create_task(
        title="Operating Systems Lab",
        category_id=cat.id,
        description="Paging & Virtual Memory",
        critical_level="High",
        pace_level="Short-term",
    )
    assert task.title == "Operating Systems Lab"
    assert task.category_id == cat.id
    assert task.status == "Pending"
    assert task.critical_level == "High"

    # Empty title raises ValueError
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="empty or whitespace-only"):
        task_svc.create_task("   ")

    # Invalid status raises ValueError
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Invalid status"):
        task_svc.create_task("Valid Title", status="Unknown")

    # Invalid critical level raises ValueError
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Invalid critical level"):
        task_svc.create_task("Valid Title", critical_level="Urgent")

    # Invalid pace level raises ValueError
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Invalid pace level"):
        task_svc.create_task("Valid Title", pace_level="Infinite")

    # Non-existent category raises ValueError
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="does not exist"):
        task_svc.create_task("Valid Title", category_id="bad-uuid")


def test_task_service_toggle_status(
    services: tuple[TaskService, CategoryService],
) -> None:
    """Verify cycling status between Pending and Completed."""
    task_svc = services[0]
    task = task_svc.create_task("Review Code")
    assert task.status == "Pending"

    # Toggle to Completed
    toggled = task_svc.toggle_status(task.id)
    assert toggled is not None
    assert toggled.status == "Completed"

    # Toggle back to Pending
    toggled2 = task_svc.toggle_status(task.id)
    assert toggled2 is not None
    assert toggled2.status == "Pending"


def test_task_service_search_and_filters(
    services: tuple[TaskService, CategoryService],
) -> None:
    """Verify multi-criteria filtering and text search."""
    task_svc, cat_svc = services
    c_work = cat_svc.create_category("Work")
    c_study = cat_svc.create_category("Study")

    t1 = task_svc.create_task(
        "Build SQLite Layer",
        category_id=c_work.id,
        description="WAL mode and ACID",
        critical_level="High",
        status="Completed",
    )
    t2 = task_svc.create_task(
        "Study DBMS Normalization",
        category_id=c_study.id,
        description="1NF to BCNF",
        critical_level="Medium",
        status="Pending",
    )
    t3 = task_svc.create_task(
        "Prepare Resume",
        category_id=c_work.id,
        description="Tailor for internships",
        critical_level="High",
        status="Pending",
    )

    # Filter by category
    work_tasks = task_svc.list_tasks(category_id=c_work.id)
    assert {t.id for t in work_tasks} == {t1.id, t3.id}

    # Filter by status
    pending_tasks = task_svc.list_tasks(status="Pending")
    assert {t.id for t in pending_tasks} == {t2.id, t3.id}

    # Filter by critical level
    high_tasks = task_svc.list_tasks(critical_level="High")
    assert {t.id for t in high_tasks} == {t1.id, t3.id}

    # Text search on title
    search_sqlite = task_svc.list_tasks(search_query="sqlite")
    assert len(search_sqlite) == 1
    assert search_sqlite[0].id == t1.id

    # Text search on description (case-insensitive)
    search_bcnf = task_svc.list_tasks(search_query="bcnf")
    assert len(search_bcnf) == 1
    assert search_bcnf[0].id == t2.id


def test_category_service_lifecycle_and_cascade(
    services: tuple[TaskService, CategoryService],
) -> None:
    """Verify category creation, updating, and soft-delete nullification."""
    task_svc, cat_svc = services
    cat = cat_svc.create_category("Personal", color="#ec4899")
    task = task_svc.create_task("Workout", category_id=cat.id)
    assert task.category_id == cat.id

    # Update category
    updated = cat_svc.update_category(cat.id, "Health & Fitness", "#10b981")
    assert updated is not None
    assert updated.name == "Health & Fitness"

    # Soft delete category
    assert cat_svc.soft_delete_category(cat.id) is True
    assert cat_svc.get_category(cat.id) is None

    # Linked task must still exist, with category_id set to None
    reloaded_task = task_svc.get_task(task.id)
    assert reloaded_task is not None
    assert reloaded_task.category_id is None


def test_reminder_service_lifecycle_and_validation(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify ReminderService validation, creation, and due querying."""
    db_path = tmp_path / "rem_svc_test.db"
    init_db(db_path)
    conn = get_connection(db_path)
    cat_repo = CategoryRepository(conn)
    task_repo = TaskRepository(conn)
    rem_repo = ReminderRepository(conn)
    task_svc = TaskService(task_repo, cat_repo)
    rem_svc = ReminderService(rem_repo, task_repo)

    task = task_svc.create_task("OS Scheduling Exam")

    # Empty task_id or non-existent task raises ValueError
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="does not exist or is deleted"):
        rem_svc.create_reminder("bad-uuid", "2026-10-16T10:00:00Z")

    # Empty remind_at raises ValueError
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="cannot be empty"):
        rem_svc.create_reminder(task.id, "   ")

    # Happy path creation
    rem = rem_svc.create_reminder(task.id, "2026-10-16T10:00:00Z")
    assert rem.task_id == task.id
    assert rem.remind_at == "2026-10-16T10:00:00Z"

    # Due query before 10:00:00Z
    assert len(rem_svc.get_due_reminders("2026-10-16T09:59:59Z")) == 0

    # Due query at or after 10:00:00Z
    due = rem_svc.get_due_reminders("2026-10-16T10:00:00Z")
    assert len(due) == 1
    assert due[0][0].id == rem.id
    assert due[0][1].id == task.id

    # Mark fired
    rem_svc.mark_fired(rem.id)
    assert rem_svc.is_fired(rem.id) is True

    # After firing, it is excluded from get_due_reminders
    assert len(rem_svc.get_due_reminders("2026-10-16T11:00:00Z")) == 0

    # Soft delete reminder
    rem2 = rem_svc.create_reminder(task.id, "2026-10-16T12:00:00Z")
    assert rem_svc.soft_delete_reminder(rem2.id) is True
    assert len(rem_svc.get_due_reminders("2026-10-16T13:00:00Z")) == 0


========== FILE: ./.tests/test_settings.py ==========
"""Tests for plus_ultra.settings module.

Validates:
- Happy paths: defaults loading, overriding settings, resetting single/all.
- Type safety: strict type matching (bool is not int, int is not bool).
- Defensive recovery: malformed JSON and non-object JSON quarantined without
  crashing, returning safe defaults.
- Security & POSIX permissions: mode 0o600 on file, mode 0o700 on config dir.
- Rejection of unknown keys and machine state (widget_x/y).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import stat
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from plus_ultra import config, settings


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def isolated_config_dir(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: Path
) -> Path:
    """Isolate configuration directory for each test run."""
    cfg_dir = tmp_path / "config"
    monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", str(cfg_dir))
    settings.reload()
    return cfg_dir / config.APP_NAME


def test_default_settings_keys_and_values() -> None:
    """Verify default settings match the locked schema."""
    defaults = settings.load_default_settings()

    assert defaults["settings_version"] == 1
    assert defaults["always_on_top"] is True
    assert defaults["glow_enabled"] is True
    assert defaults["glow_pulse_period_ms"] == 3000
    assert defaults["reminders_enabled"] is True
    assert set(defaults.keys()) == {
        "settings_version",
        "always_on_top",
        "glow_enabled",
        "glow_pulse_period_ms",
        "reminders_enabled",
    }


def test_effective_settings_fallback_to_defaults() -> None:
    """When no user file exists, effective settings are defaults."""
    effective = settings.load_effective_settings()
    defaults = settings.load_default_settings()
    assert effective == defaults


def test_update_setting_persists_and_enforces_0600() -> None:
    """Updating a setting persists atomically with mode 0o600."""
    updated = settings.update_user_setting("always_on_top", False)
    assert updated["always_on_top"] is False

    settings_file = config.get_user_settings_path()
    assert settings_file.is_file()

    file_mode = stat.S_IMODE(settings_file.stat().st_mode)
    assert file_mode == 0o600, f"Expected 0o600, got {oct(file_mode)}"

    # Reload fresh to verify persistence
    reloaded = settings.reload()
    assert reloaded["always_on_top"] is False
    assert reloaded["glow_pulse_period_ms"] == 3000


def test_reset_setting_reverts_to_default() -> None:
    """Resetting a single key removes override and restores default."""
    settings.update_user_setting("reminders_enabled", False)
    assert settings.load_effective_settings()["reminders_enabled"] is False

    settings.reset_setting("reminders_enabled")
    effective = settings.load_effective_settings()
    assert effective["reminders_enabled"] is True


def test_reset_all_settings_clears_all_overrides() -> None:
    """reset_all_settings() removes overrides and restores all defaults."""
    settings.update_user_setting("always_on_top", False)
    settings.update_user_setting("glow_pulse_period_ms", 1500)

    settings.reset_all_settings()
    effective = settings.load_effective_settings()
    defaults = settings.load_default_settings()
    assert effective == defaults


def test_unknown_key_rejected_in_api() -> None:
    """Unknown keys cannot be set via update_user_setting."""
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        settings.update_user_setting("unknown_option", 123)

    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        settings.reset_setting("unknown_option")


def test_machine_state_rejected_in_settings() -> None:
    """Machine state like widget coordinates cannot be saved in settings."""
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        settings.update_user_setting("widget_x", 400)

    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        settings.update_user_setting("widget_y", 200)


def test_type_safety_strict_equality() -> None:
    """Types must match exactly: bool is not int and int is not bool."""
    # Attempting to set int where bool is expected
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        settings.update_user_setting("always_on_top", 1)

    # Attempting to set str where bool is expected
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        settings.update_user_setting("always_on_top", "true")

    # Attempting to set bool where int is expected
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        settings.update_user_setting("glow_pulse_period_ms", True)

    # Attempting to set str where int is expected
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        settings.update_user_setting("glow_pulse_period_ms", "3000")


def test_external_file_with_unknown_and_invalid_types(
    isolated_config_dir: Path,
) -> None:
    """Invalid user file ignores unknown keys and mismatched types."""
    settings_file = config.get_user_settings_path()
    settings_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings_file.write_text(
        '{"unknown_key": "val", "always_on_top": 1, "reminders_enabled": false}'
    )

    effective = settings.reload()
    assert "unknown_key" not in effective
    # always_on_top had int (1), expected bool -> ignored, kept default True
    assert effective["always_on_top"] is True
    # reminders_enabled had bool (false), expected bool -> accepted False
    assert effective["reminders_enabled"] is False


def test_corrupt_json_quarantined_without_crashing(
    isolated_config_dir: Path,
) -> None:
    """Corrupted JSON is quarantined to .bad-<timestamp> and defaults used."""
    settings_file = config.get_user_settings_path()
    settings_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings_file.write_text("NOT_VALID_JSON{::: broken")

    effective = settings.reload()
    assert effective == settings.load_default_settings()

    # Original file should be gone (renamed)
    assert not settings_file.exists()

    # Quarantined file should exist
    quarantined = list(settings_file.parent.glob(f"{settings_file.name}.bad-*"))
    assert len(quarantined) == 1
    assert "NOT_VALID_JSON" in quarantined[0].read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_non_dict_json_quarantined(
    isolated_config_dir: Path,
) -> None:
    """Top-level JSON array is quarantined as invalid structure."""
    settings_file = config.get_user_settings_path()
    settings_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings_file.write_text("[1, 2, 3]")

    effective = settings.reload()
    assert effective == settings.load_default_settings()

    quarantined = list(settings_file.parent.glob(f"{settings_file.name}.bad-*"))
    assert len(quarantined) == 1


========== FILE: ./.tests/test_ui.py ==========
"""Tests for plus_ultra.ui (theme tokens, FloatingWidget, and popup capsule).

Validates:
- Theme tokens: Capsule dimensions, color palette, typography.
- FloatingWidget instantiation: borderless window flags, geometry placement.
- Mathematical capsule points and perimeter trajectory generation.
- Hover-driven Animated Border Beam (runs only on hover).
- Orange bell indicator visibility based on unread notification state.
- Position saving and restoration callbacks.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import tkinter as tk
from typing import Any

from plus_ultra.ui.floating import (
    FloatingWidget,
    generate_capsule_points,
    generate_perimeter_path,
)
from plus_ultra.ui.theme import GEOMETRY, THEME


def test_theme_and_geometry_defaults() -> None:
    """Verify default styling tokens for the floating capsule."""
    assert GEOMETRY.min_width == 320
    assert GEOMETRY.max_width == 880
    assert GEOMETRY.width == 320
    assert GEOMETRY.height == 72
    assert GEOMETRY.radius == 36
    assert GEOMETRY.top_offset == 16

    assert THEME.hover_border == "#00e5ff"
    assert THEME.idle_border == "#2b3448"
    assert THEME.window_bg == "#12151c"
    assert THEME.beam_body == "#00e5ff"
    assert THEME.beam_head == "#ffffff"
    assert THEME.bell_orange == "#ff9800"


def test_capsule_points_and_perimeter_generators() -> None:
    """Verify mathematical capsule point and perimeter generation."""
    points = generate_capsule_points(320, 72, 36, steps=16)
    assert len(points) > 20
    xs = points[0::2]
    ys = points[1::2]
    assert min(xs) >= 0.0 and max(xs) <= 320.0
    assert min(ys) >= 0.0 and max(ys) <= 72.0

    path = generate_perimeter_path(320, 72, 36)
    assert len(path) > 50
    for px, py in path:
        assert 0.0 <= px <= 320.0
        assert 0.0 <= py <= 72.0


def test_floating_widget_creation_and_click() -> None:
    """Verify FloatingWidget initializes canvas, beam, and bell."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    try:
        clicked = False

        def _on_click() -> None:
            nonlocal clicked
            clicked = True

        widget = FloatingWidget(root=root, on_click_callback=_on_click)

        assert widget.canvas is not None
        assert widget.capsule_id is not None
        assert widget.beam_body_id is not None
        assert widget.beam_head_id is not None
        assert widget.idle_text_id is not None
        assert widget.bell_id is not None
        assert widget.always_on_top is True

        # Initially bell is hidden
        assert widget.canvas.itemcget(widget.bell_id, "state") == "hidden"

        # Test click triggering
        widget._dragged = False
        dummy_event: tk.Event[Any] = tk.Event()
        widget._on_button_release(dummy_event)
        assert clicked is True
        assert widget._active_toggle is True
        widget.close()
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()


def test_floating_widget_hover_runs_beam() -> None:
    """Verify beam runs exclusively when mouse hovers over the capsule."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    try:
        widget = FloatingWidget(root=root)
        dummy_event: tk.Event[Any] = tk.Event()

        # Idle: not hovered, beam timer stopped
        assert widget._is_hovered is False

        # Hover entrance: starts beam
        widget._on_enter(dummy_event)
        assert widget._is_hovered is True
        assert widget._beam_timer_id is not None

        # Hover exit: stops beam and clears beam coords
        widget._on_leave(dummy_event)
        assert widget._is_hovered is False
        assert widget._beam_timer_id is None
        assert widget.canvas.coords(widget.beam_body_id) == [0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0]

        widget.close()
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()


def test_floating_widget_unread_bell_indicator() -> None:
    """Verify orange bell appears on unread notification and hides when read."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    try:
        widget = FloatingWidget(root=root)
        assert widget.notifications.has_unread is False
        assert widget.canvas.itemcget(widget.bell_id, "state") == "hidden"

        # Post notification -> unread bell visible
        widget.post_notification("Assignment Due", "OS Lab 3 due in 30m")
        assert widget.notifications.has_unread is True
        widget._update_bell_state()
        assert widget.canvas.itemcget(widget.bell_id, "state") == "normal"

        # Mark all read -> bell hides
        widget.mark_all_read()
        assert widget.notifications.has_unread is False
        widget._update_bell_state()
        assert widget.canvas.itemcget(widget.bell_id, "state") == "hidden"

        widget.close()
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()


def test_floating_widget_drag_calculation() -> None:
    """Verify drag offset calculation updates window geometry."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    try:
        widget = FloatingWidget(root=root)

        press_event: tk.Event[Any] = tk.Event()
        press_event.x_root = 200
        press_event.y_root = 100
        widget._on_button_press(press_event)

        assert widget._drag_start_x == press_event.x_root - root.winfo_x()
        assert widget._drag_start_y == press_event.y_root - root.winfo_y()

        motion_event: tk.Event[Any] = tk.Event()
        motion_event.x_root = 250
        motion_event.y_root = 120
        widget._on_motion(motion_event)

        assert widget._dragged is True
        widget.close()
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()


def test_floating_widget_saved_position_and_callback() -> None:
    """Verify FloatingWidget accepts initial position and fires callback."""
    root = tk.Tk()
    try:
        recorded_pos: list[tuple[int, int]] = []

        def _on_pos(x: int, y: int) -> None:
            recorded_pos.append((x, y))

        widget = FloatingWidget(
            root=root,
            initial_x=320,
            initial_y=45,
            on_position_change=_on_pos,
        )

        assert widget.initial_x == 320
        assert widget.initial_y == 45

        widget._dragged = True
        dummy_event: tk.Event[Any] = tk.Event()
        widget._on_button_release(dummy_event)

        assert len(recorded_pos) == 1
        widget.close()
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            root.destroy()
